### Missions

A mission is an atomic unit of work assigned to a captain (AI agent).

#### GET /api/v1/missions

List all missions with pagination.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:** [Pagination parameters](#pagination-parameters), plus:

| Parameter | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `status` | string | Filter by mission status |
| `vesselId` | string | Filter by vessel ID |
| `captainId` | string | Filter by captain ID |
| `voyageId` | string | Filter by voyage ID |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<[Mission](#mission)\>

> **Note:** The `DiffSnapshot` field is excluded from mission list responses to keep payloads compact. Use `GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/diff` to retrieve the full diff.

```bash
curl http://localhost:7890/api/v1/missions?status=InProgress&vesselId=vsl_abc123
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/missions/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of missions with optional filtering and sorting.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<[Mission](#mission)\>

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/missions/enumerate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Status": "InProgress", "VesselId": "vsl_abc123", "PageSize": 25}'
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/summaries

List lightweight mission summaries without large description, diff, or agent-output payloads. Useful for context-conserving overviews.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:** [Pagination parameters](#pagination-parameters), plus `status`, `vesselId`, `captainId`, and `voyageId` filters.

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`MissionSummary`\>

---

#### POST /api/v1/missions/summaries/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of lightweight mission summaries with optional filtering and sorting.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`MissionSummary`\>

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/history

Return aggregated mission counts by time bucket for dashboard history charts. Missions are counted per bucket and
status in the database (vessel and fleet filters included), never loaded one row each. The window divided by
`bucketMinutes` may produce at most 1000 buckets; a request that would produce more is rejected with `400` and
`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded` (use a larger bucket or a shorter window). Default: the last 7 days in 60-minute
buckets (169 buckets at most).

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:**
| Parameter | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `fromUtc` | datetime | Inclusive UTC start time |
| `toUtc` | datetime | Exclusive UTC end time |
| `bucketMinutes` | int | Bucket size in minutes |
| `fleetId` | string | Optional fleet filter |
| `vesselId` | string | Optional vessel filter |

**Response:** `200 OK` - `MissionHistorySummaryResult`

---

#### POST /api/v1/missions

Create and dispatch a new mission. If a `VesselId` is provided, the Admiral will assign a captain and set up a worktree.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body:** [Mission](#mission)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Title` | string | yes | Mission title |
| `Description` | string | no | Detailed instructions for the AI agent |
| `VesselId` | string | no | Target vessel (required for auto-dispatch) |
| `VoyageId` | string | no | Parent voyage ID |
| `Priority` | int | no | Priority (lower = higher priority, default: 100) |
| `Persona` | string | no | Persona for this mission (e.g. Worker, Architect, Judge) |
| `Mode` | string | no | Execution mode: `Implementation` (default), `Audit`, or `Research`. Audit and Research are read-only modes that produce a written report instead of a commit; their empty diff is treated as success rather than a no-op failure. |
| `SelectedPlaybooks` | array | no | Ordered [SelectedPlaybook](#selectedplaybook) rows for this standalone mission |

**Response:** `201 Created` - [Mission](#mission)

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/missions \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Title": "Fix login bug", "Description": "The login form does not validate email addresses", "VesselId": "vsl_abc123"}'
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/{id}

Get a single mission by ID.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Mission](#mission)
**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

> **Note:** The `DiffSnapshot` field is excluded from responses to keep payloads compact. Use `GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/diff` to retrieve the full diff.

A `Pending` mission also carries `AssignmentBlocker`, computed when it is read (not stored): why no captain has taken it
yet. `Reason` is one of `AwaitingDispatch`, `VesselMissing`, `VesselMisconfigured`, `DependencyNotFinished`,
`DependencyHandoffPending`, `WaitingForVoyageWorkers`, `VesselBroadScopeMissionActive`, `BroadScopeWaitingForVessel`,
`VesselConcurrencyLimit`, `NoCaptains`, `NoIdleCaptain`, or `NoEligibleCaptain`; `Summary` is an English sentence;
`UntilUtc` is when it clears on its own (for example when the only captain's quarantine ends); `DependsOnMissionId` and
`BlockingMissionIds` name the missions it waits for (the first 25; `BlockingMissionCount` is the full count); and
`Captains` lists what each captain in the tenant is doing (the first 25 by name; `CaptainCount` is the full count:
`State`, `Detail`, and the `MissionId`, `PlanningSessionId`, `RefinementSessionId`, `ObjectiveId`, or
`QuarantineUntilUtc` and `QuarantineReason` that hold it). The field is absent for other statuses and in list responses.
`PlaybookSnapshots` holds one snapshot per selected playbook (at most 256, the dispatch limit on `SelectedPlaybooks`).
`LastBlackBoxEntryId` names the mission's current [black box](#black-box) entry (`bbx_`) when it has failed, so a
client finds the entry without knowing the black box routes.

A mission whose captain is running also carries `Activity` when its runtime streams structured output (Claude Code and
Codex missions; see [Mission progress streaming](CAPTAINS.md#mission-progress-streaming)): the captain's latest tool
call, text, or reasoning, kept in memory while it runs (not stored). `Kind` is `ToolCall`, `Text`, or `Thinking`;
`ToolName`, `Description`, and `Detail` (the command or path, at most 200 characters) describe a tool call; `Summary` is
one line such as `Running tests: dotnet test src/App.sln`; `TimestampUtc` is when it was observed. The field is absent
when the captain is not running and in list responses; the `mission.activity` WebSocket event carries updates.

```json
"AssignmentBlocker": {
  "Reason": "NoIdleCaptain",
  "Summary": "Waiting for a captain: Setup Captain is refining backlog item 'Add retries' (stop the refinement session to free it).",
  "UntilUtc": null,
  "DependsOnMissionId": null,
  "BlockingMissionIds": [],
  "BlockingMissionCount": 0,
  "CaptainCount": 1,
  "Captains": [
    { "CaptainId": "cpt_abc", "CaptainName": "Setup Captain", "State": "Refining", "Detail": "refining backlog item 'Add retries' (stop the refinement session to free it)", "RefinementSessionId": "ors_abc", "ObjectiveId": "obj_abc" }
  ],
  "ComputedUtc": "2026-10-05T12:00:00Z"
}
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/github/pull-request

Read normalized GitHub pull-request evidence for one mission when that mission has a `PrUrl`.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - `GitHubPullRequestDetail`
**Error:** `400` - Mission is missing a PR URL or GitHub repository context
**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

> **Note:** The response includes PR state, review status, the first 100 requested reviewers and labels with their full counts (`RequestedReviewerCount`, `LabelCount`), and `ReviewCount`, `CommentCount`, and `CheckCount`. It embeds no review, comment, or check list. `ReviewStatus` is computed over every review (read from GitHub in pages of 100, up to 1000; `ReviewStatusComplete` is false beyond that). Token resolution follows `GitHubTokenOverride` first, then the global `GitHubToken`.

#### POST /api/v1/missions/{id}/github/pull-request/reviews/enumerate

One page of the reviews on the mission's pull request, paged by GitHub (`per_page`/`page`), with an exact `TotalRecords`.
The same shape is served by `.../comments/enumerate` (issue comments, `GitHubPullRequestComment`) and
`.../checks/enumerate` (provider checks on the head commit, `GitHubPullRequestCheck`; an empty page when there is no
head commit).

**Permission:** Authenticated (same scope as `GET /api/v1/missions/{id}`)

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional): `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 100 (GitHub's largest
page; a larger value is normalized to 100), default 10, and `Order` (`CreatedAscending` is GitHub's order, oldest first;
`CreatedDescending`, the default, is newest first). Querystring values override the body.

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`GitHubPullRequestReview`\>
**Error:** `400` - Mission is missing a PR URL or GitHub fails
**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/landing-preview

Predict how Armada would land this mission, including branch policy, check requirements, and likely blockers.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - `LandingPreviewResult`
**Error:** `400` - Mission does not have an associated vessel
**Error:** `404` - Mission not found, or mission vessel not found

For a mission, `IsReadyToLand` is true only while the mission is waiting to land (`WorkProduced`, `PullRequestOpen`, or
`LandingFailed`) and an automatic landing mode applies. The mission preview also returns `EffectiveLandingMode` (voyage,
then vessel, then the Admiral default), `ManualLandingOnly` (true when that mode is `None`: Land is refused, so merge the
branch yourself, for example from Manage Branches), and `MissionStatus`. A `Complete` mission reports the
`mission_already_landed` issue.

---

#### PUT /api/v1/missions/{id}

Update mission fields (title, description, priority, etc.). Does not change status -- use the status transition endpoint for that.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** [Mission](#mission) (fields to update)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Mission](#mission)
**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

---

#### PUT /api/v1/missions/{id}/status

Transition a mission to a new status. Only valid transitions are allowed.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** [StatusTransitionRequest](#statustransitionrequest)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Status` | string | yes | Target status name |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Mission](#mission)
**Error:** `400` - Invalid transition or invalid status name
**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

Stage checks persist `Verifying` while queued or executing, including in `mission_status`. The mission event log records `mission.stage_check_started` and `mission.stage_check` with outcome and duration. `CouldNotRun` is an unverified warning and permits handoff; only a confirmed regression blocks it.

Manual `WorkProduced` or `Complete` returns HTTP 409 (MCP `Conflict`) while the agent or exit/check handoff owns the mission. Otherwise, implementation work requires an active dock and a resolvable commit, then uses the normal vessel landing mode. A raw status write cannot mark unlanded implementation work Complete. Audit/Research may complete without code landing. A late check cannot replace a newer row version or terminal result.

`StageCheckTimeoutMinutes` is one shared execution budget for head and baseline commands. `StageCheckConcurrencyPerHarbor` in server settings defaults to 1 (range 1-64); set it before starting the server. Queue time is excluded. Baseline outcomes are cached in memory by vessel, commit, and command; timeouts and unavailable commands are not cached as failures. Existing vessel workflow overrides are preserved. New Armada defaults run the `Services` suites through `Test.Automated`; the xUnit/NUnit hosts aggregate all shared suites and would still include end-to-end tests.

**Valid Status Transitions:**

| From | Allowed Targets |
|---|---|
| `Pending` | `Assigned`, `Blocked`, `Cancelled` |
| `Blocked` | `Pending`, `Cancelled` |
| `Assigned` | `InProgress`, `Cancelled` |
| `InProgress` | `WorkProduced`, `Testing`, `Review`, `Complete`, `Failed`, `Cancelled` |
| `WorkProduced` | `PullRequestOpen`, `Complete`, `LandingFailed`, `Cancelled` |
| `PullRequestOpen` | `Complete`, `LandingFailed`, `Cancelled` |
| `Testing` | `Review`, `InProgress`, `Complete`, `Failed` |
| `Verifying` | Owned by the exit handler; manual handoff is refused; cancellation is allowed |
| `Review` | `Complete`, `InProgress`, `Failed` |
| `LandingFailed` | `WorkProduced`, `Complete`, `Failed`, `Cancelled` |
| `Complete` | (terminal) |
| `Failed` | (terminal) |
| `Cancelled` | (terminal) |

Automatic completion is blocked while the latest stage check is Failed. CouldNotRun and TimedOut checks are recorded as unverified, never as passing; such work reaches Complete only through a confirmed landing. LocalMerge and MergeAndPush retain WorkProduced when the diff snapshot or source branch cannot be confirmed; missing evidence never counts as successful landing and does not trigger branch deletion. In-place resumes retain ownership until the replacement process is attached. Orphan recovery does not treat a vanished process as a successful exit: unverified orphan work is held in WorkProduced, and a mission whose claimed exit or resume is still being handled is left alone.

```bash
curl -X PUT http://localhost:7890/api/v1/missions/msn_abc123/status \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Status": "Assigned"}'
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/missions/{id}/review/approve

Approve a mission waiting at a review gate. Non-terminal stages continue to the next pipeline stage; terminal stages continue to landing.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** `MissionReviewDecisionRequest` (optional)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Comment` | string | no | Reviewer comment |
| `Conditional` | bool | no | When `true`, the comment is attached to the next pipeline stage as guidance the next captain must take into account ("Conditionally Approve"). Ignored when there is no downstream stage. |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Mission](#mission)
**Error:** `400` - Mission is not waiting at a review gate
**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

---

#### POST /api/v1/missions/{id}/review/deny

Deny a mission waiting at a review gate. The mission either returns for rework or fails the pipeline, depending on its review policy or the supplied action.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** `MissionReviewDecisionRequest` (optional)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Comment` | string | no | Reviewer comment (injected into the re-run mission prompt) |
| `Action` | string | no | Overrides the mission's configured deny action. `RetryStage` revisits the same stage with the feedback ("More Work Required"); `FailPipeline` rejects the stage and cancels dependents ("Deny"). When omitted, the mission's configured deny action is used. |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Mission](#mission)
**Error:** `400` - Mission is not waiting at a review gate
**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

---

#### DELETE /api/v1/missions/{id}

Cancel a mission. Returns the full updated mission. Cancelling stops the mission's agent first: the captain's process is stopped through the lifecycle that owns it (on its Harbor when it runs there) and the exit is confirmed, then the captain is released when it holds no other active mission, then the mission is set to `Cancelled`. The dock is kept for inspection. When the stop is not confirmed (for example the owning Harbor is disconnected), nothing changes and the cancel is refused, so no other work is assigned to a captain whose agent may still run.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Mission](#mission) (with `Status: "Cancelled"`)

**Errors:** `404` - Mission not found; `409` - the agent did not confirm it stopped (the mission is unchanged; reconnect the owning Harbor and retry)

---

#### DELETE /api/v1/missions/{id}/purge

Permanently delete a mission from the database, removing telemetry events that referenced it. **This cannot be undone.**

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "Status": "deleted",
  "MissionId": "msn_abc123"
}
```

**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

---

#### `POST /api/v1/missions/delete/multiple`

Batch delete multiple missions from the database by ID. Returns a summary of deleted and skipped entries. **This cannot be undone.**

`Ids` holds at most 1000 ids; more is rejected with `400` and `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded` (`Field` `Ids`, `Limit` 1000, `Actual`), and nothing is deleted. `Skipped` therefore holds at most 1000 entries.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body:**

```json
{
  "Ids": ["msn_abc123", "msn_def456"]
}
```

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "Status": "deleted",
  "Deleted": 2,
  "Skipped": []
}
```

Skipped entries include the entity ID and the reason (e.g., "Not found" or "Empty ID").

---

#### POST /api/v1/missions/{id}/restart

Restart a `Failed`, `LandingFailed`, or `Cancelled` mission by resetting it to `Pending` for re-dispatch, or start a `Pending` mission as it is. A restart of a failed or cancelled mission clears captain assignment, branch, PR URL, and timing fields; a `Pending` mission only takes the new title and description, if any. Optionally update the title and description (instructions) before restarting.

The mission is then dispatched at once under the normal dispatch rules (voyage status, pipeline dependencies, vessel concurrency, requested captain). When a captain is free the response shows it `Assigned` or `InProgress`; when none is, it stays `Pending`, the response is still `200`, and the regular dispatcher picks it up later.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Request Body (optional):**
```json
{
  "Title": "Updated mission title",
  "Description": "Updated instructions for the captain"
}
```

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Title` | string | No | New mission title. Omit to keep the original. |
| `Description` | string | No | New mission description/instructions. Omit to keep the original. |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Mission](#mission) (with `Status` `Pending`, `Assigned`, or `InProgress`)

**Errors:**
- `400` - Request body is not a valid restart request
- `404` - Mission not found
- `409` - Mission is not in `Pending`, `Failed`, `LandingFailed`, or `Cancelled` status

---

#### POST /api/v1/missions/{id}/retry-landing

Rebase the mission branch onto the current target and re-attempt landing. Only available for `WorkProduced` or `LandingFailed` missions.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "Status": "landed",
  "MissionId": "msn_abc123",
  "MissionStatus": "Complete"
}
```

**Errors:**
- `400` - Mission is not in `WorkProduced` or `LandingFailed` status
- `404` - Mission not found
- `409` - Landing did not complete (for example, the branch has conflicts, has no branch/vessel, or no automatic landing mode is configured); the response message explains the specific reason. With an effective landing mode of `None` the work stays `WorkProduced` and the branch is left for manual integration: merge it yourself (for example with [POST /api/v1/vessels/{id}/branches/merge](#post-apiv1vesselsidbranchesmerge), the dashboard's Manage Branches), and the mission moves to `Complete`

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/evaluate-autoland

Dry-run the vessel's auto-land predicate against the mission's captured diff without landing it.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` -- `{ "Land": true }` or `{ "Land": false, "HoldReason": "..." }`
**Errors:** `404` mission not found; `400` mission has no associated vessel

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/diff

Returns the git diff of changes made by a captain in the mission's worktree. Checks for a saved diff file first (captured at completion), then the database snapshot, then a live worktree diff on the dock's owning Admiral or Harbor host. An unavailable Harbor returns HTTP 503 with connection guidance; an inaccessible worktree without a saved diff returns HTTP 404. A Harbor path does not need to exist on the Admiral host.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "MissionId": "msn_abc123",
  "Branch": "armada/msn_abc123",
  "Diff": "diff --git a/src/auth.ts b/src/auth.ts\n..."
}
```

**Error:** `404` - Mission not found or no diff available

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/log

Returns the session log (captured stdout/stderr) for a mission. Log files are written to disk when a captain executes a mission. Supports pagination via query parameters.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Query Parameters:**
| Parameter | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lines` | integer | 100 | Number of lines to return |
| `offset` | integer | 0 | Line offset (0-based, skip this many lines from start) |

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "MissionId": "msn_abc123",
  "Log": "Starting mission...\nCloning repository...\n...",
  "Lines": 100,
  "TotalLines": 542
}
```

If the mission exists but has no log file yet, returns an empty log:

```json
{
  "MissionId": "msn_abc123",
  "Log": "",
  "Lines": 0,
  "TotalLines": 0
}
```

**Error:** `404` - Mission not found

```bash
# Get first 50 lines
curl http://localhost:8080/api/v1/missions/msn_abc123/log?lines=50 \
  -H "X-Api-Key: your-key"

# Get lines 100-200
curl http://localhost:8080/api/v1/missions/msn_abc123/log?offset=100&lines=100 \
  -H "X-Api-Key: your-key"
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/instructions

Return the instructions file the captain was given for a mission. Resolves the live dock/worktree first (using the runtime-specific instructions file name, then common fallbacks such as `CLAUDE.md`, `CODEX.md`, `AGENTS.md`), and falls back to a saved instructions snapshot when no live worktree exists.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "MissionId": "msn_abc123",
  "FileName": "CLAUDE.md",
  "Content": "You are an Armada captain executing a mission..."
}
```

**Error:** `404` - Mission not found, or instructions are unavailable (no live dock/worktree and no saved snapshot)

---

### Captains

A captain is an AI agent instance (Claude Code, Codex, etc.) that executes missions.

#### GET /api/v1/captains

List all captains with pagination.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:** [Pagination parameters](#pagination-parameters), plus:

| Parameter | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `status` | string | Filter by captain state (`Idle`, `Working`, `Stalled`, `Stopping`) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<[Captain](#captain)\>

```bash
curl http://localhost:7890/api/v1/captains?status=Working
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/captains/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of captains with optional filtering and sorting.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<[Captain](#captain)\>

---

#### POST /api/v1/captains

Register a new captain (AI agent).

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body:** [Captain](#captain)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Name` | string | yes | Captain name |
| `Runtime` | string | no | Agent runtime type (default: `ClaudeCode`) |
| `Model` | string | no | Optional model override for this captain. When omitted, the runtime selects its default model |
| `CliPermissionPolicy` | string | no | CLI tool permission policy: `Refuse`, `ApproveInArmada`, `Bypass`, or null to inherit (see [CAPTAINS.md](CAPTAINS.md#cli-tool-permissions)) |

**Response:** `201 Created` - [Captain](#captain)
**Error:** `400 Bad Request` - Invalid or unavailable model. `Data` is a `CaptainModelValidationFailure`: `{"Reason": "...", "Message": "..."}` with `Reason` one of `EndpointRequired`, `EndpointNotFound`, `EndpointNotInference`, `EndpointDisabled`, `RuntimeUnavailable`, `ModelRejected`, `TimedOut`, `InvalidRuntimeOptions`, `NamedEndpointRequired`, `UnsupportedContractVersion`, `EndpointProbeFailed`, `EndpointNotToolEnabled` (the same on `PUT /api/v1/captains/{id}`; MCP `create_captain` / `update_captain` return `ErrorCode` `InvalidArgument` with `Code` = the reason).
**Error:** `409 Conflict` - A captain with that name already exists (`Data`: [DuplicateEntityErrorDetail](#duplicateentityerrordetail) with `Code` `DuplicateEntity`, `EntityType` `Captain`, `Field` `Name`)

Named CLI model validation follows normal Harbor routing and the captain owner's tenant/user scope. A Harbor-required deployment does not require that CLI on the Admiral container. Validation uses a scratch directory and a 30-second process limit; missing/unavailable execution hosts return `RuntimeUnavailable`, while a CLI-rejected model returns `ModelRejected`. An omitted model retains the runtime default. Mux captain validation performs actual backend inference with a bounded 120-second process deadline; endpoint list/show metadata inspection retains a ten-second deadline. An inference timeout remains a validation failure and does not trigger an automatic retry.

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/captains \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Name": "captain-1", "Runtime": "ClaudeCode", "Model": "claude-sonnet-4-20250514", "SystemInstructions": "You are a testing specialist. Always run tests before committing."}'
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/captains/{id}

Get a single captain by ID.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Captain](#captain)
**Error:** `404` - Captain not found

---

#### GET /api/v1/captains/{id}/tools

Describe the tool sources and tools available through a specific captain, including runtime-specific availability notes.
`Servers` lists the tool sources (at most 256; a runtime configuration with more is a `400` with `Data.Code`
`CollectionLimitExceeded`, never a silent cut). `Tools` is one page (`EnumerationResult<CaptainToolSummary>`) ordered by
source kind, source, and name; `ToolCount`, `McpToolCount`, and `RuntimeBuiltInToolCount` count the whole inventory. The
runtime is probed once and the snapshot (`SnapshotUtc`) is reused for 60 seconds so reading further pages does not
probe again; `refresh=true` probes again. `POST /api/v1/captains/{id}/tools/enumerate` takes the same query
(`CaptainToolsQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `SourceKind`, `Refresh`) in the JSON body.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Query Parameters:** `pageNumber` (default 1), `pageSize` (1 to 1000, default 10), `sourceKind` (`McpServer`,
`RuntimeBuiltIn`, or `Internal`; filters `Tools` before counting), `refresh` (`true` to probe again).

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "captainId": "cpt_abc123",
  "captainName": "captain-1",
  "runtime": "Mux",
  "toolsAccessible": true,
  "availabilityVerified": true,
  "availabilitySource": "mux-probe",
  "summary": "Mux probe succeeded and the endpoint reported tool calling enabled.",
  "endpointName": "local-codex",
  "toolsEnabled": true,
  "effectiveToolCount": 42,
  "armadaToolCount": 37,
  "askApprovalGated": true,
  "servers": [],
  "toolCount": 37,
  "mcpToolCount": 37,
  "runtimeBuiltInToolCount": 0,
  "snapshotUtc": "2026-10-28T12:00:00Z",
  "tools": {
    "pageNumber": 1,
    "pageSize": 10,
    "totalPages": 4,
    "totalRecords": 37,
    "objects": [
      {
        "name": "get_status",
        "description": "Get current Admiral status snapshot",
        "inputSchemaJson": "{\"type\":\"object\",\"properties\":{}}",
        "registrationSource": "armada",
        "sourceKind": "McpServer"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

**Error:** `400` - Unknown `sourceKind`, a malformed body (POST), or more than 256 tool sources (`CollectionLimitExceeded`)
**Error:** `404` - Captain not found

---

#### PUT /api/v1/captains/{id}

Update a captain's name, runtime, or model. `tenantId`, `userId`, `createdUtc`, `CliPermissionPolicy` (change it with
[`PUT /api/v1/captains/{id}/cli-permission-policy`](#put-apiv1captainsidcli-permission-policy)), and the operational
fields (state, current mission and dock, process, recovery attempts, quarantine, heartbeats) are preserved.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Request Body:**
```json
{
  "name": "captain-bravo",
  "runtime": "Codex",
  "model": "gpt-5.4",
  "systemInstructions": "Focus on code quality and always run linting before commits."
}
```

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Captain](#captain)
**Error:** `400 Bad Request` - Invalid or unavailable model
**Error:** `409 Conflict` - Another captain already has that name (`Data`: [DuplicateEntityErrorDetail](#duplicateentityerrordetail) with `Code` `DuplicateEntity`, `EntityType` `Captain`, `Field` `Name`)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Captain](#captain)
**Error:** `404` - Captain not found

```bash
curl -X PUT http://localhost:7890/api/v1/captains/cpt_abc123 \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "x-api-key: YOUR_KEY" \
  -d '{"name": "captain-bravo", "runtime": "Codex"}'
```

---

#### PUT /api/v1/captains/{id}/cli-permission-policy

Set or clear the captain's CLI tool permission policy: how the CLI's own tools (shell commands, web fetches, file tools
outside the accepted edits) are permitted on its missions and Ask turns. `null` clears it, so the next level of the
resolution order applies (the legacy `autoApprove` option, then the server default). For Ask turns a captain-level
`Bypass` is honored only when `Ask.CaptainAutoApprove` is true. See [CAPTAINS.md](CAPTAINS.md#cli-tool-permissions).

**Permission:** TenantAdmin (a global admin, or a tenant admin of the captain's tenant)

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** [CliPermissionPolicyUpdateRequest](#clipermissionpolicyupdaterequest)

```json
{ "Policy": "ApproveInArmada" }
```

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Captain](#captain)
**Error:** `403` - Not an admin of the captain's tenant; `404` - Captain not found (or in another tenant)

```bash
curl -X PUT http://localhost:7890/api/v1/captains/cpt_abc123/cli-permission-policy \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" \
  -d '{"Policy": "Refuse"}'
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/captains/{id}/unquarantine

Lift a captain's quarantine, returning it to `Idle` and clearing the quarantine deadline and reason.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Captain](#captain) (with `State: "Idle"`). When the captain is not quarantined, returns `{ "Status": "not_quarantined", "CaptainId": "cpt_abc123" }` and makes no change.

**Error:** `404` - Captain not found

---

#### POST /api/v1/captains/{id}/stop

Stop a running captain through its owning lifecycle. The captain is recalled to idle only after exit is confirmed; a Harbor process handle is never treated as an Admiral-local PID.

The mission the captain was running is set to `Cancelled` with failure kind `OperatorAction`, and its failure reason records who stopped it, through which surface, and when (for example `Stopped by operator (usr_...) via stop_captain (REST) at 2026-10-28T02:37:59Z.`). A `mission.stopped_by_operator` event carries the same data. Before the dock is reclaimed, uncommitted work in it is committed to the mission branch as `WIP: preserved on operator stop` and pushed, best effort; the commit hash is added to the failure reason and the event.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "Status": "stopped"
}
```

**Errors:** `404` - Captain not found; `409 Conflict` - exit was not confirmed or the captain is reserved by another coordinated session. An unconfirmed stop retains the captain/mission assignment and returns remediation.

---

#### POST /api/v1/captains/stop-all

Attempts all accessible working captains and active planning/refinement sessions. Global admins act across tenants; tenant admins act only within their own tenant.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "Status": "all_stopped"
}
```

**Error:** `409 Conflict` - one or more stops did not complete or confirm exit. The server attempts the remaining accessible stops and never reports `all_stopped` on partial failure. Unconfirmed captain assignments remain owned.

---

#### GET /api/v1/captains/{id}/log

Returns the current session log for a captain. The captain's `.current` pointer file is resolved to find the active mission's log file. Supports pagination via query parameters.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Query Parameters:**
| Parameter | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `lines` | integer | 100 | Number of lines to return |
| `offset` | integer | 0 | Line offset (0-based, skip this many lines from start) |

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "CaptainId": "cpt_abc123",
  "Log": "[2026-03-07] Processing task...\nRunning tests...\n...",
  "Lines": 100,
  "TotalLines": 203
}
```

If the captain has no active log (no pointer file or target file missing), returns an empty log:

```json
{
  "CaptainId": "cpt_abc123",
  "Log": "",
  "Lines": 0,
  "TotalLines": 0
}
```

**Error:** `404` - Captain not found

```bash
curl http://localhost:8080/api/v1/captains/cpt_abc123/log?lines=200 \
  -H "X-Api-Key: your-key"
```

---

#### DELETE /api/v1/captains/{id}

Delete a captain. Blocked if the captain is currently working or has active missions.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Response:** `204 No Content`
**Error:** `404` - Captain not found
**Error:** `409 Conflict` - Cannot delete captain while state is Working. Stop the captain first.
**Error:** `409 Conflict` - Cannot delete captain with active missions in Assigned or InProgress status. Cancel or complete them first.

---

#### `POST /api/v1/captains/delete/multiple`

Batch delete multiple captains from the database by ID. Captains that are Working or have active missions are skipped. Returns a summary of deleted and skipped entries. `Ids` may hold at most 1000 entries; more is a `400` with `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded` and nothing is deleted, which also bounds `Skipped`. **This cannot be undone.**

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body:**

```json
{
  "Ids": ["cpt_abc123", "cpt_def456"]
}
```

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "Status": "deleted",
  "Deleted": 2,
  "Skipped": []
}
```

Skipped entries include the entity ID and the reason (e.g., "Not found", "Cannot delete captain while state is Working", or "Cannot delete captain with N active mission(s)").

---

### Ask

The Ask surface backs Ask Armada threads and a direct chat channel to a captain's configured model. (The keyword-matching `POST /api/v1/ask` responder was removed; use Ask Armada threads.)

#### POST /api/v1/captains/{id}/chat

Send a chat turn directly to a captain's configured model (Mux/Ollama endpoints) and return the reply plus per-turn timing and token metrics.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Captain ID (`cpt_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** `CaptainChatRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Message` | string | yes | The new user message to send to the captain's model |
| `History` | array | no | Prior conversation turns (oldest first), excluding the new message |
| `TurnId` | string | no | Client-generated turn id; when set, the reply is also streamed as `ask.chunk` WebSocket events tagged with this id (to the caller's own sockets only) |
| `ShowThinking` | bool | no | When `true`, ask the runtime to surface the model's reasoning (honored by the Mux runtime) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - `CaptainChatResponse`

```json
{
  "Success": true,
  "Reply": "Here is a summary of the changes...",
  "Model": "gpt-oss:20b",
  "Metrics": {},
  "Error": null,
  "ErrorCode": null,
  "Thinking": null
}
```

The turn runs where a mission for the same captain would: on a connected, eligible Harbor (its output and final message
stream back over the link) or, when there is none and `requireHarborForLaunch` is off, on the Admiral host. ApiEndpoint
captains always run in-process on the Admiral. See [CAPTAINS.md](CAPTAINS.md#where-interactive-turns-run).

A failed turn returns `200` with `Success: false`, the reason in `Error`, and, for failures a client can act on,
`ErrorCode` (`CaptainChatErrorCodeEnum`):

| ErrorCode | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `HarborRequired` | `requireHarborForLaunch` is on and no eligible Harbor owned by the caller is connected ("No Harbor is connected to run this captain"); nothing ran on the Admiral host |
| `RuntimeNotInstalled` | The runtime's CLI is not installed on the Admiral host (and no Harbor ran the turn) |
| `HarborLaunchFailed` | The chosen Harbor could not start the captain; `Error` names the Harbor and carries its reason (for example the CLI is not installed there) |

Ask Armada turns fail the same way: the reason becomes the turn's error message in the thread.


#### Ask Armada threads

Ask Armada threads are private, persisted conversations. Every thread route requires authentication and is **owner-scoped**:
a thread of another user (even an admin's, or one in the same tenant) returns `404`. The work a thread starts stays
visible tenant-wide on the normal pages. Live updates arrive over the WebSocket (`ask.*` events, owner-only; see
WEBSOCKET_API.md). Retention: threads are kept until deleted; there is no automatic expiry.

| Method | Path | Body | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/enumerate` | `AskThreadEnumerateRequest` | `200` `EnumerationResult<AskThread>`, pinned first, then most recent activity |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads` | `AskThreadCreateRequest` | `201` `AskThread` |
| GET | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}` | | `200` `AskThreadDetail` |
| PUT | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}` | `AskThreadUpdateRequest` | `200` `AskThread` |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}` | | `204` (stops a running turn; deletes messages, tool calls, proposals, tracked-work rows; the work itself is untouched) |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/delete/multiple` | `DeleteMultipleRequest` | `200` `AskThreadDeleteMultipleResult` (`Status`, `Deleted`, `DeletedIds`, `Skipped` with `Id` and `Reason`); `400` when `Ids` is missing or empty, and `400` `CollectionLimitExceeded` (nothing deleted) when it holds more than 1000 IDs. Another user's or an unknown ID is skipped, never deleted |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/messages/enumerate` | `AskMessageEnumerateRequest` | `200` `AskMessagePage` |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/messages` | `AskMessageSendRequest` | `202` `AskMessageSendResponse`; `409` while a turn runs |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/cancel` | | `200` `{ "Cancelled": true }`; `409` when no turn runs |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/summarize` | | `202` `AskMessageSendResponse` (`TurnId` null without a captain); `409` while a turn runs |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/read` | | `200` `AskThread` with `UnreadCount = 0` |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/unread` | | `200` `AskThread` with `UnreadCount = 1` when it was 0 (a non-zero count is kept) |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/actions` | `AskActionRequest` | `200` `AskActionProposal` (executed or failed); `400` unknown tool |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/proposals/{pid}/approve` | | `200` `AskActionProposal` (`Executed` or `Failed`); `409` when not pending or expired |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/proposals/{pid}/reject` | | `200` `AskActionProposal` (`Rejected`); `409` when not pending |
| GET | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/work/{workId}` | | `200` `AskWorkSnapshot` (counts; rows are paged below) |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/work/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` (`Status`: `Active`, `Succeeded`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`) | `200` `EnumerationResult<AskTrackedWork>`, newest first by default; `400` for an unknown `Status` |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/work/{workId}/missions/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` | `200` `EnumerationResult<AskWorkMissionSnapshot>`: a voyage's missions paged in the database, or the one row of tracked Mission work; `400` for other work types (fleet action runs use `targets`) |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/work/{workId}/targets/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` | `200` `EnumerationResult<AskWorkTargetSnapshot>` for tracked FleetActionRun work; `400` for other work types |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/proposals/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` (`Status`: `Pending`, `Approved`, `Rejected`, `Expired`, `Executed`, `Failed`) | `200` `EnumerationResult<AskActionProposal>`; `400` for an unknown `Status` |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/messages/{messageId}/tool-calls/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` | `200` `EnumerationResult<AskMessageToolCall>`: one message's tool calls; `404` when the message is not in the thread |
| POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/turn/tool-calls/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` | `200` `EnumerationResult<AskMessageToolCall>`: the calls the running turn has made so far (empty when no turn runs) |
| PUT | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/cli-permission-policy` | `CliPermissionPolicyUpdateRequest` | `200` `AskThread` (with `CliPermission`); `403` when a non-admin sets `Bypass` |
| GET | `/api/v1/ask/quick-actions` | | `200` `AskQuickAction[]` |

**Turn flow.** `POST .../messages` persists the user message (the first message also names a thread still titled
"New conversation"), returns `202 { MessageId, TurnId }`, and, when the thread has a captain, runs the turn in the
background. The prompt is built server-side from the thread summary plus the last `Ask.HistoryTurns` messages. The
captain reaches Armada's MCP server with a **thread-scoped session token** (ApiEndpoint captains through
`ARMADA_MCP_URL`/`ARMADA_MCP_TOKEN`, Claude Code through a per-launch `--strict-mcp-config` file carrying an `X-Token`
header). Read-only tools run; any other tool becomes a `Pending` proposal with an `ActionProposal` confirm-card message
unless the thread's `AutoApprove` is on (then it runs and is recorded as `Executed`). The reply and its tool calls are
persisted as one `Assistant` message; failures post an `Error` message. One turn runs per thread at a time; the turn is
limited to `Ask.TurnTimeoutMinutes`.

**Approvals.** Approve executes the tool in-process through the same MCP tool handler (same validation and tenant
scoping as a direct MCP call by the approving user), records `ResultText`, posts an `ActionResult` (or `Error`) message,
starts tracking the work the result created, and runs a short follow-up captain turn. Pending proposals expire after
`Ask.ProposalExpiryMinutes`. **Quick actions** use the same path: the form is the confirmation, so the proposal is
created `Approved` (`Source: QuickAction`) and executes immediately. Tool names and argument shapes are exactly those of
the MCP API, for example `{ "ToolName": "dispatch", "Arguments": { "title": "...", "vesselId": "vsl_...", "missions": [{ "title": "...", "description": "..." }] } }`.

**Work linking.** `dispatch` tracks the Voyage; `create_mission` and `restart_mission` the Mission; `run_fleet_action` the FleetActionRun; `evaluate_vessel_health` the Job;
`import_vessels` and `discover_vessels` the VesselImportBatch (and its Job when it runs in the background);
`cancel_voyage`, `cancel_mission`, and `cancel_fleet_action_run` refresh the item if the thread tracks it.

**Monitoring.** Active tracked work is re-snapshotted on entity change events and every `Ask.TrackerIntervalSeconds`.
When a snapshot changes, `ask.work` is pushed; milestones (work started, a mission failed, produced its work, landed,
opened a pull request, or could not land, and the item succeeded, failed, or was cancelled) post `WorkUpdate` messages, worded by the
thread's captain when it is idle (`Ask.NarrateMilestones`, bounded by `Ask.NarrationTimeoutSeconds`) and otherwise a
deterministic sentence. The final milestone of a voyage, mission, or fleet action run that succeeded or failed is not
narrated: after the sentence it carries an **Outcome** block built from typed fields (elapsed time; per mission its
vessel, status, landing and pull request, failure reason, runtime, finished check runs with exit code and test counts,
and an excerpt of the captain's final message; per fleet action target its status, exit code, and reason). When the
thread has a captain and `Ask.ReportResultsOnCompletion` is on, a short captain turn then reports the outcome as a
`WorkReport` message linked to the work (`TrackedWorkId`): once per tracked item, after any running turn ends, and not
when the user posted after the final milestone or the thread was archived or deleted. It is a normal turn (`ask.turn`
events, CLI permission policy, telemetry). Captain and Armada messages increment `UnreadCount`; `POST .../read` resets it.

**CLI tool permissions.** The CLI's own tools (shell commands, web fetches, file tools outside its temporary working
directory) follow the turn's CLI tool permission policy, resolved in this order: the thread's `CliPermissionPolicy`; the
captain's `CliPermissionPolicy` (a captain-level `Bypass` only when `Ask.CaptainAutoApprove` is true); when
`Ask.CaptainAutoApprove` is true, the captain's legacy `autoApprove` option (absent or true is `Bypass`, false is
`Refuse`); `Permissions.AskDefaultPolicy` (default `ApproveInArmada`). Under `ApproveInArmada` a Claude Code or Mux (1.5.0 or later) captain's
permission prompt (or an ApiEndpoint captain's `run_process` call) becomes a [CLI permission request](#cli-permissions)
and a `CliPermission` card in the thread; the tool waits until an approver decides or the request expires
(`Permissions.PromptTimeoutSeconds`). Other runtimes, and Claude Code or Mux turns without a thread-scoped token, run
`ApproveInArmada` as `Refuse`. Narrations and
summaries never prompt (`ApproveInArmada` runs them as `Refuse`). Armada's own MCP tools stay available to the captain in
every policy (state changes become proposals). `PUT .../cli-permission-policy` is owner-only (another user's thread
returns `404`); `{ "Policy": null }` clears the override, and `Bypass` requires the owner to be a global admin or a
tenant admin. See [ASK_ARMADA_HOME_BASE.md](ASK_ARMADA_HOME_BASE.md#cli-tool-permissions).

**Models.**

`AskThread`: `Id` (ath_), `TenantId`, `UserId`, `Title`, `CaptainId`, `AutoApprove`, `CliPermissionPolicy` (`Refuse`,
`ApproveInArmada`, `Bypass`, or null to inherit), `CliPermission` (computed [CliPermissionResolution](#clipermissionresolution)
for the next turn, or null without a captain), `SummaryText`, `SummaryUtc`, `Pinned`, `Archived`, `LastMessageUtc`,
`MessageCount`, `UnreadCount`, `ActiveWorkCount` (computed), `ActiveTurnId` (computed; set while a turn runs),
`CreatedUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc`.

`AskThreadDetail`: `{ Thread, TrackedWorkCount, PendingProposalCount, PendingCliPermissionCount, ActiveTurn: AskTurnProgress | null }`.
The collections are read page by page: tracked work with `POST .../work/enumerate`, pending proposals with
`POST .../proposals/enumerate` (`Status: Pending`), and the thread's pending CLI requests with
`POST /api/v1/cli-permissions/requests/enumerate` (`ThreadId`, `Status: Pending`). `ActiveTurn` is set only while a captain turn runs (its `TurnId` equals the thread's
`ActiveTurnId`): `{ TurnId, MessageId (the reserved reply), Text, ThinkingText, ToolCallCount, StartedUtc }` with what
the turn has streamed so far; its calls are read with `POST .../turn/tool-calls/enumerate` (a call still running has a
null `Ok` and no `ResultText`). The reply and its tool
calls are persisted only when the turn ends, so a client that opens the thread mid-turn restores the live reply from it.
A turn that is stopped or fails keeps the tool calls it already made on its `Error` message.

`AskMessage`: `Id` (amg_), `ThreadId`, `Sequence` (per-thread, strictly increasing), `Role` (`User`, `Assistant`,
`System`), `Kind` (`Text`, `ActionProposal`, `ActionResult`, `WorkUpdate`, `Summary`, `Error`, `CliPermission`,
`WorkReport` (the captain's automatic report of finished work; clients that do not know it render it as assistant text)),
`ContentText`, `ThinkingText`, `ProposalId`, `TrackedWorkId`, `CaptainId`, `DurationMs`, `Metrics`, `CreatedUtc`,
`LastUpdateUtc`, `ToolCallCount` (the calls themselves are read with `POST .../messages/{messageId}/tool-calls/enumerate`;
`AskMessageToolCall`: `Id` atc_, `CallId`, `ToolName`, `ArgumentsText`, `ResultText`, `Ok`,
`ElapsedMs`, `PermissionDenied` (true when the CLI refused the call because the turn's CLI tool permission policy did not
grant it; null when unknown)), plus embedded `Proposal`, `TrackedWork` (with `Snapshot`), and `CliPermissionRequest` (on a
`CliPermission` card, a `System` message whose `ContentText` is `<Tool>: <summary>`; refreshed through `ask.message` when
the request is decided).

`Metrics` (`CaptainChatMetrics`, null for user messages, cards, and replies written before migration 82) is the
telemetry of the captain turn that wrote the reply, recorded by the Admiral for every runtime whether the turn ran on
the Admiral host or on a Harbor: `TimeToFirstTokenMs` (first output of any kind: reasoning, a tool call, or text),
`TimeToFirstTextMs` (first visible reply text), `StreamingMs` (total minus time to first token), `TotalMs` (equals
`DurationMs`), `PromptTokens` (input, cached included), `CompletionTokens` (output), `CachedTokens` (cache reads),
`TotalTokens`, `CostUsd`, `TokensEstimated` (true when `CompletionTokens` is estimated from the reply because the
runtime reported no usage), `TokensPerSecond` (completion tokens over the streaming window), `ToolCallCount`, and
`ToolTimeMs`. Token counts and cost are null unless the runtime reports them: Claude Code (stream-json `result`: input,
output, cache, cost), Codex (`turn.completed` usage: input, cached, output), OpenCode (`step_finish`: tokens and
cost), and pi (each assistant `message_end` usage: input, output, cache, cost). Durations are measured on the monotonic clock.

`AskMessagePage`: `{ Messages, HasMore }`. Without `BeforeSequence` the newest page is returned; messages are in ascending
sequence within the page; pass the oldest loaded `Sequence` as `BeforeSequence` for older pages. `PageSize` 1-200
(default 50).

`AskActionProposal`: `Id` (aap_), `ThreadId`, `MessageId`, `ToolName`, `ArgumentsText` (exact JSON arguments),
`SummaryText`, `Source` (`Captain`, `QuickAction`), `Status` (`Pending`, `Approved`, `Rejected`, `Expired`, `Executed`,
`Failed`), `ResultText`, `ErrorText`, `DecidedByUserId`, `DecidedUtc`, `ExecutedUtc`, `ExpiresUtc` (computed, pending
only), `CreatedUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc`.

`AskTrackedWork`: `Id` (atw_), `ThreadId`, `EntityType` (`Voyage`, `Mission`, `FleetActionRun`, `Job`,
`VesselImportBatch`), `EntityId`, `Title`, `Status`, `State` (`Active`, `Succeeded`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`),
`SnapshotHash`, `LastChangeUtc`, `CompletedUtc`, `Snapshot` (embedded on reads).

`AskWorkSnapshot` (flat): `TrackedWorkId`, `ThreadId`, `EntityType`, `EntityId`, `Title`, `Status`, `State`, `Found`,
`TotalCount`, `CompletedCount`, `FailedCount`, `ActiveCount`, `Progress` (0-100), `Counts` (status -> count),
`MissionCount`, `TargetCount`. The rows are paged: mission rows (`AskWorkMissionSnapshot`: `Id`, `Title`, `Status`, `VoyageId`, `VesselId`, `Persona`, `PipelineStage`, `CaptainId`, `CaptainName`,
`BranchName`, `CheckRunId`, `CheckRunStatus`, `MergeEntryId`, `MergeQueueStatus` (alias `MergeStatus`), `PrUrl`,
`LandingOutcome` (`Landed`, `PullRequestOpen`, `PullRequestMerged`, `LandingFailed`), `FailureReason`, `StartedUtc`,
`CompletedUtc`) with `POST .../work/{workId}/missions/enumerate`, and fleet action run targets (`AskWorkTargetSnapshot`:
`Id`, `VesselId`, `VesselName`, `Status`, `Reason`, `VoyageId`, `MissionId`, `ExitCode`) with
`POST .../work/{workId}/targets/enumerate`. Remaining fields: `ErrorText`, `StartedUtc`, `CompletedUtc`, `CapturedUtc`.

`AskQuickAction`: `Name`, `Command`, `Title`, `Description`, `ToolName`, `ReadOnly`, `RequiresTenantAdmin`,
`Arguments[]` (`Name`, `Label`, `Type`, `Required`, `Description`, `DefaultValue`), `ArgumentsSchema` (JSON schema). The
catalog: `/dispatch` (`dispatch`), `/fleet-action` (`run_fleet_action`), `/status` (`status`), `/health`
(`evaluate_vessel_health`, all arguments optional), `/import` (`discover_vessels`).

Requests: `AskThreadEnumerateRequest { PageNumber (1), PageSize (25, 1-100), Search, IncludeArchived }`;
`AskThreadCreateRequest { Title?, CaptainId?, AutoApprove? }`; `AskThreadUpdateRequest { Title?, CaptainId?,
AutoApprove?, Pinned?, Archived? }` (absent fields unchanged; `"CaptainId": null` clears the captain);
`AskMessageEnumerateRequest { BeforeSequence?, PageSize }`; `AskMessageSendRequest { Content (required, max 32000),
ShowThinking? }`; `AskActionRequest { ToolName, Arguments }`.

---

### CLI Permissions

CLI tool permissions govern a CLI captain's own tools (shell commands, web fetches, file tools outside the accepted
edits; not Armada's MCP tools, which are governed by Ask proposals and per-tool authorization). When a launch runs with
the `ApproveInArmada` policy, Claude Code (and Mux 1.5.0 or later) sends each permission prompt to Armada's `cli_permission_prompt` MCP tool and a
**CLI permission request** (`cpr_`) waits for an approver; a matching **rule** (`cpl_`) decides it without asking. See
[CAPTAINS.md](CAPTAINS.md#cli-tool-permissions) for the policy resolution and the per-runtime flags.

Who may act (`CliPermissionAccess`, the same on REST, MCP, WebSocket, and the inbox):

| Action | Who |
|---|---|
| See a request | Global admins; tenant admins of the request's tenant; the owner of the Ask thread or mission. Other requests return `404` |
| Decide (`AllowOnce`, `Deny`) | Global admins; tenant admins of the request's tenant; the owner only when `Permissions.AllowOwnerApproval` is true |
| `AllowAndRemember` (also stores an allow rule) | Global admins; tenant admins of the request's tenant |
| `AllowForConversation` (also sets the request's Ask thread to `Bypass`) | The Ask thread's owner, when a global admin or a tenant admin of the request's tenant (the same people who may set `Bypass` on the thread). Requests without a thread return `403` |
| List and read rules | Every authenticated user (the caller's tenant plus rules for every tenant; global admins see all) |
| Create, update, delete rules | Global admins (any tenant, or no tenant for every tenant); tenant admins (their own tenant) |

Captain sessions (mission- and thread-scoped tokens) can never decide; REST already refuses thread-scoped tokens.
Request inputs are stored redacted (`InputText`, `SummaryText`, `SuggestedRule`); the original input is held only in
memory by the waiting call. A request no approver decides within its countdown applies its snapshotted `TimeoutDecision`: `Deny` becomes `Expired`, while `Approve` becomes `Allowed`;
one whose Ask turn or mission process ends first becomes `Cancelled`. Both are denials for the captain.

**Rules** use Claude Code permission rule syntax:

| Pattern | Matches |
|---|---|
| `Bash` (or `Bash(*)`) | Every shell command |
| `Bash(git status:*)` | A command equal to `git status` or starting with `git status ` |
| `Bash(npm run *)` | Glob: `*` matches any characters |
| `Bash(make test)` | Exactly that command |
| `WebFetch(domain:example.com)`, `WebFetch(domain:*.example.com)` | The URL's host, or its subdomains |
| `Read(...)`, `Edit(...)` | Gitignore-style paths: `//abs/path/**` is absolute, `~/path` is under the home directory, `path`, `./path`, or `/path` are relative to the mission's dock. `*` stays within a segment, `**` crosses segments. `Edit` covers Edit, Write, MultiEdit, NotebookEdit; `Read` covers Read, Glob, Grep, LS |
| `mcp__server`, `mcp__server__*`, `mcp__server__tool` | Every tool of an MCP server, or one tool |

A matching deny rule denies without asking and wins over allow rules; otherwise a matching allow rule allows. A Bash
command line is split at `&&`, `||`, `;`, `|`, `|&`, `&`, and newlines outside quotes: it is allowed only when every
subcommand matches an allow rule, and denied when any subcommand matches a deny rule. A command with command substitution
or unbalanced quotes is never allowed by a `Bash(...)` rule, so it goes to an approver. A specifier on any other tool is
not interpreted and never matches. Rule scope: `Global` (every captain of the rule's tenant, or of every tenant when a
global admin leaves `TenantId` empty), `Vessel` (missions on one vessel), or `Captain` (one captain, missions and Ask
turns).

| Method | Path | Body | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/v1/cli-permissions/requests/enumerate` | `CliPermissionRequestEnumerateRequest` (optional: every `EnumerationQuery` field plus `ThreadId`) | `200` `EnumerationResult<CliPermissionRequest>`, one page, newest first, with `TotalRecords`. Filters (body or querystring): `Status` (`Pending`, `Allowed`, `Denied`, `Expired`, `Cancelled`), `MissionId`, `ThreadId`, `CaptainId`, `VesselId`; `PageNumber`, `PageSize` (1-1000, default 10); querystring values override the body. `400` for an invalid status. Replaces the removed `GET /api/v1/cli-permissions/requests` (D-015), which answers `410 Gone` |
| GET | `/api/v1/cli-permissions/requests/{id}` | | `200` `CliPermissionRequest`; `404` when missing or not visible |
| POST | `/api/v1/cli-permissions/requests/{id}/decide` | `CliPermissionDecisionRequest` | `200` the decided `CliPermissionRequest`; `400` invalid rule pattern or scope; `403` the caller may not decide (or remember); `404`; `409` no longer pending |
| POST | `/api/v1/cli-permissions/rules/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` (optional) | `200` `EnumerationResult<CliPermissionRule>`, one page. Filters: `VesselId`, `CaptainId` (body or querystring) and `scope` (querystring) |
| POST | `/api/v1/cli-permissions/rules` | `CliPermissionRule` | `201` `CliPermissionRule`; `400` invalid pattern, or a `Vessel`/`Captain` rule without its id; `403` not an admin; `404` vessel or captain not visible |
| GET | `/api/v1/cli-permissions/rules/{id}` | | `200` `CliPermissionRule`; `404` |
| PUT | `/api/v1/cli-permissions/rules/{id}` | `CliPermissionRule` (`Pattern`, `Action`, `Description`) | `200` `CliPermissionRule`; `400`; `403` not an admin of the rule's tenant; `404`. Scope and target are fixed (delete and recreate to change them) |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/cli-permissions/rules/{id}` | | `204`; `403`; `404` |
| PUT | `/api/v1/captains/{id}/cli-permission-policy` | `CliPermissionPolicyUpdateRequest` | `200` `Captain`; see [Captains](#put-apiv1captainsidcli-permission-policy) |
| PUT | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/cli-permission-policy` | `CliPermissionPolicyUpdateRequest` | `200` `AskThread`; see [Ask Armada threads](#ask-armada-threads) |

Every list and decision is the same set of rows the dashboard's Settings > CLI Tool Permissions tab, the TUI Approvals center,
the inbox (`cli_permission`), MCP, and WebSocket show. Events: `cli_permission.requested` and `cli_permission.resolved`
(see [WEBSOCKET_API.md](WEBSOCKET_API.md)).

```bash
# Allow a pending request once
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/cli-permissions/requests/cpr_abc123/decide \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Decision": "AllowOnce"}'

# Allow it and remember an allow rule for this captain
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/cli-permissions/requests/cpr_abc123/decide \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Decision": "AllowAndRemember", "RulePattern": "Bash(npm test:*)", "RuleScope": "Captain"}'

# Deny every force push for the tenant
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/cli-permissions/rules \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Pattern": "Bash(git push --force:*)", "Action": "Deny", "Scope": "Global"}'
```

---

### Stage Outcomes and Failure-Cause Rules

A **stage outcome** (`sto_`) is stored for every stage attempt Armada handles: who ran it (captain, runtime, model,
endpoint, Harbor, sampling, tools, prompt and Armada version), what was observed (exit code, runtime failure kind,
stage-check status and reason, failure kind and reason), what Armada did next (`Disposition`: `Completed`,
`Relaunched`, `OutputRepair`, `Escalated`, `Failed`, `Interrupted`, `Cancelled`, `Pending`), and, for a shortfall, a
**suspected cause** (`Protocol`, `InstructionFollowing`, `ToolUse`, `Context`, `Capability`, `NotTheModel`, `Unknown`,
`Mixed`; `None` when nothing fell short). The cause comes from the first enabled **failure-cause rule** (`fcr_`) that
matches, in ascending `Priority` (`CauseSource` `Rule`), or from a person (`Manual`). See
[MODEL_HETEROGENEITY.md](MODEL_HETEROGENEITY.md), Part 4.

A rule's `Match` lists criteria: every criterion given must match (AND), and within a list any value matches (OR):
`Personas` (case-insensitive), `Runtimes`, `FailureKinds`, `RuntimeFailureKinds`, `StageCheckStatuses`,
`StageCheckReasons`, `Dispositions`, and `TextPattern` (a case-insensitive regular expression matched against the
failure reason). A rule is rejected with `400` when it has no name, a name over 200 characters, no criterion, cause
`None`, or a `TextPattern` that is not a valid regular expression (or is over 1000 characters); the message says which.
Names are unique per tenant (`409`).

**Built-in rules.** Armada seeds 13 built-in rules (`IsBuiltIn` true, `TenantId` null) at startup, matched by name and
never overwriting a stored rule, so an edited built-in keeps its edits. A global admin may edit, disable, or delete a
built-in; a deleted built-in is stored again the next time the Admiral starts, so disable it (`Enabled` false) to keep
it out of classification for good.

Who may act (`StageOutcomeAccessService`, the same on REST and MCP):

| Action | Who |
|---|---|
| List, read, and classify outcomes; draft a rule from one | Global admins (every tenant); everyone else within their own tenant. Another tenant's outcome is `404` |
| Re-apply the rules (`reclassify`) | Global admins (any tenant, or every tenant without `TenantId`); tenant admins (their own tenant). Others `403` |
| List and read rules | Every authenticated user: the caller's tenant plus rules for every tenant (global admins see all) |
| Create, update, delete the tenant's rules | Any user of the tenant. A global admin may create a rule for any tenant, or for every tenant by omitting `TenantId` |
| Update or delete a rule for every tenant, or a built-in rule | Global admins only (`403`) |

Captain sessions (mission- and thread-scoped tokens) are refused (`403`).

| Method | Path | Body | Response |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/api/v1/stage-outcomes` | | `200` `EnumerationResult<StageOutcome>`, newest (`EndedUtc`) first. Query: `tenantId` (global admins), `missionId`, `voyageId`, `vesselId`, `captainId`, `persona`, `model`, `suspectedCause`, `causeSource`, `disposition`, `isEvaluation`, `fromUtc`, `toUtc`, `pageNumber`, `pageSize` (1-1000, default 100); `400` for a value that does not parse or `fromUtc` after `toUtc` |
| POST | `/api/v1/stage-outcomes/enumerate` | `StageOutcomeQuery` (optional): `TenantId` (global admins), `MissionId`, `VoyageId`, `VesselId`, `CaptainId`, `Persona`, `Model`, `SuspectedCause`, `CauseSource`, `Disposition`, `IsEvaluation`, `FromUtc`, `ToUtc`, `PageNumber`, `PageSize` (1-1000, default 100) | `200` the same page as the GET; querystring `pageNumber` and `pageSize` override the body; `400` for a body that does not parse |
| GET | `/api/v1/stage-outcomes/{id}` | | `200` `StageOutcome`; `404` when missing or in another tenant |
| PUT | `/api/v1/stage-outcomes/{id}/cause` | `StageOutcomeClassifyRequest` (`Cause`, `Note`) | `200` `StageOutcome` with `CauseSource` `Manual`, `ClassifiedByUserId` the caller, `ClassifiedUtc` now; rules never overwrite a manual cause. A null `Cause` clears the manual cause and re-applies the rules. `400` for `Cause` `None`; `404` |
| POST | `/api/v1/stage-outcomes/{id}/rule` | | `200` an **unsaved** `FailureCauseRule` drafted from the outcome: its `Cause` and `TenantId`, `Priority` 5 (ahead of the built-ins), and a `Match` on its failure kind, runtime failure kind, stage-check status and reason, disposition, and persona. Nothing is stored: edit the draft and `POST` it to `/api/v1/failure-cause-rules`. `404`; `409` when the outcome's cause is `None` |
| POST | `/api/v1/stage-outcomes/reclassify` | `StageOutcomeQuery` (optional; paging ignored) | `200` `StageOutcomeReclassifyResult` (`Examined`, `Changed`, `SkippedManual`); `400`; `403` not an admin |
| GET | `/api/v1/failure-cause-rules` | | `200` `EnumerationResult<FailureCauseRule>` in evaluation order (priority, then name, then id), paged in the database. Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize` (1-1000, default 10) |
| POST | `/api/v1/failure-cause-rules/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` (optional): `PageNumber`, `PageSize` (1-1000, default 10) | `200` `EnumerationResult<FailureCauseRule>` in evaluation order, the same page as the GET; querystring paging overrides the body |
| POST | `/api/v1/failure-cause-rules` | `FailureCauseRule` (`Name`, `Description`, `Priority`, `Enabled`, `Match`, `Cause`; `TenantId` for global admins). Each `Match` list holds at most 256 entries | `201` `FailureCauseRule` (`IsBuiltIn` false, `CreatedByUserId` the caller); `400` invalid (a `Match` list over 256 is `400` with `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`, also on update); `409` duplicate name |
| GET | `/api/v1/failure-cause-rules/{id}` | | `200` `FailureCauseRule`; `404` when missing or another tenant's |
| PUT | `/api/v1/failure-cause-rules/{id}` | `FailureCauseRule` | `200` `FailureCauseRule`: replaces `Name`, `Description`, `Priority`, `Enabled`, `Match`, `Cause` (`TenantId` and `IsBuiltIn` are fixed); `400`; `403`; `404`; `409` |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/failure-cause-rules/{id}` | | `204`; `403`; `404` |

```bash
# Outcomes of one persona that the rules could not explain
curl "http://localhost:7890/api/v1/stage-outcomes?persona=Worker&suspectedCause=Unknown&pageSize=25" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>"

# Classify one by hand, then turn the classification into a rule
curl -X PUT http://localhost:7890/api/v1/stage-outcomes/sto_abc123/cause \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Cause": "Protocol", "Note": "Wrote the result in prose instead of the required block"}'
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/stage-outcomes/sto_abc123/rule -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>"

# Create the (edited) rule and apply it to past attempts
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/failure-cause-rules \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Name": "Prose instead of result block", "Priority": 5, "Cause": "Protocol", "Match": {"FailureKinds": ["InvalidOutput"], "TextPattern": "result block"}}'
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/stage-outcomes/reclassify \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{"SuspectedCause": "Unknown"}'
```

---

### Handoff Artifacts

A **handoff artifact** is the durable, versioned record one stage attempt hands to the next stage
(docs/MODEL_HETEROGENEITY.md, 3.1). Armada stores one per stage attempt (Worker, Test Engineer, Judge, ...) when the
attempt's exit has been handled, linked to its stage outcome (`StageOutcomeId`), mission, persona and captain. It holds:
`SchemaVersion` (1), numbered `Findings` (each with `File`, `Line`, `Severity` `Blocking`/`NonBlocking`/`Unknown`,
`Summary` and `RequiredFix`, parsed from the stage's `Affected Case`, `Findings` and `Issues` sections), `Verdict`
(`PASS`, `NEEDS_REVISION`, `BLOCKED`, `COMPLETE`, ...), `AcceptanceCriteria` (from the task description),
`RequiredActions`, `CheckResults` (`StageCheckResult`: passed, failed, skipped with a reason, could not run),
`CommitReferences` (the produced commit first), `CompletionLines` (the `[ARMADA:...]` protocol lines), the full `Output`
and `Diff` (up to 1,000,000 characters each; a longer one is not stored, `OutputComplete`/`DiffComplete` are false and
`OutputLength`/`DiffLength` keep the size) and `DiffPointer` (where to regenerate the diff). `Findings`,
`AcceptanceCriteria` and `CompletionLines` come from agent output and the task description, so they are bounded when
the artifact is built: each holds at most 256 entries, and a longer list keeps its first 255 and ends with one marker
entry that names how many more there were (a `CompletionLines` marker starts with `[ARMADA:TRUNCATED]`). The verdict
and `RequiredActions` (at most 8) are decided from the full lists; `CheckResults` holds at most 1 and
`CommitReferences` at most 20.

When the next stage launches, Armada renders the prior stage's artifact for that captain within a token budget: one
eighth of the captain's served context when its model endpoint sets a context length, otherwise the `handoffBudgetTokens`
setting (default 4000). Unresolved findings and required actions come first, then check results, then diff sections of
files named in findings, other diff sections, and the stage output (its tail when only part fits); then a pointer to the
complete artifact through a tool the captain has (MCP `get_handoff_artifact`, or for a captain that can only read files
the file `.armada/handoff/<persona>-<missionId>.md` written into its worktree, git-excluded and never committed), and an
explicit list of what did not fit. A captain with neither MCP nor file access is told that nothing beyond the block can
reach it. Nothing is inlined past the budget.

| Method | Path | Body | Response |
|--------|------|------|----------|
| GET | `/api/v1/missions/{id}/handoff-artifacts` | | `200` `EnumerationResult<HandoffArtifact>` of the mission, newest first, **without** `Output` and `Diff` (their lengths stay). Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize` (1-1000, default 25); `400` for a value that does not parse; `404` when the mission is missing or out of scope |
| POST | `/api/v1/missions/{id}/handoff-artifacts/enumerate` | `EnumerationQuery` (optional): `PageNumber`, `PageSize` (1-1000, default 10) | `200` the same page as the GET (without `Output` and `Diff`); querystring paging overrides the body; `404` when the mission is missing or out of scope |
| GET | `/api/v1/handoff-artifacts/{id}` | | `200` `HandoffArtifact` with output and diff; `404` when missing or out of scope |

```bash
curl "http://localhost:7890/api/v1/missions/msn_abc123/handoff-artifacts?pageSize=5" -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>"
curl http://localhost:7890/api/v1/handoff-artifacts/hoa_abc123 -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>"
```

---

### Black Box

A **black box entry** (`bbx_`) records where, how and why a mission or voyage failed: the failure site (phase, step,
host, source file and method), the mechanism (exception, exit code, failing command or git operation), the status
before and after, the workspace and conflict state, the configuration in effect and where each value came from, the
evidence (stored as objects), and the advisor's analysis (findings, a triage verdict, configuration recommendations,
Armada improvements and ranked recovery paths). Armada captures one entry per failed attempt; the newest one is the
subject's **current** entry (`IsCurrent`), and `LastBlackBoxEntryId` on the mission (`GET /api/v1/missions/{id}`) and on
the voyage names it. The record is versioned (`SchemaVersion`; see [BLACKBOX_SCHEMA.md](BLACKBOX_SCHEMA.md)) and the
concepts, retention and storage targets are in [BLACKBOX.md](BLACKBOX.md). Every route calls `IBlackBoxService`, the
same service the MCP tools and WebSocket commands use, so scoping and errors match on every surface.

**Visibility.** A global admin sees every tenant's entries, a tenant admin the entries of their tenant, and a regular
user their own entries (the entry's `UserId`). An entry the caller cannot see is `404` `BlackBoxNotFound`, never `403`,
so nothing leaks whether another tenant's entry exists. An entry being deleted reads as `409` `BlackBoxDeleting`.

**Authorization.** The permission levels below are declared in `RouteAuthorizationRegistry` (resource `BlackBox`).
The handlers narrow further: the recovery and recommendation routes authorize each recorded step as if the caller had
called that MCP tool directly (`restart_mission`, `retry_landing`, `update_vessel` and the other supported tools need a
tenant admin, so a regular user's confirmed run is `403`), and storage migration needs a global admin.

| Routes | Permission |
|---|---|
| The entry, mission, voyage, brief, section, object, download and notes reads (`GET`) and their `POST .../enumerate` twins, `GET /api/v1/blackbox`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/enumerate`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/search`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/bundle`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/hotspots/enumerate`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/fingerprints/enumerate`, `GET .../fingerprints/{fingerprint}/trend` | Authenticated |
| `POST /api/v1/blackbox/{id}/notes`, `POST .../resolve`, `POST .../pin`, `POST .../unpin` | Authenticated |
| `POST /api/v1/blackbox/{id}/recovery/{pathId}`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/{id}/recommendations/{recommendationId}/apply` | Authenticated (each step authorized as its tool) |
| `GET /api/v1/blackbox/retention`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/{id}/reanalyze`, `DELETE /api/v1/blackbox/{id}`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/delete` | TenantAdmin |
| `POST /api/v1/blackbox/{id}/export-fixture`, `GET /api/v1/blackbox/storage`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/storage/migrate`, `GET /api/v1/blackbox/storage/migrations/{id}`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/purge`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/gc`, `POST /api/v1/blackbox/backfill` | AdminOnly |

#### Black box errors

Black box errors use the standard [error body](#error-responses) with a `BlackBoxErrorDetail` in `Data`: `Code` is
the black box code and `Field` names the offending parameter for `InvalidArgument`. Branch on `Data.Code`.

| HTTP | `Error` | `Data.Code` | When |
|---|---|---|---|
| 400 | `BadRequest` | `InvalidArgument` | A bad parameter or body (`Data.Field` names it when known): an unknown section or audience, an object `offset` below 0 or `length` outside 1 to 1048576, a note body outside 1 to 8000 characters, a search query outside 1 to 200 characters, a resolve note over 2000 or link over 1000 characters, an invalid filter value, an advice-only recommendation, or a recovery step that names an unsupported tool or another subject |
| 400 | `BadRequest` | `CollectionLimitExceeded` | More than 1000 ids in `POST /api/v1/blackbox/delete` (a `CollectionLimitErrorDetail`; nothing is deleted) |
| 403 | `Forbidden` | (none) | The route's permission level, a recovery step the caller could not call directly, or storage migration without a global admin |
| 404 | `NotFound` | `BlackBoxNotFound` | The entry, the mission's or voyage's entry, the object, the recovery path or the recommendation does not exist or is not visible |
| 409 | `Conflict` | `BlackBoxDeleting` | The entry is being deleted |
| 409 | `Conflict` | `BlackBoxObjectCorrupt` | The object's bytes do not match its stored SHA-256 |
| 409 | `Conflict` | `BlackBoxConfirmationRequired` | A confirmed recovery or recommendation run without a valid `ConfirmToken`, or a path marked `DataLossRisk` without `AcknowledgeDataLoss` |
| 410 | `NotFound` | `BlackBoxObjectMissing` | The object's row exists but the store lost its bytes |
| 503 | `ServiceUnavailable` | `BlackBoxStoreUnavailable` | The storage target is unreachable, the target is not implemented, or the black box is not wired on this server (every route then answers `503`) |
| 503 | `ServiceUnavailable` | `BlackBoxActionsUnavailable` | A confirmed recovery or recommendation run on a host with no action executor wired |

```json
{
  "Error": "NotFound",
  "StatusCode": 404,
  "Description": "The requested resource was not found.",
  "Message": "Black box entry bbx_doesnotexist_0000000000 not found.",
  "Data": { "Code": "BlackBoxNotFound" }
}
```

```json
{
  "Error": "BadRequest",
  "StatusCode": 400,
  "Description": "The request was invalid or malformed.",
  "Message": "section must be one of Subject, Status, Site, Mechanism, Classification, Workspace, Conflict, Scope, Configuration, Attempts, Evidence, Analysis, Related, Voyage, Redaction or Truncation.",
  "Data": { "Code": "InvalidArgument", "Field": "section" }
}
```

Other service errors keep the standard mapping: `InvalidOperationException` (another storage migration still running)
is `409` `Conflict` without a black box code.

#### GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/blackbox

The mission's current black box entry with its versioned record.

**Permission:** Authenticated

| Parameter | In | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | path | Mission ID (`msn_` prefix) |
| `includeRecord` | query | `false` returns the index row only (no `Record`). Default `true` |

**Response:** `200 OK` with a `BlackBoxEntry` (the index fields plus `Record`, a `BlackBoxRecord`; see
[BLACKBOX_SCHEMA.md](BLACKBOX_SCHEMA.md)). Trimmed:
