### Harbors

Harbor records register host-side runners that let the Admiral run detached (in Docker or on another host) while agent CLIs, git, and worktrees execute on a developer machine over an authenticated client-to-server link. These routes manage the Harbor registrations only -- creating, reading, updating, enabling, disabling, and deleting them. Runtime state such as `ConnectionStatus`, `LastSeenUtc`, `ProtocolVersion`, `OsPlatform`, and `Architecture` is reported by the link and is not operator-editable; the operator-editable fields are `Name`, `MaxConcurrentJobs`, and `Enabled`. **Experimental:** the Harbor routes are excluded from the 1.0 compatibility promise (their OpenAPI summaries start with `[Experimental]`), because Harbor split mode and its link transport are experimental (see [docs/HARBOR.md](HARBOR.md), [docs/HARBOR_PROTOCOL.md](HARBOR_PROTOCOL.md), and [COMPATIBILITY.md](COMPATIBILITY.md)).

#### POST /api/v1/harbors/enumerate

One page of the Harbors visible to the caller, counted and cut in the database (`TotalRecords` is exact for the caller), newest first (`Order` `CreatedAscending` for oldest first; ties break by id). Paging and filters come from the JSON body (`EnumerationQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 1000, default 10, `CreatedAfter`, `CreatedBefore`) or the matching querystring parameters. Each Harbor's `Capabilities` holds at most 256 entries: a create (`POST /api/v1/harbors`) or a Harbor link handshake advertising more is rejected (`CollectionLimitExceeded`; the handshake is refused with the reason).

**Permission:** Authenticated (tenant-scoped; this replaces the removed `GET /api/v1/harbors`)

**Response:** `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<T>` (`PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `TotalPages`, `TotalRecords`, `Objects`)

---

#### GET /api/v1/harbors (removed)

> **Removed** (see [DEPRECATION.md](../DEPRECATION.md), D-001): answers `410 Gone` with a message naming the
> replacement. Use `POST /api/v1/harbors/enumerate`.

#### POST /api/v1/harbors

Register one Harbor. Only `name`, `maxConcurrentJobs`, and `enabled` are honored on create; all other fields are managed by the link.

**Permission:** Authenticated

```json
{
  "name": "workstation-01",
  "maxConcurrentJobs": 4,
  "enabled": true
}
```

- Response: `201 Created` - `Harbor`

#### GET /api/v1/harbors/{id}

Read one Harbor.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `Harbor`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### GET /api/v1/harbors/{id}/metrics

Charts for one Harbor over a window, computed by the Admiral from what the Harbor link reported (see
[Harbor metrics](HARBOR.md#harbor-metrics)). Every series has one entry per bucket over the same buckets.

**Permission:** Authenticated. The caller must be able to read the Harbor (tenant scoping as for `GET /api/v1/harbors/{id}`).
Token usage follows the token-usage scope: an admin, a tenant admin, or the Harbor's owner sees every record on the
Harbor in the tenant; another user only their own.

- Query: `range` = `1h` (60 one-minute buckets), `24h` (48 thirty-minute buckets, the default), or `7d` (56 three-hour buckets)
- Response: `200 OK` - `HarborMetrics`
- Bounds: every per-bucket series has at most 60 entries. `LaunchSpeed` lists at most 16 runtimes: the 15 busiest one by
  one and the rest in one entry with `IsOther` (`Runtime` "Other", `RuntimeCount`). Token `Series` (overall and in each
  bucket) list at most 20 runtime and model pairs: the window's top 19 and one `IsOther` aggregate (`SeriesCount`).
  `Link.Segments` holds at most 1000 stretches; a window with more is shown at bucket resolution with
  `SegmentsCoalesced` true and `RawSegmentCount` (ConnectedPercent still uses every stretch). Aggregates carry the
  totals, so nothing is dropped.
- Errors: `400 Bad Request` (unknown range), `404 Not Found` (no such Harbor, or not visible to the caller)

```json
{
  "HarborId": "hbr_...",
  "HarborName": "Studio Mac",
  "Range": "24h",
  "FromUtc": "2026-10-07T21:00:00Z",
  "ToUtc": "2026-10-08T21:00:00Z",
  "BucketMinutes": 30,
  "BucketCount": 48,
  "GeneratedUtc": "2026-10-08T20:47:12Z",
  "ConnectionStatus": "Connected",
  "Jobs": {
    "Buckets": [{ "BucketStartUtc": "...", "MissionsFinished": 1, "MissionsFailed": 0, "InteractiveFinished": 2, "InteractiveFailed": 0 }],
    "MissionsFinished": 43, "MissionsFailed": 4, "InteractiveFinished": 70, "InteractiveFailed": 14, "Running": 1
  },
  "Slots": {
    "MaxConcurrentJobs": 4,
    "Buckets": [{ "BucketStartUtc": "...", "Peak": 2, "Average": 0.85 }],
    "Peak": 3, "Average": 0.31
  },
  "LaunchSpeed": [{
    "Runtime": "ClaudeCode", "JobCount": 42,
    "FirstOutputCount": 42, "FirstOutputMedianMs": 3506, "FirstOutputP95Ms": 5091,
    "DurationCount": 42, "DurationMedianMs": 74500, "DurationP95Ms": 630000,
    "FirstOutputMedianMsByBucket": [null, 3400, 3650]
  }],
  "Link": {
    "Segments": [{ "State": "Connected", "StartUtc": "...", "EndUtc": "..." }, { "State": "Reconnecting", "StartUtc": "...", "EndUtc": "..." }],
    "RoundTrip": [{ "BucketStartUtc": "...", "HeartbeatCount": 120, "SampleCount": 120, "AverageMs": 18.4, "MaxMs": 312 }],
    "ConnectedPercent": 97.1, "Disconnects": 3, "ReconnectCount": 3, "LastReconnectUtc": "...", "RoundTripMedianMs": 18
  },
  "Tokens": {
    "Buckets": [{ "BucketStartUtc": "...", "InputTokens": 900, "OutputTokens": 300, "CachedTokens": 200, "TotalTokens": 1200,
                  "Series": [{ "Runtime": "ClaudeCode", "Model": "claude-sonnet-4", "InputTokens": 900, "OutputTokens": 300, "CachedTokens": 200, "TotalTokens": 1200 }] }],
    "Series": [{ "Runtime": "ClaudeCode", "Model": "claude-sonnet-4", "TotalTokens": 400336 }],
    "InputTokens": 878800, "OutputTokens": 293000, "CachedTokens": 351500, "TotalTokens": 1171806,
    "RecordCount": 129, "EstimatedCount": 50
  }
}
```

- `Jobs` counts a job in the bucket in which it ended; `Succeeded` and `Stopped` count as finished, `Failed` and `Lost`
  as failed. Interactive means every launch that is not a mission (Ask turns and chat, planning, refinement, context
  builds, other).
- `Slots`: a job holds a slot from its start until it ends; `Average` is time-weighted (over the elapsed part of the
  current bucket).
- `Link.Segments` cover the window up to now; `Unknown` means no link event is recorded for that stretch.
  `Reconnecting` lasts at most `harbor.heartbeatTimeoutSeconds`, after which the stretch is `Down`.
- `Tokens` holds token counts only; Armada records no prices, so there is no cost series.

#### PUT /api/v1/harbors/{id}

Update one Harbor. Only `name`, `maxConcurrentJobs`, and `enabled` are updated; runtime state reported by the link is preserved server-side.

**Permission:** Authenticated

```json
{
  "name": "workstation-01",
  "maxConcurrentJobs": 8,
  "enabled": true
}
```

- Response: `200 OK` - `Harbor`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/harbors/{id}

Delete one Harbor registration.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/harbors/{id}/enable

Enable one Harbor so the router may route new missions to it.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `Harbor`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/harbors/{id}/disable

Disable one Harbor. A disabled Harbor keeps its docks but receives no new missions.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `Harbor`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/harbors/{id}/probe

Run a one-off host command on a connected Harbor over its link and return the result, verifying the end-to-end server-issues-work / Harbor-replies loop. Defaults to `git --version`.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body:** `HarborProbeRequest` (optional)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Executable` | string | no | Executable to run on the Harbor host (default: `git`) |
| `Arguments` | array | no | Command-line arguments (default: `["--version"]`) |
| `WorkingDirectory` | string | no | Working directory on the Harbor host, or empty for the Harbor's default |
| `TimeoutMs` | int | no | Timeout in milliseconds (default: `15000`) |

- Response: `200 OK` - `HostCommandResult`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`; `409 Conflict` when the Harbor is not connected (no link to probe over)

#### GET /api/v1/harbors/{id}/jobs

One page of the captain launches the Admiral recorded on a Harbor, newest launch first (`order=CreatedAscending` for oldest first; ties break by id), counted and cut in the database. Use it with the stop route below to find and stop an agent no mission or captain still points at.

**Permission:** Authenticated (the Harbor must be visible to the caller)

**Query Parameters:**

| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `open` | `true` to list only jobs that have not ended |
| `pageNumber` | Page number (1-based, default 1) |
| `pageSize` | Page size (1 to 1000, default 10) |
| `createdAfter`, `createdBefore` | Launch-time window when `open` is not set (default: the last 7 days) |

- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<HarborJobRecord>` (`JobId`, `MissionId`, `CaptainId`, `Runtime`, `Model`, `LaunchedUtc`, `EndedUtc`, `Outcome`, `ExitCode`, `StopRequested`, ...)
- Errors: `404 Not Found` when the Harbor does not exist or is not visible

#### POST /api/v1/harbors/{id}/jobs/enumerate

The same page as `GET /api/v1/harbors/{id}/jobs`, with paging and the launch-time window in a JSON body.

**Permission:** Authenticated (the Harbor must be visible to the caller)

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional): `PageNumber`, `PageSize` (1 to 1000, default 10), `CreatedAfter`, `CreatedBefore` (default: the last 7 days). `?open=true` in the querystring lists only jobs that have not ended; querystring `pageNumber` and `pageSize` override the body.

- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<HarborJobRecord>`
- Errors: `404 Not Found` when the Harbor does not exist or is not visible

```bash
curl -X POST "http://localhost:7890/api/v1/harbors/hbr_abc123/jobs/enumerate?open=true" \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"PageNumber": 1, "PageSize": 25}'
```

#### POST /api/v1/harbors/{id}/jobs/{jobId}/stop

Ask a connected Harbor to stop one job. The Admiral stops tracking it at once; the Harbor stops the process and reports the exit, which closes the job record.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `202 Accepted` - `HarborJobStopResult` with `Status: "Requested"`; `200 OK` with `Status: "AlreadyEnded"` when the job had already ended (nothing is sent)
- Errors: `404 Not Found` (no such job on that Harbor); `409 Conflict` when the Harbor is not connected. A disconnected Harbor stops jobs this Admiral no longer owns when it reconnects.

---

### Jobs

Jobs are background tasks tracked for status polling.

`Kind` is one of `Generic`, `Cleanup`, `Sync`, `Report` (vessel health evaluation), `VesselDiscovery` (background import discovery), `VesselImport` (background vessel import), and `FleetCategorization` (a captain recommending fleets for an import). Import-related job names include the batch ID (`vib_...`). Cancelling a `VesselDiscovery` or `FleetCategorization` job stops the scan or the captain's process within a few seconds.

#### GET /api/v1/jobs

List one page of background jobs, newest first, scoped to the caller (global admins see every tenant, tenant admins
their tenant, other users their own jobs). There is no unpaged form: without parameters the response is the first page
of 100 (see [DEPRECATION.md](../DEPRECATION.md), D-014; before, the parameterless form returned every job). The
dashboard header polls `?status=Queued,Running`.

**Permission:** Authenticated

| Parameter | Description |
|-----------|-------------|
| `status` | Comma-separated statuses: `Queued`, `Running`, `Succeeded`, `Failed`, `Cancelled` (case-insensitive) |
| `kind` | One job kind, for example `Report` |
| `pageNumber` | One-based page (default 1) |
| `pageSize` | 1 to 1000 (default 100; larger values are clamped) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`Job`\> (`Success`, `PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `TotalPages`, `TotalRecords`, `Objects`). An unknown `status` or `kind` returns `400 Bad Request`.

#### POST /api/v1/jobs/enumerate

The same page with the filters in a JSON body.

**Permission:** Authenticated (scoped like `GET /api/v1/jobs`; a body `TenantId` or `UserId` only narrows a global admin's view)

**Request Body:** `JobQuery` (optional): `Statuses` (array of `Queued`, `Running`, `Succeeded`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`), `Kind`, `PageNumber` (default 1), `PageSize` (1 to 1000, default 100). Querystring `pageNumber` and `pageSize` override the body.

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`Job`\>

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/jobs/enumerate \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Statuses": ["Queued", "Running"], "PageNumber": 1, "PageSize": 25}'
```

#### GET /api/v1/jobs/{id}

Get a single background job by ID.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Job ID (`job_` prefix) |

- Response: `200 OK` - `Job`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/jobs/{id}/cancel

Cancel a background job.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Job ID (`job_` prefix) |

- Response: `200 OK` - `Job` (the cancelled job)
- Errors: `404 Not Found`; `409 Conflict` when the job cannot be cancelled in its current state

---

### Fleet Actions

Fleet actions apply one action across many vessels and record a per-vessel result. A **Command** action runs a shell command in each vessel's working directory and captures exit code and output. A **Mission** action dispatches one voyage per vessel through the normal dispatch path. Every invocation is a persisted, cancellable **run** with one **target** per vessel. See [FLEET_ACTIONS.md](FLEET_ACTIONS.md) for the operator guide.

**Permissions.** Enumerations and `GET` reads are `Authenticated`. Every other write under `/api/v1/fleet-actions` and `/api/v1/fleet-action-runs` is `TenantAdmin`, the same level as voyage dispatch. The service additionally requires tenant admin for anything that creates, edits, deletes, runs, or cancels a **Command** action, because that check depends on the body. All reads and writes are scoped to the caller's tenant; another tenant's ID returns `404`. Denials (`403`) are logged with the request ID.

**Template variables.** `CommandText` and `PromptTemplate` may use `{{vessel.name}}`, `{{vessel.id}}`, `{{vessel.defaultBranch}}`, `{{vessel.workingDirectory}}`, `{{vessel.buildCommand}}` (the vessel's `DefinitionOfDoneBuildCommand`) and `{{health.summary}}`. Names are case-insensitive and may have spaces inside the braces. Any other `{{name}}` is rejected with `400` and the message names it. Values are substituted verbatim in a single pass and never re-expanded.

**Error mapping.** `400` invalid input or unknown template variable; `403` permission; `404` action, run, target, or any target vessel not found in the caller's tenant; `409` cancelling a run that already finished.

#### POST /api/v1/fleet-actions/enumerate

Paged action definitions, newest first. Built-in actions are seeded into the caller's tenant on first use.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body** (`FleetActionEnumerateRequest`, all optional): the standard enumeration fields plus `IncludeInactive`.

```json
{ "PageNumber": 1, "PageSize": 25, "IncludeInactive": false }
```

- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<FleetAction>`

#### POST /api/v1/fleet-actions

Create an action. Command kind requires tenant admin.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body** (`FleetActionUpsertRequest`):

```json
{
  "Name": "Fast-forward default branch",
  "Description": "git pull --ff-only everywhere",
  "Kind": "Command",
  "CommandText": "git pull --ff-only",
  "PromptTemplate": null,
  "PipelineId": null,
  "Persona": null,
  "TimeoutSeconds": 300,
  "DefaultConcurrency": 4,
  "RequiresCleanWorkingTree": true
}
```

| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Name` | string | Required, 1-200 characters |
| `Kind` | `Command` \| `Mission` | Default `Command` |
| `CommandText` | string | Required for Command |
| `PromptTemplate` | string | Required for Mission |
| `PipelineId` | string | Mission only; optional |
| `Persona` | string | Mission only; stored, not yet applied at dispatch |
| `TimeoutSeconds` | int | Default `FleetActions.DefaultTimeoutSeconds`; clamped 5-7200 |
| `DefaultConcurrency` | int | Default 4; clamped 1-32 |
| `RequiresCleanWorkingTree` | bool | Default `true` for Command, `false` for Mission |
| `RequiredTools` | string[] | Executable names; at most 256 |
| `Inputs` | `FleetActionInput[]` | Typed runtime inputs; at most 256, each `AllowedValues` at most 256 |

- Response: `201 Created` - `FleetAction`
- Errors: `400`, `403`. A list over its limit (on create, update and ad hoc runs) is `400` with `Data: {"Code": "CollectionLimitExceeded", "Field", "Limit", "Actual"}` (MCP `InvalidArgument`, `Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`). A command or prompt template that uses an unknown template variable is `400` with `Data: {"Code": "UnknownTemplateVariable", "VariableName": "<name>"}` (also on update and ad-hoc runs; MCP returns `Code` `UnknownTemplateVariable`).

#### GET /api/v1/fleet-actions/{id}

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `FleetAction`
- Errors: `404`

#### PUT /api/v1/fleet-actions/{id}

Partial update: only supplied (non-null) fields change. An empty string clears `Description`, `PipelineId` and `Persona`. Built-in actions can be edited.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request Body: `FleetActionUpsertRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `FleetAction`
- Errors: `400`, `403`, `404`

#### DELETE /api/v1/fleet-actions/{id}

Deletes an action. Built-ins are soft-deleted (`Active = false`) and are never re-seeded; other actions are removed. Past runs keep their snapshot.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `403`, `404`

#### POST /api/v1/fleet-actions/{id}/run

Start a run of a saved action. Every vessel is re-read with the tenant-scoped read before anything is written; a single unknown or cross-tenant vessel rejects the whole request with `404` and no run is created.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body** (`FleetActionRunRequest`):

```json
{
  "VesselIds": ["vsl_abc", "vsl_def"],
  "Concurrency": 2,
  "Overrides": { "TimeoutSeconds": 600, "RequiresCleanWorkingTree": false, "PipelineId": null }
}
```

| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `VesselIds` | string[] | Required; 1-500 after de-duplication; more than 1000 entries is `400` `CollectionLimitExceeded` |
| `InputValues` | object | Values for the action's `Inputs`; at most 256 entries |
| `Concurrency` | int | Optional; defaults to the action's `DefaultConcurrency`; clamped 1-32 |
| `Overrides.TimeoutSeconds` | int | Optional; clamped 5-7200 |
| `Overrides.RequiresCleanWorkingTree` | bool | Optional; Command only |
| `Overrides.PipelineId` | string | Optional; Mission only; empty string clears |

**Response:** `202 Accepted` - `FleetActionRunStartResult` (never contains output)

```json
{
  "RunId": "far_...",
  "ActionId": "fac_...",
  "Kind": "Command",
  "Status": "Pending",
  "TargetCount": 2,
  "Concurrency": 2
}
```

- Errors: `400`, `403`, `404`

#### POST /api/v1/fleet-actions/run

Ad hoc run with an inline definition; the run's `ActionId` is null. Same body as above plus `Definition` (a `FleetActionUpsertRequest`, validated exactly as on create). `Overrides` are ignored for ad hoc runs; put the values in `Definition`.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "VesselIds": ["vsl_abc"],
  "Concurrency": 1,
  "Definition": { "Name": "Status", "Kind": "Command", "CommandText": "git status -sb", "RequiresCleanWorkingTree": false }
}
```

- Response: `202 Accepted` - `FleetActionRunStartResult`

#### POST /api/v1/fleet-action-runs/enumerate

Paged runs, newest first. `Status` filters by run status (`Pending`, `Running`, `Completed`, `CompletedWithFailures`, `Cancelled`, `Failed`); an unknown value is `400`.

**Permission:** Authenticated

```json
{ "PageNumber": 1, "PageSize": 25, "Status": "Running" }
```

- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<FleetActionRun>`

#### GET /api/v1/fleet-action-runs/{id}

The run with its target counts. Targets are not embedded (see [ENUMERATION.md](ENUMERATION.md)): page them with
`POST /api/v1/fleet-action-runs/{id}/targets/enumerate`.

**Permission:** Authenticated

```json
{
  "Run": { "Id": "far_...", "ActionName": "Build", "Kind": "Command", "Status": "Running", "TargetCount": 3, "SucceededCount": 1, "FailedCount": 0, "SkippedCount": 1, "CancelledCount": 0, "...": "..." }
}
```

- Errors: `404`

#### POST /api/v1/fleet-action-runs/{id}/targets/enumerate

Paged target summaries, ordered by creation. No output text.

```json
{
  "Id": "fat_...", "RunId": "far_...", "VesselId": "vsl_...", "VesselName": "api",
  "Status": "Succeeded", "SkipReason": null, "FailureReason": null, "ExitCode": 0,
  "OutputTruncated": false, "OutputLength": 1834, "ErrorLength": 0, "RenderedLength": 18,
  "VoyageId": null, "StartedUtc": "2026-10-03T20:00:00Z", "CompletedUtc": "2026-10-03T20:00:04Z",
  "DurationMs": 4021, "CreatedUtc": "...", "LastUpdateUtc": "..."
}
```

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body** (`FleetActionTargetEnumerateRequest`): `PageNumber` (from 1), `PageSize` (default 25, clamped 1-500), `Status` (optional target status).

- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<FleetActionRunTargetSummary>`
- Errors: `404`

#### GET /api/v1/fleet-action-runs/{id}/targets/{targetId}

One target including `RenderedText`, `OutputText` and `ErrorText`. This is the only endpoint that returns captured output.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `FleetActionRunTarget`
- Errors: `404`

#### POST /api/v1/fleet-action-runs/{id}/cancel

Cancel a run. Pending targets become `Cancelled`; running Command targets are killed and become `Cancelled`; running Mission targets have their voyage cancelled (pending and assigned missions are cancelled, missions already in progress finish) unless the voyage already finished. Cancelling an already cancelled run returns it unchanged. No body.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `200 OK` - `FleetActionRun`
- Errors: `403`, `404`, `409` (run already finished)

#### Fleet action models

`FleetAction`: `Id` (`fac_`), `TenantId`, `UserId`, `Name`, `Description`, `Kind`, `CommandText`, `PromptTemplate`, `PipelineId`, `Persona`, `TimeoutSeconds`, `DefaultConcurrency`, `RequiresCleanWorkingTree`, `IsBuiltIn`, `BuiltInKey`, `Active`, `CreatedUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc`.

`FleetActionRun`: `Id` (`far_`), `TenantId`, `UserId`, `ActionId` (null for ad hoc), snapshot fields `ActionName`, `Kind`, `CommandText`, `PromptTemplate`, `PipelineId`, `Persona`, `TimeoutSeconds`, `RequiresCleanWorkingTree`, plus `Concurrency`, `Status`, `TargetCount`, `SucceededCount`, `FailedCount` (Failed + TimedOut), `SkippedCount`, `CancelledCount`, `StartedUtc`, `CompletedUtc`, `CreatedUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc`.

`FleetActionRunTarget`: `Id` (`fat_`), `TenantId`, `RunId`, `VesselId`, `VesselName`, `Status`, `SkipReason`, `FailureReason`, `RenderedText`, `ExitCode`, `OutputText`, `ErrorText`, `OutputTruncated`, `VoyageId`, `StartedUtc`, `CompletedUtc`, `DurationMs`, `CreatedUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc`. Output is truncated to the last `FleetActions.MaxOutputBytes` bytes of each stream.

Run status: `Pending`, `Running`, `Completed` (no failures), `CompletedWithFailures` (at least one Failed or TimedOut target), `Cancelled`, `Failed` (the runner itself failed). Target status: `Pending`, `Skipped`, `Running`, `Succeeded`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`, `TimedOut`.

Skip reasons: `DirtyTree`, `NoWorkingDirectory`, `NoBuildCommand`, `DispatchRejected` (validator message in `ErrorText`), `VesselNotFound`, `HarborUnavailable`, `NotAuthorized` (reserved). Failure reasons: `Interrupted`, `NonZeroExit`, `Timeout`, `GitStatusFailed`, `TemplateError`, `ExecutionError`, `DispatchFailed`, `DispatchUnavailable`, `VoyageFailed`, `VoyageMissing`.

---

### Inbox

The inbox is a consolidated, most-urgent-first list of items awaiting a human decision or intervention. Purely
informational changes (completions, normal progress) are not included. Items are ordered by `Severity` (`Critical`,
then `Warning`), then by `Title`. Every item is returned (no category is capped); only pending CLI permission requests stop at 1000, since they expire within minutes.

| `Kind` | `Severity` | `EntityType` | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| `review` | `Warning` (`Critical` once the review deadline has passed) | `mission` | Mission in `Review` |
| `landing_failed` | `Critical` | `mission` | Mission in `LandingFailed` |
| `failed` | `Warning` | `mission` | Mission in `Failed` |
| `stalled_captain` | `Warning` | `captain` | Captain in `Stalled` |
| `merge_failed` | `Critical` | `merge_entry` | Merge queue entry in `Failed` |
| `deployment_approval` | `Warning` | `deployment` | Deployment pending approval |
| `deployment_failed` | `Critical` | `deployment` | Deployment `Failed` or `VerificationFailed` |
| `ask_proposal` | `Warning` | `ask_proposal` | Pending, unexpired Ask Armada action proposal in one of the caller's own threads (`Href` is `/ask/{threadId}`) |
| `cli_permission` | `Warning` | `cli_permission_request` | Pending, unexpired [CLI permission request](#cli-permissions) the caller may see (`Href` is `/ask/{threadId}` for a request from the caller's own Ask thread, otherwise `/server?tab=cli-permissions&request={id}`; the pre-v1.2.0 `/cli-permissions?request={id}` still redirects there) |

Operational items are scoped like other reads: a global admin sees everything, a tenant admin sees the tenant, and a
regular user sees only the items they own. Ask proposals are always limited to the caller's own threads (only the
thread owner can approve them), and proposals older than `Ask.ProposalExpiryMinutes` are left out. CLI permission
requests are visible to global admins, the tenant's tenant admins, and the owner of the thread or mission; the owner can
decide only when `Permissions.AllowOwnerApproval` is on, otherwise `Detail` ends with "An admin must decide it."

#### POST /api/v1/inbox/enumerate

One page of the needs-you inbox items (`TotalRecords` is the exact count). Each kind is read from its own source one page at a time (missions, merge entries, and deployments are paged and counted in the database under the caller's scope), and kinds follow a fixed urgency order: `review`, `landing_failed`, `merge_failed`, `deployment_failed`, `deployment_approval`, `ask_proposal`, `cli_permission`, `failed`, `stalled_captain`, newest first within a kind. Paging comes from the JSON body (`EnumerationQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 1000, default 10) or the matching querystring parameters.

**Permission:** Authenticated (scoped as described above; this replaces the removed `GET /api/v1/inbox`)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`InboxItem`\> (`PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `TotalPages`, `TotalRecords`, `Objects`)

```json
{
  "Success": true,
  "PageNumber": 1,
  "PageSize": 10,
  "TotalPages": 1,
  "TotalRecords": 1,
  "Objects": [
    {
      "Kind": "review",
      "Severity": "Warning",
      "Title": "Review: Add retry logic",
      "Detail": "Awaiting your review.",
      "EntityType": "mission",
      "EntityName": "Add retry logic",
      "EntityId": "msn_abc123",
      "Href": "/missions/msn_abc123"
    }
  ]
}
```

#### GET /api/v1/inbox (removed)

> **Removed** (see [DEPRECATION.md](../DEPRECATION.md), D-006): answers `410 Gone` with a message naming the
> replacement. Use `POST /api/v1/inbox/enumerate`.

#### InboxItem fields

`EntityName` is the display name of the referenced entity (mission title, captain name, deployment environment, merge
target branch, or the proposal summary); use it instead of parsing `Title`. Deployment items also carry
`EnvironmentName` and `DeploymentTitle` (null for other kinds); their `Title` is the approval label
"Deploy to <environment>: <title>" ("Deploy to <environment>" without a title, "Deploy: <title>" without an
environment), which clients can rebuild and localize from those two fields.

`cli_permission` items also carry `CliPermission` (the [CliPermissionRequest](#clipermissionrequest): tool, input summary,
captain, vessel, mission or thread, expiry, and the caller's `CanDecide` and `CanRemember`) and `ExpiresUtc` (the request
expiry); both are null for other kinds. Their `EntityName` is the tool name.

---

### Skills

Skills are Category B shared configuration entities (`scope`: `TenantWide` or `UserSpecific`). Reads return tenant-wide plus the caller's own user-specific skills; a regular user creates/edits/deletes only their own user-specific skills, and a tenant-wide skill requires a tenant admin.

**Paging and visibility:** list and enumerate routes page in the database with the caller's visibility applied before counting (a regular user sees the tenant-wide rows plus their own, a tenant admin the whole tenant), so every page is full and `TotalRecords` is exact for the caller. `pageSize` defaults to 10 and is normalized into 1..1000 (querystring and body alike); ties are broken by id, so pages never overlap.

#### GET /api/v1/skills

One page of the skills visible to the caller (admins see all; tenant admins their tenant; others their tenant's
tenant-wide skills plus their own), ordered by name with ties broken by id. Visibility applies in the database before
counting, so `TotalRecords` is exact for the caller.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:**
| Parameter | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `pageNumber` | integer | One-based page number |
| `pageSize` | integer | Page size |
| `category` | string | Optional category filter |
| `search` | string | Optional name/description search |
| `active` | bool | Optional active-state filter |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`Skill`\>

#### POST /api/v1/skills/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of skills. Query-string parameters override body values.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** `SkillQuery` (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`Skill`\>

#### POST /api/v1/skills

Create a skill. A regular user may create only user-specific skills; the requested scope is coerced accordingly.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** `Skill`

- Response: `201 Created` - `Skill`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request` when `Name` is missing

#### GET /api/v1/skills/{id}

Get a single skill by ID.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Skill ID (`skl_` prefix) |

- Response: `200 OK` - `Skill`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/skills/{id}

Update a skill. Only tenant/global admins may change an existing skill's ownership scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Skill ID (`skl_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** `Skill`

- Response: `200 OK` - `Skill`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`; `403 Forbidden` when modifying a skill the caller does not own

#### DELETE /api/v1/skills/{id}

Delete a skill.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Skill ID (`skl_` prefix) |

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`; `403 Forbidden` when deleting a skill the caller does not own

---

### Project Profiles

Project profiles are Category B shared configuration entities that bundle a project's pipeline, workflow profile, persona overrides, and skills. Ownership is expressed via `ownershipScope` (`TenantWide` or `UserSpecific`), while `scope` carries the application scope (`Global`, `Fleet`, or `Vessel`). Reads return tenant-wide plus the caller's own user-specific profiles.

**Paging and visibility:** list and enumerate routes page in the database with the caller's visibility applied before counting (a regular user sees the tenant-wide rows plus their own, a tenant admin the whole tenant), so every page is full and `TotalRecords` is exact for the caller. `pageSize` defaults to 10 and is normalized into 1..1000 (querystring and body alike); ties are broken by id, so pages never overlap. `PersonaOverrides` and `Skills` hold at most 256 entries each (`CollectionLimits.MaxConfigItems`): create, update and
validate reject more with `400 Bad Request`, `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded` (`Field`, `Limit`, `Actual`).

#### GET /api/v1/project-profiles

List project profiles scoped to the authenticated tenant.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:**
| Parameter | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `pageNumber` | integer | One-based page number |
| `pageSize` | integer | Page size |
| `scope` | string | Optional scope filter (`Global`, `Fleet`, `Vessel`) |
| `fleetId` | string | Optional fleet filter |
| `vesselId` | string | Optional vessel filter |
| `search` | string | Optional name/description search |
| `active` | bool | Optional active-state filter |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`ProjectProfile`\>

#### POST /api/v1/project-profiles/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of project profiles. Query-string parameters override body values.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** `ProjectProfileQuery` (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`ProjectProfile`\>

#### POST /api/v1/project-profiles/validate

Validate a project-profile definition (scope consistency, referenced entities, persona overrides) without saving it.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** `ProjectProfile`

**Response:** `200 OK` - `ProjectProfileValidationResult`

#### GET /api/v1/project-profiles/resolve/vessels/{vesselId}

Resolve the best matching active project profile for a vessel using vessel, then fleet, then global precedence.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `vesselId` | Vessel ID (`vsl_` prefix) |

**Query Parameters:**
| Parameter | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `projectProfileId` | string | Optional explicit project-profile override |

- Response: `200 OK` - `ProjectProfileResolutionResult`
- Errors: `404 Not Found` when the vessel is not found or no profile can be resolved

#### GET /api/v1/project-profiles/{id}/persona-preview/{persona}

Return the base and effective (override-applied) persona prompt so the dashboard can render a live diff.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Project profile ID (`ppf_` prefix) |
| `persona` | Persona name (e.g. `Architect`, `Worker`, `Test Engineer`) |

- Response: `200 OK` - `PersonaPromptPreview`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/project-profiles

Create a project profile. A regular user may create only user-specific profiles; the requested ownership scope is coerced accordingly.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** `ProjectProfile`

- Response: `201 Created` - `ProjectProfile`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request` when validation fails

#### GET /api/v1/project-profiles/{id}

Get a single project profile by ID.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Project profile ID (`ppf_` prefix) |

- Response: `200 OK` - `ProjectProfile`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/project-profiles/{id}

Update a project profile. Only tenant/global admins may change the ownership scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Project profile ID (`ppf_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** `ProjectProfile`

- Response: `200 OK` - `ProjectProfile`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`; `403 Forbidden` when modifying a profile the caller does not own; `400 Bad Request` when validation fails

#### DELETE /api/v1/project-profiles/{id}

Delete a project profile.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Project profile ID (`ppf_` prefix) |

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`; `403 Forbidden` when deleting a profile the caller does not own

### Playbooks

Playbooks are tenant-scoped markdown documents that can be attached to voyages or standalone missions. Each selection carries its own delivery mode so the model receives either the full content inline or a file path it should read.

**Paging and visibility:** list and enumerate routes page in the database with the caller's visibility applied before counting (a regular user sees the tenant-wide rows plus their own, a tenant admin the whole tenant), so every page is full and `TotalRecords` is exact for the caller. `pageSize` defaults to 10 and is normalized into 1..1000 (querystring and body alike); ties are broken by id, so pages never overlap.

#### GET /api/v1/playbooks

List playbooks with pagination.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<[Playbook](#playbook)\>

#### POST /api/v1/playbooks/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of playbooks with optional filtering and sorting.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<[Playbook](#playbook)\>

#### POST /api/v1/playbooks

Create a playbook.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body:** [Playbook](#playbook). `FileName` must end with `.md` (default `PLAYBOOK.md`) and `Content` must not be empty.

**Response:** `201 Created` - [Playbook](#playbook). `409 Conflict` when a playbook with that file name already exists (`Data`: [DuplicateEntityErrorDetail](#duplicateentityerrordetail) with `Code` `DuplicateEntity`, `EntityType` `Playbook`, `Field` `FileName`).

#### GET /api/v1/playbooks/{id}

Return a single playbook by ID.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Playbook](#playbook)

#### PUT /api/v1/playbooks/{id}

Update a playbook's file name, description, content, or active state.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body:** [Playbook](#playbook)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [Playbook](#playbook). `404` when not found, `409` when the new file name is taken (`Data.Code` `DuplicateEntity`, `Data.Field` `FileName`).

#### DELETE /api/v1/playbooks/{id}

Delete a playbook. Existing mission snapshots remain immutable.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Response:** `200 OK` - `{ "Status": "deleted", "PlaybookId": "pbk_..." }`. `404` when not found.

### Prompt Templates

Prompt templates define the instruction text used when generating captain mission briefs. Armada ships with built-in templates that can be customized. Custom templates can also be created per tenant.

**Paging and visibility:** list and enumerate routes page in the database with the caller's visibility applied before counting (a regular user sees the tenant-wide rows plus their own, a tenant admin the whole tenant), so every page is full and `TotalRecords` is exact for the caller. `pageSize` defaults to 10 and is normalized into 1..1000 (querystring and body alike); ties are broken by id, so pages never overlap.

The built-in **`ask.system`** template (category `ask`) is the system prompt prepended to every **Ask Armada** dashboard chat turn. It is seeded automatically on first run and is editable exactly like any other template - through **Configuration > Prompts** in the dashboard, through these REST endpoints (`GET`/`PUT /api/v1/prompt-templates/ask.system`, `POST /api/v1/prompt-templates/ask.system/reset`), or through the MCP tools (`get_prompt_template`, `update_prompt_template`, `reset_prompt_template` with `name = "ask.system"`).

#### GET /api/v1/prompt-templates

List all prompt templates with pagination.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:** [Pagination parameters](#pagination-parameters)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<PromptTemplate\>

```bash
curl http://localhost:7890/api/v1/prompt-templates
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/prompt-templates/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of prompt templates with optional filtering and sorting.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<PromptTemplate\>

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/prompt-templates/enumerate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"PageSize": 10}'
```

#### POST /api/v1/prompt-templates

Create a prompt template.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Name` | string | yes | Unique template name |
| `Category` | string | yes | Template category such as `persona`, `mission`, or `structure` |
| `Content` | string | yes | Template content text |
| `Description` | string | no | Template description |
| `Active` | bool | no | Whether the template is active |

**Response:** `201 Created` - PromptTemplate
**Error:** `409 Conflict` - A prompt template with that name already exists (`Data`: [DuplicateEntityErrorDetail](#duplicateentityerrordetail) with `Code` `DuplicateEntity`, `EntityType` `PromptTemplate`, `Field` `Name`)

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/prompt-templates \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Name": "persona.product_manager.copy", "Category": "persona", "Content": "You are...", "Description": "Derived persona template"}'
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/prompt-templates/{name}

Get a prompt template by name.

**Permission:** Authenticated

| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Template name |

**Response:** `200 OK` - PromptTemplate

```bash

```

#### PUT /api/v1/prompt-templates/{name}

**Permission:** Authenticated

| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Template name |

**Request Body:**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Content` | string | yes | Template content text |
| `Description` | string | no | Template description |

### Personas

A persona associates a name and description with a prompt template. Personas are used to configure the behavior of captains within a pipeline. Armada ships with built-in personas that cannot be deleted.

#### GET /api/v1/personas

One page of the personas visible to the caller (admins see all; tenant admins their tenant; others their tenant's
tenant-wide personas plus their own), counted and cut in the database so `TotalRecords` is exact for the caller, newest
first with ties broken by id. `POST /api/v1/personas/enumerate` is the POST twin.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:** [Pagination parameters](#pagination-parameters)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<Persona\>

```bash
curl http://localhost:7890/api/v1/personas
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/personas/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of personas with optional filtering and sorting.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<Persona\>

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/personas/enumerate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"PageSize": 10}'
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/personas/{name}

Get a persona by name.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Persona name |

**Response:** `200 OK` - Persona
**Error:** `404` - Persona not found

```bash
curl http://localhost:7890/api/v1/personas/default
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/personas

Create a new persona.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Name` | string | yes | Persona name |
| `Description` | string | no | Persona description |
| `PromptTemplateName` | string | yes | Name of the prompt template to use |
| `DefaultCaptainId` | string? | no | Legacy preferred captain (must be visible to the caller); superseded by a nonempty hierarchy |
| `Scope` | enum? | no | TenantWide or UserSpecific; a regular user always gets UserSpecific, an administrator defaults to TenantWide |
| `CaptainPriorities` | object[] | no | Ordered matching classes (at most 256; more is a 400 with `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`); defaults empty |
| `PriorityAvailability` | enum | no | FallThrough (default) or WaitForFirstPriority |
| `PriorityExhaustion` | enum | no | Wait (default) or UseEligibleCaptain; ignored by first-priority wait |

Each priority has optional Runtime (AgentRuntimeEnum), Model (exact case-insensitive identifier, maximum 1024 characters), Endpoint (Mux endpoint name, case-insensitive, when Runtime is Mux; exact case-sensitive managed inference endpoint ID matching Captain.ModelEndpointId when Runtime is ApiEndpoint; maximum 1024 characters), and MinimumTier (Economy, Standard, Premium). At least one field is required. Entries combine their fields with AND; array order defines priority. Other runtimes cannot specify Endpoint. For example, `{ "Runtime": "ApiEndpoint", "Endpoint": "mep_your_endpoint" }` restricts the priority to captains bound to that endpoint. Runtime ApiEndpoint without Endpoint preserves the legacy any-API-captain match. Invalid routing returns 400. Persona reads and enumerations return these fields, including an empty CaptainPriorities array for legacy personas.

```json
{"Name":"reviewer","PromptTemplateName":"persona.judge","CaptainPriorities":[{"Runtime":"Codex","MinimumTier":"Premium"},{"Runtime":"Mux","Endpoint":"local","Model":"gpt-oss:120b"}],"PriorityAvailability":"FallThrough","PriorityExhaustion":"Wait"}
```

The persona is owned by the caller. `IsBuiltIn` is ignored: only Armada's own personas are built in.

**Response:** `201 Created` - Persona
**Errors:** `400` - `Name` or `PromptTemplateName` is missing, the default captain is not found, or the routing configuration is invalid; `409 Conflict` - A persona with that name already exists (`Data`: [DuplicateEntityErrorDetail](#duplicateentityerrordetail) with `Code` `DuplicateEntity`, `EntityType` `Persona`, `Field` `Name`)

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/personas \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Name": "reviewer", "Description": "Code review specialist", "PromptTemplateName": "default"}'
```

---

#### PUT /api/v1/personas/{name}

Update an existing persona. Omitted routing fields preserve their stored values; an empty CaptainPriorities array clears the hierarchy. Null routing fields also preserve their values. An omitted `DefaultCaptainId` keeps the stored default captain; an explicit `null` or empty string clears it. Scope changes require administrator privileges.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Persona name |

**Request Body:**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Description` | string | no | Updated description |
| `PromptTemplateName` | string | no | Updated prompt template name |
| `DefaultCaptainId` | string? | no | Legacy preferred captain (must be visible to the caller); omitted keeps it, explicit null or empty clears it |
| `CaptainPriorities` | object[]? | no | Ordered matching classes described under POST (at most 256); omitted/null preserves, empty clears |
| `PriorityAvailability` | enum? | no | FallThrough or WaitForFirstPriority; omitted/null preserves |
| `PriorityExhaustion` | enum? | no | Wait or UseEligibleCaptain; omitted/null preserves |
| `Scope` | enum? | no | TenantWide or UserSpecific; administrator-only change |

**Response:** `200 OK` - Persona
**Errors:** `400` - Invalid routing configuration, an empty `PromptTemplateName`, or a default captain that is not found; `403` - Caller may not edit this persona; `404` - Persona not found

```bash
curl -X PUT http://localhost:7890/api/v1/personas/reviewer \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Description": "Updated reviewer persona", "PromptTemplateName": "review-template"}'
```

---

#### DELETE /api/v1/personas/{name}

Delete a persona. Built-in personas cannot be deleted.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Persona name |

**Response:** `204 No Content`
**Errors:** `400` - Built-in persona cannot be deleted; `403` - Caller may not delete this persona; `404` - Persona not found

```bash
curl -X DELETE http://localhost:7890/api/v1/personas/reviewer
```

### Pipelines

A pipeline defines an ordered sequence of stages, each associated with a persona. Pipelines control the multi-stage workflow that missions progress through. Armada ships with built-in pipelines that cannot be deleted.

**Paging and visibility:** list and enumerate routes page in the database with the caller's visibility applied before counting (a regular user sees the tenant-wide rows plus their own, a tenant admin the whole tenant), so every page is full and `TotalRecords` is exact for the caller. `pageSize` defaults to 10 and is normalized into 1..1000 (querystring and body alike); ties are broken by id, so pages never overlap. `Stages` holds at most 256 entries (`CollectionLimits.MaxConfigItems`): `POST` and `PUT` (and the MCP and WebSocket
create/update forms) reject more with `400 Bad Request`, `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`, and store nothing.

#### GET /api/v1/pipelines

List all pipelines with pagination. Response includes the stages for each pipeline.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Query Parameters:** [Pagination parameters](#pagination-parameters)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<Pipeline\>

```bash
curl http://localhost:7890/api/v1/pipelines
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/pipelines/enumerate

Paginated enumeration of pipelines with optional filtering and sorting.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) (optional)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<Pipeline\>

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/pipelines/enumerate \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"PageSize": 10}'
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/pipelines/{name}

Get a pipeline by name, including its stages.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Pipeline name |

**Response:** `200 OK` - Pipeline
**Error:** `404` - Pipeline not found

```bash
curl http://localhost:7890/api/v1/pipelines/default
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/pipelines

Create a new pipeline with stages.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Name` | string | yes | Pipeline name |
| `Description` | string | no | Pipeline description |
| `Stages` | array | yes | Ordered list of pipeline stages |

**Stage fields:**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `PersonaName` | string | yes | Name of the persona for this stage |
| `IsOptional` | bool | no | Reserved: stored and returned, but dispatch runs every stage regardless (default: false) |
| `Description` | string | no | Stage description |
| `Order` | int | no | 1-based execution order (default 1). Set it on every stage of a multi-stage pipeline; the REST API does not number stages for you |
| `RequiresReview` | bool | no | Park the stage's mission in `Review` until a reviewer approves it (default: false) |
| `ReviewDenyAction` | string | no | `RetryStage` (default) or `FailPipeline` when the review is denied |

**Response:** `201 Created` - Pipeline
**Error:** `409 Conflict` - A pipeline with that name already exists (`Data`: [DuplicateEntityErrorDetail](#duplicateentityerrordetail) with `Code` `DuplicateEntity`, `EntityType` `Pipeline`, `Field` `Name`)

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/pipelines \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Name": "review-pipeline", "Description": "Code with review", "Stages": [{"Order": 1, "PersonaName": "Worker", "Description": "Implementation"}, {"Order": 2, "PersonaName": "Judge", "IsOptional": false, "Description": "Code review"}]}'
```

---

#### PUT /api/v1/pipelines/{name}

Update an existing pipeline.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Pipeline name |

**Request Body:**

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Description` | string | no | Updated description |
| `Stages` | array | no | Updated ordered list of pipeline stages (replaces all existing stages) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - Pipeline
**Error:** `404` - Pipeline not found

```bash
curl -X PUT http://localhost:7890/api/v1/pipelines/review-pipeline \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"Description": "Updated pipeline", "Stages": [{"PersonaName": "default"}, {"PersonaName": "reviewer"}]}'
```

#### DELETE /api/v1/pipelines/{name}

Delete a pipeline. Built-in pipelines cannot be deleted.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `name` | Pipeline name |

**Response:** `204 No Content`
**Error:** `404` - Pipeline not found
**Error:** `403` - Built-in pipeline cannot be deleted

```bash
curl -X DELETE http://localhost:7890/api/v1/pipelines/review-pipeline
```

### Model Endpoints

A model endpoint is a managed reference to an external embedding or inference (chat/completion) model behind a provider API. Armada stores the endpoint, health-checks it (deduplicated by base URL), and can validate one with a real request. The stored `ApiKey` is write-only: it is accepted on create and update but is never returned on reads. Reads expose `HasApiKey` instead.

Two capability constraints are enforced:

- `Anthropic` cannot be used with `Kind` `Embedding` (no embeddings API).
- `VoyageAI` cannot be used with `Kind` `Inference` (embeddings only).

Requests that violate either constraint are rejected with `400 Bad Request`.

**Ollama Cloud and xAI endpoints**

`OllamaCloud` and `XAI` support both `Inference` and `Embedding` through PolyPrompt 3.5.0. Both require an API key and accept only `ProviderDefault` or `Bearer` authentication. Credentials are write-only; omit the API key on update to preserve it.

Ollama Cloud uses the fixed URL `https://ollama.com:443` (omit the base URL or supply that address). Choose a hosted model; embedding availability depends on the cloud catalog. xAI defaults to `https://api.x.ai` when the base URL is omitted, uses the native Responses API for inference, and requires an explicit accessible embedding model for embeddings.

To use either for a captain, create an `Inference` endpoint, set the captain runtime to `ApiEndpoint`, and set `ModelEndpointId` to the endpoint ID. The chosen model must support tool calling for agent work.

CLI example: `armada captain add grok-worker --runtime api --model-endpoint <endpoint-id>`.

#### Inference execution controls

Endpoint create/update accepts `inferenceOptions`, returned on reads and shared with the .NET SDK. It controls tool enablement, read-only built-ins, scoped MCP tools, exact tool allowlists, maximum model turns/output tokens, temperature, top P and required Harbor execution. See [API endpoint captains in pipelines](CAPTAIN_ROUTING.md#api-endpoint-captains-in-pipelines) for fields, defaults, validation, examples and remaining configuration gaps.

Omit `inferenceOptions` on PUT to preserve the stored policy. Supplying an object replaces the policy; explicit null resets defaults. Invalid limits or duplicate/blank allowlist names return 400. The policy is snapshotted at launch and travels to the Harbor alongside the resolved endpoint. Nondefault settings require a policy-capable Harbor; `requireHarbor` prevents local fallback.

#### Duplicate an endpoint without re-entering credentials

`POST /api/v1/model-endpoints/{id}/duplicate` creates an edited copy when the caller can **edit** the source endpoint. Send the complete configuration for the new endpoint (the same fields as create). Omit `apiKey` to copy the source credential server-side, supply a string to replace it, or explicit null to clear it. Omit `headers` to copy all source headers; send a list to replace it, with null values inheriting by case-insensitive header name and `[]` removing all headers. Omitted `inferenceOptions` inherit the source policy. The source remains unchanged.

The result is 201 with a fresh endpoint ID, caller ownership, normal create-scope rules and fresh health state. Credentials remain write-only. The operation performs no inference request. A visible source the caller cannot edit returns 403; an invisible or missing source returns 404; malformed or invalid edited configuration returns 400; unauthenticated requests return 401. Read access to a shared endpoint does not authorize copying its secrets to a different address.

```json
{
  "name": "Ollama Cloud second model",
  "kind": "Inference",
  "provider": "OllamaCloud",
  "baseUrl": "https://ollama.com:443",
  "model": "your-second-model",
  "authType": "Bearer"
}
```

The dashboard row menu's **Duplicate** opens an unsaved, prefilled endpoint form. Edit the model, name or settings, then **Save Copy**; leaving the credential field blank inherits the source credential. Cancel creates nothing. The .NET SDK exposes `DuplicateModelEndpointAsync(sourceId, editedConfiguration, apiKey: null)`. MCP exposes `duplicate_model_endpoint` with `endpointId` and `configuration` arguments.

In the dashboard's create/edit inference endpoint form, select **Create a captain for this endpoint** to create an `ApiEndpoint` captain after saving. The optional captain name defaults to the endpoint name. This also works when duplicating an inference endpoint. The captain references the saved endpoint ID and inherits its model and inference settings; credentials remain on the endpoint. The option is off by default and unavailable for embedding endpoints. If captain creation fails, the saved endpoint is retained and the form offers a retry without creating another endpoint.

#### Dashboard duplication coverage

Reusable configuration tables offer Duplicate: fleets, vessels, captains, endpoints, skills, project profiles, workflow profiles, personas, pipelines, prompt templates, playbooks, fleet actions, environments and runbooks. Harbors can copy their editable registration settings; the new registration does not reuse the source machine identity or link. Existing duplication flows open the copied record's editor. Newly added endpoint, skill, profile and Harbor actions prefill the create form for review before saving.

Memories copy editable content, tags, salience and vessel context into a new Manual memory; they do not reuse an upsert key, ID, version or agent provenance. Missions copy full instructions, mode, priority, persona, explicit requested captain, review policy and playbook references into an editable standalone Pending mission. Old voyage/dependency links, actual captain, process, branch, diff, review outcomes and landing state are excluded. Mission menus are also available on the home dashboard and captain/vessel/voyage detail tables; fleet detail vessel menus link to vessel duplication. Pipeline stage tables also offer Duplicate, opening the pipeline editor with an independent stage copy that is applied only on Save.

Deployment copies open the create form with immediate execution off. Release copies reset to Draft and clear version/tag identity; incident copies reset to Open and omit the old timeline and resolution. These actions never rerun a deployment, publish a release, or copy recorded outcomes merely by opening the menu.

Duplication is deliberately absent from observed or historical tables: voyages (execution graphs), docks/worktrees, checks, jobs, signals/events, request/activity/history logs, merge queue entries, execution results, storage inventory, discovered runtime tools, git branch inventory, health/metrics and import results. Reuse their underlying definition, retry/run controls, or the individual mission duplication action instead. Tenant, user, credential and CLI permission-rule tables also do not clone identities, grants or credentials.

#### POST /api/v1/model-endpoints/enumerate

One page of the model endpoints visible to the caller (API keys are never returned), counted and cut in the database with the caller's visibility applied before counting, newest first with ties broken by id. Paging and filters come from the JSON body (`EnumerationQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 1000, default 10, `Order`, `CreatedAfter`, `CreatedBefore`) or the matching querystring parameters. Each endpoint embeds at most 500 `HealthHistory` records.

**Permission:** Authenticated (tenant-wide endpoints plus the caller's own; this replaces the removed `GET /api/v1/model-endpoints`)

**Response:** `200 OK` - [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<[ModelEndpoint](#modelendpoint)\> (`PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `TotalPages`, `TotalRecords`, `Objects`). Each object has the shape below (`HasApiKey` instead of the key):

```json
{
  "Id": "mep_abc123",
  "TenantId": "default",
  "UserId": "default",
  "Name": "Primary embeddings",
  "Kind": "Embedding",
  "Provider": "OpenAI",
  "BaseUrl": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
  "Model": "text-embedding-3-small",
  "Dimensionality": 1536,
  "TimeoutMs": 120000,
  "Enabled": true,
  "HasApiKey": true,
  "HealthStatus": "Healthy",
  "LastHealthCheckUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z",
  "LastHealthError": null,
  "LastLatencyMs": 84,
  "CreatedUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z",
  "LastUpdateUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z"
}
```

```bash
curl -X POST -H "Authorization: Bearer default" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"PageNumber": 1, "PageSize": 25}' http://localhost:7890/api/v1/model-endpoints/enumerate
```

---

#### GET /api/v1/model-endpoints (removed)

> **Removed** (see [DEPRECATION.md](../DEPRECATION.md), D-002): answers `410 Gone` with a message naming the
> replacement. Use `POST /api/v1/model-endpoints/enumerate`.

---

#### POST /api/v1/model-endpoints

Create a model endpoint. Supply `ApiKey` to store a provider key; it is write-only and is never returned on subsequent reads.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Request Body:** [ModelEndpoint](#modelendpoint)

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Name` | string | yes | Display name |
| `BaseUrl` | string | conditional | Provider API base URL. Omit for OllamaCloud (fixed) or XAI (hosted default); optional override for VertexAI and Bedrock. |
| `Kind` | string | no | `Embedding` (default) or `Inference` |
| `Provider` | string | no | `Ollama`, `OpenAI`, `OpenAICompatible`, `Anthropic`, `Gemini`, `VoyageAI`, `AzureOpenAI`, `VertexAI`, `Bedrock`, `OllamaCloud`, or `XAI` |
| `Model` | string | no | Model name to target (Azure OpenAI: deployment name; Bedrock: Bedrock model id) |
| `Region` | string | no | Cloud region. Required for `VertexAI` and `Bedrock`. |
| `Project` | string | no | GCP project id. Required for `VertexAI`. |
| `ApiVersion` | string | no | API version for `AzureOpenAI` (defaults to the current GA version when omitted). |
| `AccessKeyId` | string | no | AWS access key id for `Bedrock`. The paired secret access key is supplied write-only via `ApiKey`. |
| `Dimensionality` | int | no | Embedding dimensionality (default 0) |
| `TimeoutMs` | int | no | Request timeout in milliseconds (default 120000, clamped to 1000..600000) |
| `Enabled` | bool | no | Whether the endpoint participates in health sweeps (default true) |
| `ApiKey` | string | no | The secret: provider key, bearer token, Basic password, header or query value, Vertex service-account JSON, or AWS secret access key, depending on `AuthType`. Write-only: accepted here, never returned on reads. |
| `AuthType` | string | no | How requests authenticate: `ProviderDefault` (default; Bearer for Ollama/OpenAI/OpenAI-compatible/Voyage AI, `x-api-key` for Anthropic, `x-goog-api-key` for Gemini, `api-key` for Azure OpenAI, signing for Vertex AI and Bedrock), `None`, `Bearer` (`Authorization: Bearer <ApiKey>`), `Basic` (`AuthUsername` and `ApiKey`), `Header` (`<AuthHeaderName>: <ApiKey>`), or `QueryParameter` (`?<AuthQueryParameter>=<ApiKey>`). `VertexAI` and `Bedrock` accept only `ProviderDefault`. |
| `AuthUsername` | string | no | User name for `Basic`. |
| `AuthHeaderName` | string | no | Header that carries the secret for `Header`, for example `X-API-Key`. |
| `AuthQueryParameter` | string | no | Query parameter that carries the secret for `QueryParameter`, for example `key`. |
| `Headers` | array | no | Extra headers sent with every request, each `{ "Name", "Value" }` (for example Cloudflare Access headers). Values are write-only. On update a supplied list replaces the stored one, an entry without `Value` keeps the stored value of the header with the same name (case-insensitive), and an omitted list keeps the stored headers. |

For `VertexAI` and `Bedrock`, `BaseUrl` is optional (the endpoint is derived from `Region`).

```json
{
  "Name": "Primary embeddings",
  "Kind": "Embedding",
  "Provider": "OpenAI",
  "BaseUrl": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
  "Model": "text-embedding-3-small",
  "Dimensionality": 1536,
  "TimeoutMs": 120000,
  "Enabled": true,
  "ApiKey": "sk-example-key"
}
```

**Response:** `201 Created` - [ModelEndpoint](#modelendpoint) (note `HasApiKey: true`, and no `ApiKey` field)

```json
{
  "Id": "mep_abc123",
  "Name": "Primary embeddings",
  "Kind": "Embedding",
  "Provider": "OpenAI",
  "BaseUrl": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
  "Model": "text-embedding-3-small",
  "Dimensionality": 1536,
  "TimeoutMs": 120000,
  "Enabled": true,
  "HasApiKey": true,
  "HealthStatus": "Unknown",
  "LastHealthCheckUtc": null,
  "LastHealthError": null,
  "LastLatencyMs": null,
  "CreatedUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z",
  "LastUpdateUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z"
}
```

**Errors:**
- `400 Bad Request` - Missing `Name` or `BaseUrl`, or a rejected provider/kind combination (`Anthropic` + `Embedding`, or `VoyageAI` + `Inference`)

---

#### GET /api/v1/model-endpoints/{id}

Get a single model endpoint by ID.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Model endpoint ID (`mep_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [ModelEndpoint](#modelendpoint)
**Error:** `404` - Model endpoint not found

---

#### PUT /api/v1/model-endpoints/{id}

Update a model endpoint. Omit `ApiKey` to keep the stored key; send `ApiKey` to replace it.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Model endpoint ID (`mep_` prefix) |

**Request Body:** [ModelEndpoint](#modelendpoint) (fields to update)

```json
{
  "Name": "Primary embeddings (updated)",
  "Model": "text-embedding-3-large",
  "Dimensionality": 3072,
  "Enabled": true
}
```

**Response:** `200 OK` - [ModelEndpoint](#modelendpoint)
**Error:** `400` - Rejected provider/kind combination
**Error:** `404` - Model endpoint not found

---

#### DELETE /api/v1/model-endpoints/{id}

Delete a model endpoint.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Model endpoint ID (`mep_` prefix) |

**Response:** `204 No Content`
**Error:** `404` - Model endpoint not found

---

#### POST /api/v1/model-endpoints/{id}/validate

Validate one endpoint by issuing a real request against the provider: an embedding request for `Embedding` endpoints, or a completion request for `Inference` endpoints. The resulting health status, timestamp, latency, and any error are persisted on the endpoint.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Model endpoint ID (`mep_` prefix) |

**Response:** `200 OK` - [ModelEndpointProbeResult](#modelendpointproberesult)

```json
{
  "Success": true,
  "BaseUrl": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
  "LatencyMs": 92,
  "StatusCode": 200,
  "Error": null,
  "EmbeddingDimensions": 1536,
  "SampleText": null,
  "TimestampUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z"
}
```

**Error:** `404` - Model endpoint not found

---

#### POST /api/v1/model-endpoints/health-check

Probe all enabled model endpoints, deduplicated by base URL, and persist each endpoint's health status. Returns the number of distinct base URLs that were probed.

**Permission:** Authenticated

**Response:** `200 OK` - [ModelEndpointHealthSweepResponse](#modelendpointhealthsweepresponse)

```json
{
  "DistinctBaseUrlsProbed": 3
}
```
