### Memories

Durable agent memories distilled from voyages (episodic, semantic, procedural). Memories are Category B (shared
configuration) entities: reads return tenant-wide memories plus the caller's own user-specific ones, and edits and
deletes follow the same ownership rules (see [Data Scoping](#data-scoping-who-sees-and-edits-what)).

**Permission:** Authenticated (every route)

| Method | Path | Description |
|--------|------|-------------|
| GET | `/api/v1/memories` | List/search memories visible to the caller. Accepts every [enumeration query](#memory-enumeration) field as a query parameter: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `order`, `sortBy`, `createdAfter`, `createdBefore`, `vesselId`, `voyageId`, `missionId`, `tenantId`, `userId`, `search`, `type` (`Episodic`/`Semantic`/`Procedural`), `topic`, `topicContains`, `vesselName`, `summaryContains`, `scope`, `minSalience`. Without `sortBy` or `order`, ordered by salience then recency. Returns a paged [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`Memory`\>. |
| POST | `/api/v1/memories/enumerate` | Enumerate memories visible to the caller with a `MemoryEnumerateRequest` body (see [Memory enumeration](#memory-enumeration)). Query parameters override body fields, as on the other enumerate routes. Returns a paged [EnumerationResult](#enumerationresultt)\<`Memory`\>. |
| POST | `/api/v1/memories` | Create a memory, or update it in place when a memory with the same `Key` already exists in the caller's tenant (and the caller may edit it). Returns `201`; `400` for an invalid body. |
| GET | `/api/v1/memories/{id}` | Read one memory by id (`mem_` prefix). `404` when it does not exist or is not visible. |
| PUT | `/api/v1/memories/{id}` | Update a memory's fields; increments its `Version`. `403` when the caller cannot edit it, `404` when not found. |
| DELETE | `/api/v1/memories/{id}` | Delete a memory (e.g. a stale one). Returns `204`; `403` when the caller cannot edit it, `404` when not found. |

**Memory fields:** `Id`, `TenantId`, `UserId`, `Scope` (`TenantWide`\|`UserSpecific`), `Type`
(`Episodic`\|`Semantic`\|`Procedural`, default `Semantic`), `Topic`, `Key` (stable idempotency slug), `Summary`
(one-line recall hook), `Content`, `Salience` (0.0-1.0, default 0.5, orders recall), `Version` (starts at 1),
`SourceKind` (`Voyage`\|`Mission`\|`Vessel`\|`Conversation`\|`Manual`\|`Other`, default `Manual`), `SourceVoyageId`,
`SourceMissionId`, `SourceVesselId`, `SourceDetail`, `VesselId`, `Tags` (at most 256 on create and update; more is
`400` `CollectionLimitExceeded`), `CreatedUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc`. Only tenant and global admins can change `Scope` on
update.

#### Memory enumeration

`POST /api/v1/memories/enumerate` takes a `MemoryEnumerateRequest`: every [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery) field
plus the memory filters below. Filtering, sorting, and paging all run in the database (on every provider), and
`TotalRecords` counts only the memories the caller can see. Text filters are case-insensitive substrings in which `%`
and `_` are literal characters.

| Field | Type | Description |
|-------|------|-------------|
| `PageNumber`, `PageSize` | int | 1-based page and page size (clamped to 1-1000), as on every enumerate route |
| `Order` | string | `CreatedAscending` or `CreatedDescending`: the sort direction. Ties break by created time, then ID, in the same direction |
| `SortBy` | string | `Created` (when omitted), `Updated`, `Salience`, `Vessel` (vessel name), `Type`, `Topic`, `Summary` (summary, else content), or `Visibility` |
| `CreatedAfter`, `CreatedBefore` | datetime | Exclusive creation-time bounds |
| `VesselId` | string | Matches the memory's `VesselId` or `SourceVesselId` |
| `VoyageId`, `MissionId` | string | Match `SourceVoyageId` and `SourceMissionId` |
| `TenantId`, `UserId` | string | Narrowing for admins, as on other enumerate routes: a global admin may filter by tenant and user, a tenant admin by owner within their tenant; ignored for regular users |
| `Search` | string | Matches content, topic, or any tag |
| `Type` | string | `Episodic`, `Semantic`, or `Procedural` |
| `Topic` | string | Exact topic |
| `TopicContains` | string | Topic substring |
| `VesselName` | string | Vessel name substring (the memory's vessel, else its source vessel) |
| `SummaryContains` | string | Summary substring (content when the summary is empty) |
| `Scope` | string | `TenantWide` or `UserSpecific` |
| `MinSalience` | number | Minimum salience (inclusive) |

When the request has neither `SortBy` nor `Order` (in the body or the query string), results keep the historical
relevance order: salience descending, then newest first. An explicit `Order` with no `SortBy` sorts by created time,
like the other enumerate routes. Malformed bodies, unknown enum values, and out-of-range page sizes behave exactly as
on `POST /api/v1/missions/enumerate`. Visibility is the same as reads: tenant-wide memories in the caller's tenant plus
the caller's own user-specific ones; tenant admins see their whole tenant and global admins every tenant.

```bash
curl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:7890/api/v1/memories/enumerate \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ARMADA_TOKEN" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "PageNumber": 1,
    "PageSize": 25,
    "SortBy": "Updated",
    "Order": "CreatedDescending",
    "Type": "Procedural",
    "VesselName": "billing",
    "MinSalience": 0.5
  }'
```

```json
{
  "Success": true,
  "PageNumber": 1,
  "PageSize": 25,
  "TotalPages": 1,
  "TotalRecords": 1,
  "Objects": [
    {
      "Id": "mem_mvrhwjnm_NFnebbXk0WL",
      "Scope": "TenantWide",
      "Type": "Procedural",
      "Topic": "deployment",
      "Summary": "Billing deploy checklist",
      "Content": "Run the migration dry run before deploying billing-api.",
      "Salience": 0.8,
      "VesselId": "vsl_mvrhwjnm_yprmNsLjmco",
      "Tags": ["deploy"],
      "CreatedUtc": "2026-10-27T18:02:11.0000000Z",
      "LastUpdateUtc": "2026-10-27T18:40:03.0000000Z"
    }
  ],
  "TotalMs": 1.42
}
```

The same query as a GET: `GET /api/v1/memories?sortBy=Updated&order=CreatedDescending&type=Procedural&vesselName=billing&minSalience=0.5`.

**Create/upsert example:**

```bash
curl -X POST http://127.0.0.1:7890/api/v1/memories \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $ARMADA_TOKEN" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "Type": "Semantic",
    "Topic": "code-style",
    "Key": "code-style/no-var",
    "Summary": "No var in C#",
    "Content": "This user dislikes the use of var in C# code.",
    "Salience": 0.9,
    "Tags": ["csharp", "style"],
    "SourceKind": "Voyage"
  }'
```

---

### Backup and Restore

#### GET /api/v1/backup

Create and download a ZIP backup of the Armada database and settings.

**Permission:** AdminOnly

**Response:** `200 OK` - Binary ZIP file stream with `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="armada-backup-{timestamp}.zip"` header.

**ZIP Contents:**

| File | Description |
|---|---|
| `armada.db` | SQLite database snapshot created via the SQLite online backup API |
| `settings.json` | Current Armada server configuration, including `GitHubToken` when configured |
| `manifest.json` | Backup metadata: timestamp, schema version, Armada version, record counts per table |

**Example:**

```bash
curl -H "X-Api-Key: your-key" http://localhost:7890/api/v1/backup -o backup.zip
```

---

#### POST /api/v1/restore

Restore Armada from a previously created backup ZIP file.

**Permission:** AdminOnly

**Request:** Binary ZIP file in the request body (`Content-Type: application/zip`).

**Headers:**

| Header | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Content-Type` | Yes | `application/zip` |
| `X-Original-Filename` | No | Original filename of the uploaded backup (used in the response message). If omitted, the server's temp filename is used. |

**Validation:**
- ZIP must contain `armada.db` with a valid `schema_migrations` table
- A safety backup is automatically created before overwriting the current database
- A body that is not a ZIP, a ZIP without `armada.db`, or an `armada.db` that is not an Armada SQLite database is rejected with `400 BadRequest` (`Error: "BadRequest"`) and nothing is changed

**Response:** `200 OK`

```json
{
  "Status": "restored",
  "SafetyBackupPath": "~/.armada/backups/armada-safety-backup-20260311T120000Z.zip",
  "SchemaVersion": 9,
  "Message": "Database restored from backup.zip. Restart the server to reload the restored data."
}
```

**Example:**

```bash
curl -X POST -H "X-Api-Key: your-key" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/zip" \
  -H "X-Original-Filename: backup.zip" \
  --data-binary @backup.zip \
  http://localhost:7890/api/v1/restore
```

> **Note:** Restart the server after restoring to ensure all in-memory state is refreshed.

---

### Workspace

Workspace is a first-class REST surface for browsing and editing a vessel working tree. All paths are repository-relative, normalized to forward slashes, and constrained to the vessel's checkout. Armada blocks traversal outside that root and reserves `.git` internals.

The checkout is the vessel `workingDirectory` when it exists on the Admiral host; otherwise it is the checkout of a connected Harbor that can serve the vessel (split mode), and every route below runs there with the same rules, the Harbor confining each request to that checkout (see [HARBOR.md](HARBOR.md#checkouts-outside-missions)). When neither exists, the routes return `409 Conflict` with `Data` a `VesselCheckoutErrorDetail` (`Code` `VesselCheckoutUnavailable`, `Reason` `NoHarborConnected` or `NoHarborCheckout`, `VesselId`, `VesselName`, `HarborReasons`) and a `Message` that says what to set; `GET .../status` instead returns `200` with `HasWorkingDirectory: false` and the reason in `Error`. `WorkspaceStatusResult` and `WorkspaceExecResult` carry `Host` (`Admiral` or `Harbor Name (hbr_...)`), and the status also `HarborId`.

#### GET /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/tree

List one page of one directory in the vessel workspace: folders first, then files, each by name. The page is cut on the
host that has the checkout (the Admiral, or a Harbor in split mode, whose file listing request carries the page), so
the reply over the Harbor link is bounded too.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: optional `path`, `pageNumber` (default 1), `pageSize` (1 to 1000, default 10)
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceTreeResult`: `RootPath`, `CurrentPath`, `ParentPath`, `PageNumber`, `PageSize`,
  `TotalPages`, `TotalRecords` (every direct child), and `Entries` (one page)

#### GET /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/diff

Return a unified git diff of the vessel working tree against HEAD, optionally scoped to one path.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: optional `path` to scope the diff
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceDiffResult`

#### GET /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/file

Read one file in the vessel workspace.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: required `path`
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceFileResponse`
- Errors: `400` when `path` is missing, `404` when the file is not found

#### PUT /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/file

Save one text file with optimistic concurrency validation.

**Permission:** Authenticated

```json
{
  "Path": "src/Armada.Server/Routes/RequestHistoryRoutes.cs",
  "Content": "// updated file content",
  "ExpectedHash": "sha256:previous-hash"
}
```

- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceSaveResult`
- Errors: `409 Conflict` when the on-disk hash no longer matches `ExpectedHash`

#### POST /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/exec

Execute a shell command in the vessel working tree (the in-browser dock terminal), bounded by a timeout. Tenant administrators only.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Request Body:** `WorkspaceExecRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Command` | string | yes | The command line to execute in the workspace root via the platform shell |
| `TimeoutSeconds` | int | no | Timeout before the command (and its process tree) is killed. Clamped to `[1, 600]` (default: `60`) |

- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceExecResult`
- Errors: `403 Forbidden` when the caller is not a tenant administrator; `404 Not Found` when the vessel is not found; `409 Conflict` (`VesselCheckoutErrorDetail`) when the vessel has no checkout on the Admiral host or a connected Harbor
- The `audit.command` event's `host` names the Harbor when the command ran on one.

#### POST /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/open-terminal

Open a terminal window at the vessel's checkout on the computer that holds it (the Workspace's Open in Terminal). No request body.

**Permission:** Authenticated, then checked by where the checkout is:

- **On a Harbor:** the Harbor's owner, a global admin, or a tenant admin of the Harbor's tenant (`403` otherwise). The Admiral sends the Harbor an `OpenTerminal` file operation (see [HARBOR_PROTOCOL.md](HARBOR_PROTOCOL.md)) only when the Harbor advertised the `open-terminal` capability; the Harbor opens the terminal on its desktop and only in the checkout it maps the vessel to.
- **On the Admiral host:** a global or tenant admin (`403` otherwise), and only when the request comes from the Admiral's own computer (a loopback address and no `X-Forwarded-For` or `Forwarded` header) and the Admiral can reach a desktop (not in a container, not a Windows service, a Linux display).

- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceOpenTerminalResult`, also when no terminal opened for an expected reason (`Opened: false` with `Path` and `Message`), so a client can offer the path instead
- Errors: `403 Forbidden` when the caller may not open a terminal on that host; `404 Not Found` when the vessel is not found

```json
{
  "Opened": false,
  "Path": "/Users/alex/Code/Timestamps",
  "Location": "Harbor",
  "HostName": "Alex-MacBook-Pro",
  "Message": "Update the Harbor on Alex-MacBook-Pro to open a terminal from the dashboard."
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Opened` | bool | True when a terminal window opened |
| `Path` | string? | The checkout's path on the host that holds it; null when the vessel has none |
| `Location` | [WorkspaceTerminalLocationEnum](#workspaceterminallocationenum) | `Harbor`, `AdmiralHost`, or `None` (no checkout) |
| `HostName` | string? | The Harbor's display name, or the Admiral computer's name |
| `Message` | string? | Why no terminal opened, as one sentence (for example the Harbor is offline or older, the browser is not on the Admiral's computer, or the Admiral runs in a container); null when one opened |

#### POST /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/directory

Create a directory inside the vessel workspace.

**Permission:** Authenticated

```json
{
  "Path": "docs/new-folder"
}
```

- Response: `201 Created` - `WorkspaceOperationResult`

#### POST /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/rename

Rename or move one file or directory.

**Permission:** Authenticated

```json
{
  "Path": "docs/old-name.md",
  "NewPath": "docs/new-name.md"
}
```

- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceOperationResult`

#### DELETE /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/entry

Delete one file or directory.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: required `path`
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceOperationResult`

#### GET /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/search

Search text files in the vessel workspace.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: required `q`, optional `maxResults`
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceSearchResult`

#### GET /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/changes

Return branch state and one page of changed files in `git status` order. git status has no paging, so the whole status
is read and the page is cut from it; `IsDirty`, `CommitsAhead`, and `CommitsBehind` cover the whole tree.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber` (default 1), `pageSize` (1 to 1000, default 10)
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceChangesResult`: `BranchName`, `IsDirty`, `CommitsAhead`, `CommitsBehind`, `Error`,
  `PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `TotalPages`, `TotalRecords` (every changed file), and `Changes` (one page)

#### GET /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/status

Return high-level workspace health, git state, and the number of active missions (`ActiveMissionCount`). The active
missions are not embedded; page them with `GET /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/active-missions`.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkspaceStatusResult`

#### GET /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/active-missions

One page of the vessel's active missions (`Pending`, `Assigned`, `InProgress`, `Review`, `Testing`), newest first with an
id tie-break, each with the repository-relative files its description scopes it to ("Touch only ...", "Edit only ...",
"Modify only ...": `ScopedFiles` lists at most 256, `ScopedFileCount` is the full count). Read from the database one
bounded LIMIT/OFFSET page per status (tenant-scoped for non-admins), merged in order, with an exact total.
`POST /api/v1/workspace/vessels/{vesselId}/active-missions/enumerate` takes the `EnumerationQuery` in the body
(querystring values override it; a malformed body is `400`).

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber` (default 1), `pageSize` (1 to 1000, default 10)
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<WorkspaceActiveMission>` (`MissionId`, `Title`, `Status`, `ScopedFiles`,
  `ScopedFileCount`)
- Error: `404` vessel not visible

---

### Planning Sessions

Planning sessions back the dashboard's captain chat flow and transcript-to-dispatch handoff. These routes are implemented for SQLite first; other database backends return `501 Not Supported`.

#### POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/enumerate

One page of the planning sessions visible to the caller, most recently updated first. Paging and filters come from the JSON body (`EnumerationQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 1000, default 10) or the matching querystring parameters.

**Permission:** Authenticated (caller scope; this replaces the removed `GET /api/v1/planning-sessions`)

**Response:** `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<T>` (`PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `TotalPages`, `TotalRecords`, `Objects`)

---

#### GET /api/v1/planning-sessions (removed)

> **Removed** (see [DEPRECATION.md](../DEPRECATION.md), D-003): answers `410 Gone` with a message naming the
> replacement. Use `POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/enumerate`.

#### POST /api/v1/planning-sessions

Create a planning session, reserve the selected captain, and provision a planning dock.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "Title": "Refactor request history filters",
  "CaptainId": "cpt_abc123",
  "VesselId": "vsl_def456",
  "FleetId": "flt_xyz789",
  "PipelineId": "pln_fullpipeline",
  "SelectedPlaybooks": []
}
```

`SelectedPlaybooks` holds at most 256 entries (`400` `CollectionLimitExceeded` otherwise).

- Response: `201 Created`
- Response shape (`PlanningSessionDetail`):

```json
{
  "Session": { "...": "PlanningSession" },
  "MessageCount": 0,
  "Captain": { "...": "Captain" },
  "Vessel": { "...": "Vessel" },
  "LinkedObjectiveCount": 0
}
```

#### GET /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}

Read one planning session with its captain and vessel, the transcript's `MessageCount`, and `LinkedObjectiveCount`. The
detail no longer embeds `Messages`; read the transcript with `POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}/messages/enumerate`.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `PlanningSessionDetail`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}/messages/enumerate

One page of the session's transcript, paged in the database: newest first (`CreatedDescending`, the default) or oldest
first (`CreatedAscending`), by sequence with the id as the tie-break. `TotalRecords` is the transcript length. Paging
comes from the JSON body (`EnumerationQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 1000) or the matching querystring parameters.

**Permission:** Authenticated (caller scope)

- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<PlanningSessionMessage>`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}/messages

Append one user message and launch the next planning turn.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "Content": "Summarize the changes and propose a safe rollout."
}
```

- Response: `200 OK` - same detail shape as `GET /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}`

#### POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}/summarize

Generate a dispatch-ready draft from a selected or inferred assistant message without launching the voyage.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "MessageId": "psm_abc123",
  "Title": "Refresh request history docs"
}
```

- Response: `200 OK` - `PlanningSessionSummaryResponse`

#### POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}/dispatch

Create a voyage directly from planning output.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "MessageId": "psm_abc123",
  "Title": "Refresh request history docs",
  "Description": "Update docs and validation assets for the shipped request-history feature."
}
```

- Response: `200 OK` - `Voyage`
- The voyage is linked to every objective linked to the session. When that would push an objective's id lists over 1000
  the call fails with `400` `CollectionLimitExceeded`, naming the voyage that was dispatched.

#### POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}/stop

Stop an active planning session and release its resources.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `200 OK` - same detail shape as `GET /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}`

#### POST /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}/stop-turn

Abort the in-flight planning turn (cancelling the captain runtime) while keeping the session active so the user can keep chatting.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

**Path Parameters:**
| Parameter | Description |
|---|---|
| `id` | Planning session ID (`psn_` prefix) |

- Response: `200 OK` - same detail shape as `GET /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`; `501 Not Supported` on database backends other than SQLite

#### DELETE /api/v1/planning-sessions/{id}

Delete a planning session and its transcript. Active sessions are stopped first.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`

---

### Objectives

Backlog is the primary user-facing label for future work in Armada. The persisted domain record remains `Objective`, so the legacy `/api/v1/objectives/...` routes remain supported while `/api/v1/backlog/...` exposes the same record shape using backlog-first terminology.

Important shared `Objective` fields now include:

- `Kind`, `Category`, `Priority`, `Rank`, `BacklogState`, `Effort`
- `TargetVersion`, `DueUtc`
- `ParentObjectiveId`, `BlockedByObjectiveIds`
- `RefinementSummary`, `SuggestedPipelineId`, `RefinementSessionIds`
- all existing linkage fields for fleets, vessels, planning sessions, voyages, missions, checks, releases, deployments, and incidents

#### GET /api/v1/objectives and GET /api/v1/backlog

List objective or backlog records in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `owner`, `category`, `parentObjectiveId`, `vesselId`, `fleetId`, `planningSessionId`, `voyageId`, `missionId`, `checkRunId`, `releaseId`, `deploymentId`, `incidentId`, `tag`, `status`, `backlogState`, `kind`, `priority`, `effort`, `targetVersion`, `search`, `fromUtc`, `toUtc`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Objective>`

#### POST /api/v1/objectives/enumerate and POST /api/v1/backlog/enumerate

Enumerate objectives or backlog items using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `ObjectiveQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Objective>`

#### POST /api/v1/objectives and POST /api/v1/backlog

Create one scoped objective or backlog item.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "Title": "Stabilize May rollout",
  "Description": "Track the full rollout objective across the API and dashboard vessels.",
  "Status": "Scoped",
  "Kind": "Feature",
  "Category": "Delivery",
  "Priority": "P1",
  "Rank": 10,
  "BacklogState": "ReadyForPlanning",
  "Effort": "M",
  "Owner": "Delivery",
  "TargetVersion": "1.2.0",
  "AcceptanceCriteria": ["Deployment verified in staging", "Incident rollback documented"],
  "NonGoals": ["Do not change release cadence"],
  "RolloutConstraints": ["Keep existing rollback path intact"],
  "VesselIds": ["vsl_abc123"],
  "ReleaseIds": ["rel_def456"]
}
```

- Request body: `ObjectiveUpsertRequest`
- Response: `201 Created` - `Objective`
- Notes: `POST /api/v1/backlog` is the preferred user-facing alias; both routes persist the same underlying `Objective`
- Limits: `Tags`, `AcceptanceCriteria`, `NonGoals`, `RolloutConstraints`, `EvidenceLinks`, and `SuggestedPlaybooks` hold
  at most 256 entries each, and every id list (`FleetIds`, `VesselIds`, `PlanningSessionIds`, `RefinementSessionIds`,
  `VoyageIds`, `MissionIds`, `CheckRunIds`, `ReleaseIds`, `DeploymentIds`, `IncidentIds`, `BlockedByObjectiveIds`) at
  most 1000. A larger list is `400` with `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded` naming the field; nothing is stored. The
  same limits apply to updates and to refinement summaries applied to the item.

#### POST /api/v1/objectives/reorder and POST /api/v1/backlog/reorder

Apply one or more explicit backlog rank updates.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "Items": [
    { "ObjectiveId": "obj_abc123", "Rank": 10 },
    { "ObjectiveId": "obj_def456", "Rank": 20 }
  ]
}
```

- Request body: `ObjectiveReorderRequest` (at most 1000 `Items`; more is `400` `CollectionLimitExceeded`)
- Response: `200 OK` - `ObjectiveReorderResult`: `{ Updated, Items: [{ ObjectiveId, Rank }] }`, the ranks that were
  applied (it no longer returns the reordered objectives; read them with `POST /api/v1/backlog/enumerate`)

#### POST /api/v1/objectives/import/github

Import or refresh one objective from GitHub issue or pull-request metadata using the selected vessel's GitHub repository mapping.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

Credential resolution order:

1. `Vessel.GitHubTokenOverride`
2. global `GitHubToken` from Armada configuration

```json
{
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "SourceType": "Issue",
  "Number": 123
}
```

- Request body: `GitHubObjectiveImportRequest`
- Response: `201 Created` when a new objective is created, `200 OK` when an existing objective is refreshed
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`
- Notes:
  - Set `SourceType` to `Issue` or `PullRequest`
  - Include `ObjectiveId` to refresh an already-linked objective in place
  - Reads never return the resolved GitHub token value

#### GET /api/v1/objectives/{id} and GET /api/v1/backlog/{id}

Read one objective or backlog item.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `Objective`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/objectives/{id} and PUT /api/v1/backlog/{id}

Update one objective or backlog item and its linked lifecycle scope.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `ObjectiveUpsertRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `Objective`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/objectives/{id} and DELETE /api/v1/backlog/{id}

Delete one objective or backlog item.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/objectives/{id}/refinement-sessions/enumerate and POST /api/v1/backlog/{id}/refinement-sessions/enumerate

One page of the refinement sessions linked to one objective or backlog item. Paging comes from the JSON body
(`EnumerationQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 1000, default 10) or the matching querystring parameters.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<ObjectiveRefinementSession>`
- Notes: backlog refinement reads are authenticated and scoped like other backlog reads. These replace the removed
  `GET /api/v1/objectives/{id}/refinement-sessions` and `GET /api/v1/backlog/{id}/refinement-sessions` (D-004, D-005),
  which answer `410 Gone` naming the replacement.

#### POST /api/v1/objectives/{id}/refinement-sessions and POST /api/v1/backlog/{id}/refinement-sessions

Create one captain-backed refinement session for an objective or backlog item.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "CaptainId": "cpt_abc123",
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "Title": "Refine rollout stabilization plan"
}
```

- Request body: `ObjectiveRefinementSessionCreateRequest`
- Response: `201 Created` - `ObjectiveRefinementSessionDetail`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`, `409 Conflict`, `501 Not Implemented`
- Notes:
  - `CaptainId` is required
  - `VesselId` is optional for refinement and falls back to the first linked vessel when available
  - refinement is lighter than planning and does not provision a dock/worktree by default

#### GET /api/v1/objective-refinement-sessions/{id}

Read one refinement session with its captain, vessel, and linked objective/backlog item, and the transcript's
`MessageCount`. The detail no longer embeds `Messages`; read the transcript with
`POST /api/v1/objective-refinement-sessions/{id}/messages/enumerate`.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `ObjectiveRefinementSessionDetail` (`Session`, `MessageCount`, `Captain`, `Vessel`, `Objective`)
- Errors: `404 Not Found`, `501 Not Implemented`

#### POST /api/v1/objective-refinement-sessions/{id}/messages/enumerate

One page of the session's transcript, paged in the database: newest first (`CreatedDescending`, the default) or oldest
first (`CreatedAscending`), by sequence with the id as the tie-break. Paging comes from the JSON body
(`EnumerationQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 1000) or the matching querystring parameters.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<ObjectiveRefinementMessage>`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`, `501 Not Implemented`

#### POST /api/v1/objective-refinement-sessions/{id}/messages

Append one user message to the refinement transcript and launch the next captain turn.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "Content": "Focus on rollback safety and the release verification sequence."
}
```

- Request body: `ObjectiveRefinementMessageRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `ObjectiveRefinementSessionDetail`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`, `409 Conflict`, `501 Not Implemented`

#### POST /api/v1/objective-refinement-sessions/{id}/summarize

Generate a structured refinement summary from a selected or inferred assistant message.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "MessageId": "orm_abc123"
}
```

- Request body: `ObjectiveRefinementSummaryRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `ObjectiveRefinementSummaryResponse`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`, `409 Conflict`, `501 Not Implemented`

#### POST /api/v1/objective-refinement-sessions/{id}/apply

Summarize and apply a refinement result back to the linked backlog item.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "MessageId": "orm_abc123",
  "MarkMessageSelected": true,
  "PromoteBacklogState": true,
  "EndSession": true
}
```

Applying ends the refinement session by default (`EndSession` true): the session is stopped and its captain returns to
Idle, so it does not keep holding a captain that missions are waiting for. Pass `EndSession: false` to keep refining in
the same session.

- Request body: `ObjectiveRefinementApplyRequest` (optional): `MessageId` (assistant message to apply; default: the
  selected assistant message, else the latest one), `MarkMessageSelected` (default `true`), `PromoteBacklogState` (default `true`), `EndSession` (default `true`)
- Response: `200 OK` - `ObjectiveRefinementApplyResponse`: `Summary` (`ObjectiveRefinementSummaryResponse`),
  `Objective` (the updated backlog item), and `Session` (the session as stored after the apply; its `Status` is
  `Stopped` when the apply ended it)
- Errors: `404 Not Found`, `409 Conflict`, `501 Not Implemented`

#### POST /api/v1/objective-refinement-sessions/{id}/stop

Stop an active refinement session and release the selected captain.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `200 OK` - `ObjectiveRefinementSessionDetail`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`, `501 Not Implemented`

#### DELETE /api/v1/objective-refinement-sessions/{id}

Delete a refinement session and its transcript. Active sessions are stopped first.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`, `409 Conflict`, `501 Not Implemented`

---

### Workflow Profiles

Workflow profiles define how a vessel or fleet builds, tests, versions, deploys, rolls back, and verifies itself. These routes back `Configuration > Workflow Profiles` and the preflight/resolution logic used by structured checks.

**Default profiles.** Every vessel gets a workflow profile out of the box. When a vessel is created, and on every server start, Armada gives each vessel that no active profile applies to (none scoped to the vessel, its fleet, or every vessel) a `Vessel`-scoped, `IsDefault` profile named `<vessel>: <toolchain>`. The toolchain is recognized from the repository's files: the vessel's working directory, else its bare clone, else a shallow clone of `RepoUrl` that fetches the file list only. Templates (`SourceTemplate`):

| `SourceTemplate` | Recognized by (checked in this order) | Lint / build / unit test / package |
|---|---|---|
| `dotnet` | any `.sln`, `.slnx`, `.csproj`, or `.fsproj` (the shallowest is the target) | `dotnet format "<target>" --verify-no-changes` / `dotnet build "<target>" -c Release` / `dotnet test "<target>" -c Release` / `dotnet pack "<target>" -c Release -o artifacts` |
| `node` | `package.json` at the root | `npm run lint --if-present` / `npm ci && npm run build --if-present` / `npm test --if-present` / `npm pack` |
| `go` | `go.mod` at the root | `go vet ./...` / `go build ./...` / `go test ./...` / none |
| `rust` | `Cargo.toml` at the root | `cargo clippy` / `cargo build --release` / `cargo test` / `cargo package` |
| `maven` | `pom.xml` at the root | none / `mvn -B package -DskipTests` / `mvn -B test` / `mvn -B package` |
| `gradle` | `build.gradle` or `build.gradle.kts` at the root | none / `./gradlew build -x test` (`gradle` without a wrapper) / `./gradlew test` / `./gradlew assemble` |
| `python` | `pyproject.toml`, `setup.py`, or `requirements.txt` at the root | `python -m ruff check .` / `python -m pip install -r requirements.txt` (else `-e .`) / `python -m pytest` / `python -m build` (with `pyproject.toml`) |
| `generic` | anything else, or files that could not be read | `git diff --check` (conflict markers and whitespace errors in uncommitted changes) / none / none / none; named `<vessel>: basic checks`, and its description says to add the vessel's commands |

A `generic` profile is replaced with the matching template once the toolchain can be recognized. A profile a person created is never touched, and editing a default profile (`PUT`) clears its `SourceTemplate`, so Armada leaves it alone from then on.

**Paging and visibility:** list and enumerate routes page in the database with the caller's visibility applied before counting (a regular user sees the tenant-wide rows plus their own, a tenant admin the whole tenant), so every page is full and `TotalRecords` is exact for the caller. `pageSize` defaults to 10 and is normalized into 1..1000 (querystring and body alike); ties are broken by id, so pages never overlap. `LanguageHints`, `RequiredInputs`, `ExpectedArtifacts` and `Environments` hold at most 256 entries each
(`CollectionLimits.MaxConfigItems`); create, update and validate (REST and MCP) reject more with `400 Bad Request` /
MCP `InvalidArgument`, `Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`. `CommandPreviews` therefore holds at most 15 + 6 x 256 = 1551
entries, and `Errors`, `Warnings` and `AvailableCheckTypes` are fixed by the validation rules and check types.

#### GET /api/v1/workflow-profiles

List workflow profiles in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `scope`, `fleetId`, `vesselId`, `search`, `active`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<WorkflowProfile>`

#### POST /api/v1/workflow-profiles/enumerate

Enumerate workflow profiles using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `WorkflowProfileQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<WorkflowProfile>`

#### POST /api/v1/workflow-profiles/validate

Validate a workflow profile without saving it.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `WorkflowProfile`
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkflowProfileValidationResult`
- Notes: validation returns errors, warnings, resolved command previews, available check types, and required input issues

#### GET /api/v1/workflow-profiles/preview/vessels/{vesselId}

Preview the resolved workflow commands for one vessel.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: optional `workflowProfileId`
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkflowProfileResolutionPreviewResult`
- Errors: `404 Not Found` when the vessel does not exist or no workflow profile can be resolved

#### GET /api/v1/workflow-profiles/resolve/vessels/{vesselId}

Resolve the active workflow profile for one vessel.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: optional `workflowProfileId`
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkflowProfile`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/workflow-profiles

Create a workflow profile.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `WorkflowProfile`
- Response: `201 Created` - `WorkflowProfile`
- Notes: reads are available to any authenticated caller in scope; create/update/delete require tenant admin or global admin

#### GET /api/v1/workflow-profiles/{id}

Read one workflow profile by ID.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkflowProfile`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/workflow-profiles/{id}

Update one workflow profile.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `WorkflowProfile`
- Response: `200 OK` - `WorkflowProfile`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/workflow-profiles/{id}

Delete one workflow profile.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `403 Forbidden` (a profile the caller may not edit), `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/workflow-profiles/delete/multiple

Permanently delete several workflow profiles by ID. Ids that are empty, missing, in another tenant, or not editable by
the caller are skipped with a reason (`Empty ID`, `Not found`, `Not permitted`) instead of failing the batch.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `DeleteMultipleRequest` - `{ "Ids": ["wfp_abc123", "wfp_def456"] }`
- Response: `200 OK` - `DeleteMultipleResult` (`Status`, `Deleted`, `Skipped[{Id, Reason}]`)
- Errors: `400 Bad Request` when `Ids` is missing or empty, or holds more than 1000 ids (`Data.Code`
  `CollectionLimitExceeded`; nothing is deleted). `Skipped` therefore holds at most 1000 entries.

The same create, update, delete, validate, resolve, and preview rules are available over MCP (`create_workflow_profile`,
`update_workflow_profile`, `delete_workflow_profile`, `delete_workflow_profiles`, `validate_workflow_profile`,
`resolve_workflow_profile`, `preview_workflow_profile`, `get_workflow_profile`); REST and MCP share
`WorkflowProfileAccessService`. On create, update, and validate, a `FleetId` or `VesselId` outside the caller's tenant is
refused with `400 Bad Request` ("Fleet not found" / "Vessel not found") for callers other than a global admin.

### Check Runs

Structured check runs are the delivery-memory record for build, test, packaging, publish, deploy, rollback, smoke-test, health-check, release-versioning, and other workflow-profile-backed commands.

#### GET /api/v1/check-runs

List structured check runs in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `workflowProfileId`, `vesselId`, `missionId`, `voyageId`, `type`, `status`, `source`, `providerName`, `environmentName`, `externalId`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<CheckRun>`

#### POST /api/v1/check-runs/enumerate

Enumerate structured check runs using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `CheckRunQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<CheckRun>`

#### POST /api/v1/check-runs

Execute one structured check run.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "WorkflowProfileId": "wfp_def456",
  "Type": "UnitTest",
  "Label": "Unit tests",
  "EnvironmentName": null
}
```

- Response: `201 Created` - `CheckRun`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request` when readiness, workflow resolution, or command validation fails; `409 Conflict` with `Data` a `VesselCheckoutErrorDetail` (`Code` `VesselCheckoutUnavailable`, `Reason` `NoHarborConnected` or `NoHarborCheckout`) when the vessel has no working directory on the Admiral host and no connected Harbor has a checkout of it; the `Message` says what to set
- The check runs in the vessel's working directory on the Admiral host, or, when that does not exist, in the checkout of a connected Harbor that can serve the vessel (through the Harbor's login shell; `WorkingDirectory` is then the path on the Harbor, and expected artifacts are read from the Harbor). See [HARBOR.md](HARBOR.md#checkouts-outside-missions).

#### POST /api/v1/check-runs/import

Import an externally executed or provider-hosted check run into Armada history.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "Type": "Build",
  "Status": "Passed",
  "ProviderName": "GitHub Actions",
  "Label": "CI build",
  "StartedUtc": "2026-05-04T16:00:00Z",
  "CompletedUtc": "2026-05-04T16:02:15Z"
}
```

- Request body: `CheckRunImportRequest`: `VesselId` (required), `WorkflowProfileId`, `MissionId`, `VoyageId`, `DeploymentId`, `Type`, `Status`, `ProviderName`, `ExternalId`, `ExternalUrl`, `EnvironmentName`, `Label`, `BranchName`, `CommitHash`, `Command`, `Summary`, `Output`, `ExitCode`, `TestSummary`, `CoverageSummary`, `Artifacts` (at most 256), `DurationMs`, `StartedUtc`, `CompletedUtc`
- Response: `201 Created` - `CheckRun`
- Errors: `400` with `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded` when `Artifacts` holds more than 256 entries. A run started by Armada collects one artifact per workflow profile `ExpectedArtifacts` entry; a profile with more than 256 is rejected before the check runs.

#### POST /api/v1/check-runs/sync/github-actions

Pull recent GitHub Actions workflow runs for one vessel into Armada check history. This is an on-demand pull surface; it does not require a webhook listener.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

Credential resolution order:

1. `Vessel.GitHubTokenOverride`
2. global `GitHubToken` from Armada configuration

```json
{
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "WorkflowProfileId": "wfp_def456",
  "DeploymentId": "dep_ghi789",
  "EnvironmentName": "staging",
  "BranchName": "main",
  "RunCount": 10,
  "PageNumber": 1
}
```

- Request body: `GitHubActionsSyncRequest`. `RunCount` is the GitHub page size (normalized to 1-100) and `PageNumber` the GitHub page (newest first, default 1); one call imports one page.
- Response: `200 OK` - `GitHubActionsSyncResult`: `ProviderName`, `VesselId`, `DeploymentId`, `CreatedCount`, `UpdatedCount`, `PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `SourceTotalCount` (GitHub's `total_count` for the branch, status and commit filters), `SourceTotalPages`, `FetchedCount` (runs on the page), `FilteredOutCount` (runs on the page that did not match `WorkflowName` or `CommitHash`), and `CheckRunIds` (the imported runs, at most 100; read them with `GET /api/v1/check-runs/{id}`). The result no longer embeds the check runs.
- Notes:
  - Imported runs are normalized into `CheckRun` records with `Source = External` and `ProviderName = GitHubActions`
  - When `DeploymentId` is supplied, synced runs are linked back to that deployment
  - Sync is idempotent by provider external run ID

#### GET /api/v1/check-runs/{id}

Read one structured check run.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `CheckRun`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/check-runs/{id}/retry

Retry a prior check run using the same resolved scope and command context.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `201 Created` - `CheckRun`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/check-runs/{id}

Delete one structured check run.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

### Environments

Environment records define named rollout targets such as `Development`, `Staging`, or `Production`, including verification definitions, approval requirements, and rollout monitoring settings.

#### GET /api/v1/environments

List environments in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `vesselId`, `kind`, `isDefault`, `active`, `search`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<DeploymentEnvironment>`

#### POST /api/v1/environments/enumerate

Enumerate environments using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `DeploymentEnvironmentQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<DeploymentEnvironment>`

#### POST /api/v1/environments

Create one environment. `VerificationDefinitions` holds at most 256 definitions and each definition's `Headers` at most 256 entries; create and update (`PUT /api/v1/environments/{id}`) reject more with `400` (`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`, `Data.Field` such as `VerificationDefinitions.Headers`).

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "Name": "Staging",
  "Kind": "Staging",
  "BaseUrl": "https://staging.example.com",
  "HealthEndpoint": "/api/v1/status/health",
  "RequiresApproval": true,
  "IsDefault": false
}
```

- Response: `201 Created` - `DeploymentEnvironment`

#### GET /api/v1/environments/{id}

Read one environment.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `DeploymentEnvironment`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/environments/{id}

Update one environment.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `DeploymentEnvironmentUpsertRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `DeploymentEnvironment`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/environments/{id}

Delete one environment.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

---

### Deployments

Deployment records track rollout approval, execution, verification, rollback, linked checks, and request-history evidence for a named environment.

#### GET /api/v1/deployments

List deployments in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `vesselId`, `workflowProfileId`, `environmentId`, `environmentName`, `releaseId`, `missionId`, `voyageId`, `checkRunId`, `status`, `verificationStatus`, `search`, `fromUtc`, `toUtc`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Deployment>`

#### POST /api/v1/deployments/enumerate

Enumerate deployments using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `DeploymentQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Deployment>`

#### POST /api/v1/deployments

Create one deployment. When approval is not required and `AutoExecute` is `true`, the deployment begins immediately.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "EnvironmentId": "env_def456",
  "ReleaseId": "rel_ghi789",
  "Title": "Deploy May release to staging",
  "SourceRef": "refs/tags/v1.4.2",
  "AutoExecute": true
}
```

- Response: `201 Created` - `Deployment`

#### GET /api/v1/deployments/{id}

Read one deployment.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `Deployment`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/deployments/{id}

Update one deployment and its mutable metadata.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `DeploymentUpsertRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `Deployment`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/deployments/{id}/approve

Approve one pending deployment and begin execution.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: optional `{ "Comment": "Ship it" }`
- Response: `200 OK` - `Deployment`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/deployments/{id}/deny

Deny one pending deployment without executing it.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: optional `{ "Comment": "Need one more verification run" }`
- Response: `200 OK` - `Deployment`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/deployments/{id}/verify

Re-run the configured post-deploy verification for an existing deployment. Every verification run is linked in `CheckRunIds`, which holds at most 1000 ids: a verify, rollback, or link that would push it past the limit is rejected with `400` (`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`, `Data.Field` `CheckRunIds`) before any check runs, and rollout monitoring stops for that deployment. Every check run also carries its `DeploymentId`, so `POST /api/v1/check-runs/enumerate` with `deploymentId` reads them all.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `200 OK` - `Deployment`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/deployments/{id}/rollback

Run the configured rollback flow for an existing deployment.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `200 OK` - `Deployment`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/deployments/{id}

Delete one deployment record.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

---

### Releases

Release records group versions, notes, artifacts, and linked work so a user can inspect what is shipping and what evidence exists that it is ready.

#### GET /api/v1/releases

List release records in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `vesselId`, `workflowProfileId`, `voyageId`, `missionId`, `checkRunId`, `status`, `search`, `fromUtc`, `toUtc`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Release>`

#### POST /api/v1/releases/enumerate

Enumerate releases using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `ReleaseQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Release>`

#### POST /api/v1/releases

Create a release from linked voyages, missions, and check runs. `VoyageIds`, `MissionIds`, `CheckRunIds`, and `ObjectiveIds` hold at most 1000 ids each. Linked voyages expand to every one of their missions, and a release may link at most 1000 missions and 1000 derived artifacts in all; a create, update, or refresh past any of these limits is rejected with `400` (`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`, `Data.Field` naming the list) rather than cut short.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "Title": "May release",
  "Version": "1.4.2",
  "Status": "Candidate",
  "VoyageIds": ["vyg_abc123"],
  "MissionIds": ["msn_def456"],
  "CheckRunIds": ["chk_ghi789"]
}
```

- Response: `201 Created` - `Release`
- Notes: draft/candidate/shipped state, derived artifacts, notes, and version inference are all supported by the current internal release surface

#### GET /api/v1/releases/{id}

Read one release.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `Release`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/releases/{id}/github/pull-requests/enumerate

One page of the GitHub pull requests linked to one release's missions (`404` when the release is not visible). The distinct pull requests are resolved from the release's mission links (at most 1000), ordered by pull request number (newest first) then repository, and only the requested page is read from GitHub, so `TotalRecords` is exact and each page costs at most `PageSize` GitHub reads. Paging comes from the JSON body (`EnumerationQuery`: `PageNumber`, `PageSize` 1 to 1000, default 10) or the matching querystring parameters.

**Permission:** Authenticated (release scope; this replaces the removed `GET /api/v1/releases/{id}/github/pull-requests`)

**Response:** `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<T>` (`PageNumber`, `PageSize`, `TotalPages`, `TotalRecords`, `Objects`)

---

#### GET /api/v1/releases/{id}/github/pull-requests (removed)

> **Removed** (see [DEPRECATION.md](../DEPRECATION.md), D-009): answers `410 Gone` with a message naming the
> replacement. Use `POST /api/v1/releases/{id}/github/pull-requests/enumerate`. Each page object is a normalized
> `GitHubPullRequestDetail` derived from the `PrUrl` values on the release's missions; duplicate repository/PR
> combinations are de-duplicated, and Armada does not persist pull requests as entities.

#### PUT /api/v1/releases/{id}

Update one release and revalidate its linked work.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `ReleaseUpsertRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `Release`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### POST /api/v1/releases/{id}/refresh

Refresh one release from its current linked work.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `200 OK` - `Release`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/releases/{id}

Delete one release.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

---

### Incidents

Incident records capture operational failures, hotfix handoff, rollback linkage, and postmortem context tied to current deployment entities.

#### GET /api/v1/incidents

List incidents in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `vesselId`, `environmentId`, `deploymentId`, `releaseId`, `missionId`, `voyageId`, `status`, `severity`, `search`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Incident>`

#### POST /api/v1/incidents/enumerate

Enumerate incidents using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides. Incidents are stored one row each (their `incident.snapshot` events remain as history), so the filters (`VesselId`, `EnvironmentId`, `DeploymentId`, `ReleaseId`, `MissionId`, `VoyageId`, `Status`, `Severity`, `Search`) apply, the page is cut, and `TotalRecords` is counted in the database inside the caller's scope. Ordered by last update (newest first) then id. `PageSize` 1 to 1000 (default 50).

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `IncidentQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Incident>`

#### POST /api/v1/incidents

Create one incident. `RescueMissionIds` and `ObjectiveIds` hold at most 1000 ids (create and update reject more with `400`, `Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`); autonomous mission recovery appends at most `MaxMissionRecoveryAttempts` (5) rescues and never past the limit.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `IncidentUpsertRequest`
- Response: `201 Created` - `Incident`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`

#### GET /api/v1/incidents/{id}

Read one incident.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `Incident`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/incidents/{id}

Update one incident, including status, impact, recovery, and postmortem details.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `IncidentUpsertRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `Incident`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/incidents/{id}

Delete one incident.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

---

### Runbooks

Runbooks extend playbooks into parameterized operational procedures with step tracking, deployment and incident linkage, and event-backed execution history.

#### GET /api/v1/runbooks

List runbooks in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `workflowProfileId`, `environmentId`, `defaultCheckType`, `active`, `search`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Runbook>`

#### POST /api/v1/runbooks/enumerate

Enumerate runbooks using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides. A runbook is a playbook row, so the list is filtered, paged, and counted in the playbooks table inside the caller's scope: `WorkflowProfileId`, `EnvironmentId`, and `DefaultCheckType` match the runbook metadata block, `Active` the playbook flag, and `Search` the file name, description, and content (title, steps, overview), ignoring case. Ordered by file name then id. `PageSize` 1 to 1000 (default 50). `Parameters` and `Steps` hold at most 256 entries; a playbook written outside the runbook API with more reports the rest in `OmittedParameterCount` and `OmittedStepCount` (the text stays in `OverviewMarkdown`).

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `RunbookQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<Runbook>`

#### POST /api/v1/runbooks

Create one runbook backed by a playbook and optional explicit steps/parameters. `Steps` and `Parameters` hold at most 256 entries; create and update reject more with `400` (`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`).

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `RunbookUpsertRequest`
- Response: `201 Created` - `Runbook`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`

#### GET /api/v1/runbooks/{id}

Read one runbook.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `Runbook`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/runbooks/{id}

Update one runbook.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `RunbookUpsertRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `Runbook`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/runbooks/{id}

Delete one runbook.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### GET /api/v1/runbook-executions

List runbook executions in the caller scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize`, `runbookId`, `deploymentId`, `incidentId`, `status`, `search`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<RunbookExecution>`

#### POST /api/v1/runbook-executions/enumerate

Enumerate runbook executions using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides. Executions are stored one row each (their `runbook-execution.snapshot` events remain as history), so `RunbookId`, `DeploymentId`, `IncidentId`, `Status`, and `Search` filter, page, and count in the database inside the caller's scope. Ordered by last update (newest first) then id. `PageSize` 1 to 1000 (default 50).

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `RunbookExecutionQuery`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<RunbookExecution>`

#### POST /api/v1/runbooks/{id}/executions

Start one runbook execution. `ParameterValues` (the supplied values plus the runbook's defaults) holds at most 256 entries; more is rejected with `400` (`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`).

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `RunbookExecutionStartRequest`
- Response: `201 Created` - `RunbookExecution`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### GET /api/v1/runbook-executions/{id}

Read one runbook execution.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `RunbookExecution`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### PUT /api/v1/runbook-executions/{id}

Update one runbook execution, including step completion and notes. `CompletedStepIds` and `StepNotes` hold at most 256 entries; more is rejected with `400` (`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`).

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Request body: `RunbookExecutionUpdateRequest`
- Response: `200 OK` - `RunbookExecution`
- Errors: `400 Bad Request`, `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/runbook-executions/{id}

Delete one runbook execution.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

### History

`Activity` (All Activity) is backed by a cross-entity timeline that spans current Armada lifecycle entities such as objectives, releases, deployments, incidents, runbook executions, missions, voyages, planning sessions, merge entries, check runs, events, and request history.

#### GET /api/v1/history

List one page of historical timeline entries in the caller scope. The query is a `HistoricalTimelineQuery`, a
subclass of [EnumerationQuery](#enumerationquery): standard paging and `order` plus the timeline filters.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber` (default 1), `pageSize` (1-1000, default 50; values outside the range are clamped; the cap was 500), `order` (`CreatedDescending`, the default: newest `OccurredUtc` first; `CreatedAscending`: oldest first), `vesselId`, `missionId`, `voyageId`, `objectiveId`, `environmentId`, `deploymentId`, `incidentId`, `actor`, `text`, `sourceType` (comma-separated), `fromUtc`, `toUtc`, `postmortemOnly` (bool), `excludeReadRequests` (bool; leaves out request-history entries for `GET`, `HEAD`, and `OPTIONS` so the timeline is not dominated by polling)
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<HistoricalTimelineEntry>` in the requested order. `TotalRecords` and `TotalPages` count every matching entry; entries with the same `OccurredUtc` are ordered by a stable tie-break, so consecutive pages never overlap or skip an entry while the data is unchanged. A page past the last returns an empty `Objects` list with the true totals.
- Scope: a global admin sees every tenant; a tenant admin sees the tenant; a regular user sees only their own entries
- Source types: `Objective`, `ObjectiveRefinementSession`, `Planning`, `Voyage`, `Mission`, `MergeEntry`, `CheckRun`, `Release`, `Deployment`, `Incident`, `RunbookExecution`, `Event`, `Request`
- `HistoricalTimelineEntry`: `Id`, `SourceType`, `SourceId`, `EntityType`, `EntityId`, `ObjectiveId`, `VesselId`, `EnvironmentId`, `DeploymentId`, `IncidentId`, `MissionId`, `VoyageId`, `ActorId`, `ActorDisplay`, `Title`, `Description`, `Status`, `Severity`, `Route`, `OccurredUtc`, `MetadataJson`

#### POST /api/v1/history/enumerate

Enumerate historical timeline entries using a JSON body and optional querystring overrides.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Request body: `HistoricalTimelineQuery` (optional): `ObjectiveId`, `VesselId`, `EnvironmentId`, `DeploymentId`, `IncidentId`, `MissionId`, `VoyageId`, `PostmortemOnly`, `ExcludeReadRequests` (default `false`), `Actor`, `Text`, `SourceTypes` (string array), `FromUtc`, `ToUtc`, `PageNumber` (default 1), `PageSize` (1-1000, default 50), `Order` (`CreatedDescending` default, or `CreatedAscending`). `TenantId` and `UserId` narrow the view for admins only; they are overridden by the caller's scope otherwise.
- Querystring values (same names as `GET /api/v1/history`) override the body.
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<HistoricalTimelineEntry>`, with the same ordering, totals, and stable paging as the GET.

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:7890/api/v1/history/enumerate \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer <token>" -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"PageNumber": 2, "PageSize": 50, "Order": "CreatedAscending", "SourceTypes": ["Mission", "Voyage"]}'
```

---

### Runtime Helpers

These helper routes support runtime-specific UX and validation: Mux endpoint discovery for captain setup and editing.

#### GET /api/v1/runtimes/mux/endpoints

One page of the saved Mux endpoints, ordered by name, optionally from an explicit config directory. `mux` reads its
whole configuration file and cannot page, so the Admiral pages its answer: `Objects` is one page and `TotalRecords`
counts every saved endpoint. `POST /api/v1/runtimes/mux/endpoints/enumerate` is the POST twin (paging in the JSON body,
`configDirectory` and `harborId` in the querystring).

Mux runs where Mux captains run: on the caller's own connected Harbor, otherwise on another Harbor the tenant may use (unless `requireHarborForLaunch` restricts launches to the caller's own Harbors). Only an Admiral with no eligible Harbor runs `mux` itself. `configDirectory` is a path on that machine.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber` (default 1), `pageSize` (1 to 1000, default 10); optional `configDirectory`; optional `harborId` to ask a specific connected Harbor
- Response: `200 OK` - `MuxEndpointPage` (`EnumerationResult<MuxEndpointInfo>` plus `ContractVersion`, `ConfigDirectory`, `ErrorCode`, `ErrorMessage`), with `HarborId` set to the Harbor that answered (null when the Admiral ran mux). Each endpoint's `HeaderNames` and `Headers` hold at most 256 entries; mux reporting more is a `400` (`CollectionLimitExceeded`).
- Errors: `503 Service Unavailable` with `ErrorCode` `harbor_unavailable` when Harbors are required and none of the caller's is connected; `400 Bad Request` when mux cannot be started on the chosen machine

#### GET /api/v1/runtimes/mux/endpoints/{name}

Inspect one saved Mux endpoint with redacted secret values. Mux runs on the same machine as for the list above.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: optional `configDirectory`; optional `harborId`
- Response: `200 OK` - `MuxEndpointShowResult`, with `HarborId`
- Errors: `404 Not Found` when the named endpoint does not exist; `503 Service Unavailable` (`harbor_unavailable`) as above

---

### Request History

Armada captures sanitized REST request and response metadata for authenticated and unauthenticated traffic, excluding the request-history routes themselves. Secret-bearing headers and request bodies are redacted before persistence.

#### GET /api/v1/request-history

List captured request-history entries in the caller's scope.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `pageNumber`, `pageSize` (1-500, default 25), `method`, `route`, `statusCode`, `principal`, `tenantId`, `userId`, `credentialId`, `isSuccess`, `fromUtc`, `toUtc`
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<RequestHistoryEntry>`

#### POST /api/v1/request-history/enumerate

The same page with the filters in a JSON body.

**Permission:** Authenticated (scoped like the GET)

- Request body: `RequestHistoryQuery` (optional): `TenantId`, `UserId`, `CredentialId`, `Principal`, `Method`, `Route`, `StatusCode`, `IsSuccess`, `FromUtc`, `ToUtc`, `PageNumber` (default 1), `PageSize` (1-500, default 25). Querystring values override the body.
- Response: `200 OK` - `EnumerationResult<RequestHistoryEntry>`

#### GET /api/v1/request-history/summary

Return aggregate counts and time buckets for matching request-history entries.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Query: `bucketMinutes` (1 to 527040), `fromUtc`, `toUtc`, `method`, `route`, `statusCode`, `principal`
- Defaults: last 24 hours, 15-minute buckets
- At most 1000 buckets: a window and `bucketMinutes` that would produce more are rejected with `400` (`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`, `Data.Field` `Buckets`) before any entry is read
- Response: `200 OK` - `RequestHistorySummaryResult`

#### GET /api/v1/request-history/{id}

Read one captured request, including expanded headers, params, and body snapshots.

**Permission:** Authenticated

- Response: `200 OK` - `RequestHistoryRecord`
- Errors: `404 Not Found`

#### DELETE /api/v1/request-history/{id}

Delete one captured request-history entry within the caller's scope.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

- Response: `204 No Content`

#### POST /api/v1/request-history/delete/multiple

Delete multiple request-history entries by identifier.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "Ids": ["req_abc123", "req_def456"]
}
```

- At most 1000 ids; more is rejected with `400` (`Data.Code` `CollectionLimitExceeded`) and nothing is deleted. `Skipped` has one entry per id that was not deleted, so it is bounded by the request.
- Response: `200 OK` - `DeleteMultipleResult`

#### POST /api/v1/request-history/delete/by-filter

Delete all request-history entries matching the supplied filters within the caller's scope.

**Permission:** TenantAdmin

```json
{
  "Method": "GET",
  "Route": "/api/v1/status",
  "FromUtc": "2026-05-01T00:00:00Z",
  "ToUtc": "2026-05-02T00:00:00Z"
}
```

- Response: `200 OK` - `DeleteMultipleResult`
