#### ModelEndpoint

A managed reference to an external embedding or inference model behind a provider API.

```json
{
  "Id": "mep_abc123",
  "TenantId": "default",
  "UserId": "default",
  "Name": "Primary embeddings",
  "Kind": "Embedding",
  "Provider": "OpenAI",
  "BaseUrl": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
  "Model": "text-embedding-3-small",
  "Dimensionality": 1536,
  "TimeoutMs": 120000,
  "Enabled": true,
  "HasApiKey": true,
  "HealthStatus": "Healthy",
  "LastHealthCheckUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z",
  "LastHealthError": null,
  "LastLatencyMs": 84,
  "CreatedUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z",
  "LastUpdateUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z"
}
```

| Field | Type | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Id` | string | auto-generated | Unique ID with `mep_` prefix |
| `TenantId` | string? | null | Owning tenant ID |
| `UserId` | string? | null | Owning user ID |
| `Scope` | [ScopeEnum](#scopeenum) | `TenantWide` | Ownership scope: `TenantWide` or `UserSpecific`. See [Data Scoping](#data-scoping-who-sees-and-edits-what). |
| `Name` | string | required | Display name |
| `Kind` | string | `Embedding` | `Embedding` or `Inference` |
| `Provider` | string | `Ollama` | `Ollama`, `OpenAI`, `OpenAICompatible`, `Anthropic`, `Gemini`, `VoyageAI`, `AzureOpenAI`, `VertexAI`, `Bedrock`, `OllamaCloud`, or `XAI` |
| `BaseUrl` | string | required | Provider API base URL. Azure OpenAI: resource endpoint. Vertex AI / Bedrock: optional override (endpoint is derived from `Region`). OllamaCloud: fixed `https://ollama.com:443`. XAI: defaults to `https://api.x.ai`. |
| `Model` | string? | null | Model name to target. Azure OpenAI: deployment name. Bedrock: Bedrock model id. |
| `Region` | string? | null | Cloud region. Required for `VertexAI` and `Bedrock`. |
| `Project` | string? | null | GCP project id. Required for `VertexAI`. |
| `ApiVersion` | string? | null | API version for `AzureOpenAI` (defaults to the current GA version when omitted). |
| `AccessKeyId` | string? | null | AWS access key id for `Bedrock`. The paired secret access key is supplied write-only via `ApiKey`. |
| `Dimensionality` | int | 0 | Embedding dimensionality |
| `TimeoutMs` | int | 120000 | Request timeout in milliseconds (clamped to [1000, 600000]) |
| `Enabled` | bool | true | Whether the endpoint participates in health sweeps |
| `ApiKey` | string | -- | Write-only input (provider key / service-account JSON / AWS secret). Accepted on create/update; never returned on reads. |
| `HasApiKey` | bool | false | Read-only. Indicates whether a provider key/credential is stored. |
| `AuthType` | string | `ProviderDefault` | `ProviderDefault`, `None`, `Bearer`, `Basic`, `Header`, or `QueryParameter`; see Create Model Endpoint. |
| `AuthUsername` | string? | null | User name for `Basic` authentication. |
| `AuthHeaderName` | string? | null | Header that carries the secret for `Header` authentication. |
| `AuthQueryParameter` | string? | null | Query parameter that carries the secret for `QueryParameter` authentication. |
| `Headers` | array | [] | Extra headers sent with every request. Reads return each `{ Name, Value: null, HasValue }`; values are never returned. |
| `HealthStatus` | string | `Unknown` | `Unknown`, `Healthy`, or `Unhealthy` |
| `LastHealthCheckUtc` | datetime? | null | Timestamp of the last probe (UTC) |
| `LastHealthError` | string? | null | Error text from the last failed probe |
| `LastLatencyMs` | int? | null | Latency of the last probe in milliseconds |
| `HealthHistory` | array | [] | Rolling series of recent probes (oldest first), each `{ TimestampUtc, Success }`, at most 500 records (the health check drops the oldest). Server-owned: a create or duplicate ignores a supplied history and an update leaves it unchanged. Drives the dashboard health-history bar. `Headers` and `InferenceOptions.AllowedTools` hold at most 256 entries each (400 `CollectionLimitExceeded`). |
| `UptimePercentage` | double | 0 | Read-only. Percentage of retained probes that succeeded (0-100), derived from `HealthHistory`. |
| `ConsecutiveSuccesses` | int | 0 | Read-only. Trailing run of successful probes, derived from `HealthHistory`. |
| `ConsecutiveFailures` | int | 0 | Read-only. Trailing run of failed probes, derived from `HealthHistory`. |
| `FirstHealthCheckUtc` | datetime? | null | Read-only. Timestamp of the earliest retained probe, derived from `HealthHistory`. |
| `LastHealthyUtc` | datetime? | null | Read-only. Timestamp of the most recent successful probe, derived from `HealthHistory`. |
| `LastUnhealthyUtc` | datetime? | null | Read-only. Timestamp of the most recent failed probe, derived from `HealthHistory`. |
| `CreatedUtc` | datetime | now | Creation timestamp (UTC) |
| `LastUpdateUtc` | datetime | now | Last update timestamp (UTC) |

The `Anthropic` provider cannot be paired with `Kind` `Embedding`, and the `VoyageAI` provider cannot be paired with `Kind` `Inference`; both combinations are rejected with `400 Bad Request`.

Each health probe (from the base-URL-deduplicated background sweep or from `/validate`) appends a `{ TimestampUtc, Success }` record to `HealthHistory`; the derived fields above are computed from that series.

---

#### CliPermissionRequest

A permission prompt a CLI captain raised for one of its own tools (see [CLI Permissions](#cli-permissions)).

```json
{
  "Id": "cpr_abc123",
  "TenantId": "ten_abc123",
  "UserId": "usr_abc123",
  "CaptainId": "cpt_abc123",
  "MissionId": null,
  "VoyageId": null,
  "VesselId": null,
  "ThreadId": "ath_abc123",
  "MessageId": "amg_abc123",
  "Runtime": "ClaudeCode",
  "ToolName": "Bash",
  "InputText": "{\"command\":\"npm test\",\"description\":\"Run the tests\"}",
  "SummaryText": "npm test",
  "SuggestedRule": "Bash(npm test:*)",
  "Status": "Pending",
  "DecisionSource": null,
  "RuleId": null,
  "DecidedByUserId": null,
  "DecisionMessage": null,
  "ExpiresUtc": "2026-10-05T12:10:00Z",
  "DecidedUtc": null,
  "CreatedUtc": "2026-10-05T12:00:00Z",
  "LastUpdateUtc": "2026-10-05T12:00:00Z",
  "CaptainName": "captain-1",
  "VesselName": null,
  "MissionTitle": null,
  "ThreadTitle": "Fix the flaky test",
  "CanDecide": true,
  "CanRemember": true
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Id` | string | `cpr_` prefix |
| `TenantId`, `UserId` | string? | Tenant and owner (the thread or mission owner) |
| `CaptainId` | string? | Captain that asked |
| `MissionId`, `VoyageId`, `VesselId` | string? | The mission, its voyage and vessel; null for an Ask turn |
| `ThreadId`, `MessageId` | string? | The Ask thread and its `CliPermission` card message; null for a mission |
| `Runtime` | [AgentRuntimeEnum](#agentruntimeenum) | Runtime of the captain |
| `ToolName` | string | Tool as the CLI reported it (`Bash`, `WebFetch`, `Edit`, `mcp__server__tool`, ...) |
| `InputText` | string | Tool input JSON with secrets redacted (display only; the original input is never persisted) |
| `SummaryText` | string | One line: the command, URL, query, or path (redacted) |
| `SuggestedRule` | string? | Suggested allow rule, the starting pattern of `AllowAndRemember` (for Bash, the first subcommand's program and subcommand word as a prefix rule; for WebFetch the domain; for file tools the exact path) |
| `Status` | [CliPermissionRequestStatusEnum](#clipermissionrequeststatusenum) | |
| `DecisionSource` | [CliPermissionDecisionSourceEnum](#clipermissiondecisionsourceenum)? | What decided it; null while pending |
| `RuleId` | string? | Rule (`cpl_`) that decided it, or that `AllowAndRemember` created |
| `DecidedByUserId` | string? | Approver |
| `DecisionMessage` | string? | Message returned to the captain with a denial, or the approver's note |
| `ExpiresUtc` | datetime | Server deadline for applying the pending request’s default answer |
| `TimeoutDecision` | string | Snapshotted `Deny` or `Approve` answer at expiry |
| `DecidedUtc`, `CreatedUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc` | datetime | Timestamps (UTC) |
| `CaptainName`, `VesselName`, `MissionTitle` | string? | Filled on read |
| `ThreadTitle` | string? | Filled on read for the thread owner only |
| `CanDecide`, `CanRemember` | bool | Whether the caller may decide this pending request, and also remember it as a rule (filled per caller) |
| `CanAllowForConversation` | bool | Whether the caller may also allow every later call in the request's Ask thread (`AllowForConversation`; filled per caller) |

---

#### PushDevice

A mobile app installation registered for push notifications (see [Push Notifications](#push-notifications)).

```json
{
  "Id": "pdv_abc123",
  "TenantId": "ten_abc123",
  "UserId": "usr_abc123",
  "Platform": "Ios",
  "ExpoPushToken": "ExponentPushToken[****wxyz]",
  "DeviceName": "My iPhone",
  "AppVersion": "1.2.0",
  "Locale": "en-US",
  "Categories": ["AskProposal", "CliPermission"],
  "Active": true,
  "CreatedUtc": "2026-10-07T12:00:00Z",
  "LastSeenUtc": "2026-10-07T12:00:00Z",
  "LastUpdateUtc": "2026-10-07T12:00:00Z"
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Id` | string | `pdv_` prefix |
| `TenantId`, `UserId` | string? | Owner |
| `Platform` | [PushPlatformEnum](#pushplatformenum) | |
| `ExpoPushToken` | string | Masked in every response (prefix and the last four characters) |
| `DeviceName`, `AppVersion`, `Locale` | string? | As registered |
| `Categories` | [PushCategoryEnum](#pushcategoryenum)[] | Categories the device receives; empty mutes it |
| `Active` | bool | False after the push service reported the device as not registered; registering again reactivates it |
| `CreatedUtc`, `LastSeenUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc` | datetime | Creation, last registration, and last change (UTC) |

---

#### PushTestResult

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `DeviceId` | string | `pdv_` prefix |
| `Status` | [PushTestStatusEnum](#pushteststatusenum) | Outcome |
| `TicketId` | string? | Expo ticket id when accepted |
| `Error` | [PushErrorCodeEnum](#pusherrorcodeenum)? | Error code reported by the push service |
| `Message` | string? | Human-readable detail |

---

#### CliPermissionRule

```json
{
  "Id": "cpl_abc123",
  "TenantId": "ten_abc123",
  "Scope": "Captain",
  "VesselId": null,
  "CaptainId": "cpt_abc123",
  "Pattern": "Bash(npm test:*)",
  "Action": "Allow",
  "Description": "Remembered from cpr_abc123",
  "CreatedByUserId": "usr_admin",
  "CreatedUtc": "2026-10-05T12:01:00Z",
  "LastUpdateUtc": "2026-10-05T12:01:00Z"
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Id` | string | `cpl_` prefix |
| `TenantId` | string? | Tenant the rule applies to; null (global admins only) applies to every tenant. Tenant admins' rules always belong to their tenant; `Vessel` and `Captain` rules take the target's tenant |
| `Scope` | [CliPermissionRuleScopeEnum](#clipermissionrulescopeenum) | Default `Global` |
| `VesselId` | string? | Required for a `Vessel` rule |
| `CaptainId` | string? | Required for a `Captain` rule |
| `Pattern` | string | Rule in Claude Code permission rule syntax (validated; `400` when malformed) |
| `Action` | [CliPermissionRuleActionEnum](#clipermissionruleactionenum) | Default `Allow` |
| `Description` | string? | Optional note (max 1000 characters) |
| `CreatedByUserId` | string? | Creator (server-set) |
| `CreatedUtc`, `LastUpdateUtc` | datetime | Timestamps (UTC) |

---

#### CliPermissionResolution

The policy a captain launch runs with (`AskThread.CliPermission` for the thread's next turn; mission logs print its
`Note`).

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Requested` | [CliPermissionPolicyEnum](#clipermissionpolicyenum) | The policy the resolution order selected |
| `Effective` | [CliPermissionPolicyEnum](#clipermissionpolicyenum) | The policy the launch uses (`Requested`, or `Refuse` after a fallback) |
| `Source` | string | `AskThread`, `VesselAutoApprove`, `Captain`, `CaptainAutoApprove` (the legacy `autoApprove` option), or `ServerDefault` |
| `FallbackReason` | string? | Why `ApproveInArmada` runs as `Refuse`: `RuntimeUnsupported` (no permission prompt hook; only Claude Code, Mux 1.5.0 or later, pi, and ApiEndpoint have one), `NoSessionToken` (a Claude Code, Mux, or pi launch without a scoped MCP token), or `RemoteHarbor` (the launch runs on a Harbor that does not advertise the `cli-permission-prompt` capability for the runtime); null otherwise |
| `Note` | string | One-line English explanation, including where to change the policy |

---

### Enumerations

All enumerations serialize as strings in JSON (e.g., `"InProgress"`, not `2`).

#### ScopeEnum

Ownership scope for Category B configuration entities (see [Data Scoping](#data-scoping-who-sees-and-edits-what)). On workflow profiles and project profiles this is carried by the `ownershipScope` field (their `scope` field means application scope).

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `TenantWide` | Visible to everyone in the tenant; editable only by tenant/global admins. The default and the value for built-in/seeded objects. |
| `UserSpecific` | Owned by a single user (`UserId`); visible and editable only by that user (or an admin). |

---

#### MissionStatusEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Pending` | Created but not yet assigned to a captain |
| `Assigned` | Assigned to a captain, awaiting work start |
| `InProgress` | Captain is actively working |
| `WorkProduced` | Agent exited successfully; work ready for landing |
| `PullRequestOpen` | Pull request created, awaiting merge confirmation |
| `Testing` | Work complete, under automated testing |
| `Review` | Awaiting human review |
| `Complete` | Successfully completed - code landed (terminal) |
| `Failed` | Mission failed (terminal) |
| `LandingFailed` | Landing (merge/PR) failed; may be retried |
| `Cancelled` | Mission cancelled (terminal) |

---

#### MissionModeEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Implementation` | Standard write mission: the captain changes the repository and the result lands or holds for review. An empty diff is treated as a no-op failure. This is the default. |
| `Audit` | Read-only audit: the captain inspects the repository and reports findings without modifying files. The landing gate treats "no commit" as success. |
| `Research` | Read-only research: the captain investigates a question and reports its conclusions without modifying files. The landing gate treats "no commit" as success. |

---

#### LandingModeEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `LocalMerge` | Merge the branch into the default branch in the vessel's working directory; nothing is pushed |
| `MergeAndPush` | Integrate and verify in the mission dock, then push the exact verified commit to the remote target branch (the default); a dirty developer checkout is left untouched |
| `PullRequest` | Create a pull request and poll for merge confirmation |
| `MergeQueue` | Enqueue the branch into Armada's merge queue |
| `None` | No automated landing; leave work on the branch |

---

#### BranchCleanupPolicyEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `LocalOnly` | Delete the local branch after landing |
| `LocalAndRemote` | Delete both local and remote branches after landing |
| `None` | Do not delete branches after landing |

---

#### VoyageStatusEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Open` | Created, missions being set up |
| `InProgress` | Has active missions in progress |
| `Complete` | All missions completed |
| `Cancelled` | Voyage was cancelled |

---

#### PlaybookDeliveryModeEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `InlineFullContent` | Include the entire markdown body directly in the rendered mission instructions |
| `InstructionWithReference` | **Default.** Materialize the playbook outside the worktree and instruct the model to read the resolved path (on a Harbor, where the captain cannot read the Admiral's files, it is attached into the dock instead) |
| `AttachIntoWorktree` | Materialize the playbook under the dock worktree and instruct the model to read it there |

---

#### CaptainStateEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Idle` | Available for assignment |
| `Working` | Actively working on a mission |
| `Stalled` | Process appears stalled (no heartbeat) |
| `Stopping` | In the process of stopping |

---

#### AgentRuntimeEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `ClaudeCode` | Anthropic Claude Code CLI |
| `Codex` | OpenAI Codex CLI |
| `Gemini` | Google Gemini CLI |
| `Cursor` | Cursor agent CLI |
| `Mux` | Mux CLI |
| `OpenCode` | OpenCode CLI (OpenAI-compatible providers) |
| `Pi` | pi coding agent CLI (pi.dev; many providers) |
| `Custom` | Custom agent runtime |

---

#### SignalTypeEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Assignment` | Mission assignment notification |
| `Progress` | Progress update from captain |
| `Completion` | Mission completion notification |
| `Error` | Error notification |
| `Heartbeat` | Heartbeat signal |
| `Nudge` | Ephemeral nudge message |
| `Mail` | Persistent mail message |

---

#### MergeStatusEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Queued` | Waiting to be picked up |
| `Testing` | Currently being tested |
| `Passed` | Tests passed, ready to land |
| `Failed` | Tests failed |
| `Landed` | Successfully merged into target branch |
| `Cancelled` | Removed from queue |

---

#### HarborConnectionStatusEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Unknown` | No link has been established yet, or the state is not yet known |
| `Connected` | The Harbor currently has a live link to the Admiral |
| `Degraded` | The link is present but impaired (e.g. missed heartbeats) |
| `Disconnected` | The Harbor is registered but has no live link |

---

#### EnumerationOrderEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `CreatedAscending` | Sort by creation date, oldest first |
| `CreatedDescending` | Sort by creation date, newest first (default) |

---

#### CliPermissionPolicyEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Refuse` | Tools that need approval are refused; the turn or mission continues without them |
| `ApproveInArmada` | The CLI's permission prompts become CLI permission requests that an approver decides (Claude Code, Mux 1.5.0 or later, and the built-in `run_process` tool of ApiEndpoint captains; other runtimes run it as `Refuse`) |
| `Bypass` | The runtime's own permission-bypass flag (for example `--dangerously-skip-permissions`) |

---

#### PushPlatformEnum

`Ios`, `Android`.

#### PushCategoryEnum

`AskProposal`, `CliPermission`, `MissionReview`, `DeploymentApproval`, `MissionFailed`, `LandingFailed`,
`CaptainStalled`, `VoyageFinished` (see [Push Notifications](#push-notifications)).

#### PushTestStatusEnum

| Value | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `Sent` | Accepted by the Expo Push Service (final delivery is reported by a receipt later) |
| `Disabled` | `Push.Enabled` is false |
| `DeviceInactive` | The device is inactive; register it again from the app |
| `DeviceNotRegistered` | The push service reported the device as not registered; it was deactivated |
| `RateLimited` | The per-user rate limit was reached |
| `Failed` | The push service rejected the message or could not be reached |

#### PushErrorCodeEnum

`DeviceNotRegistered`, `MessageTooBig`, `MessageRateExceeded`, `MismatchSenderId`, `InvalidCredentials`, `Unknown`
(mapped from the Expo Push Service's `details.error`).

#### CliPermissionRequestStatusEnum

| Value | Description |
|---|---|
| `Pending` | Waiting for an approver |
| `Allowed` | Allowed by an approver or an allow rule; the tool ran with its original input |
| `Denied` | Denied by an approver or a deny rule |
| `Expired` | Timer expired with `TimeoutDecision: Deny`; denied |
| `Cancelled` | The turn or mission ended (or the Admiral restarted) before a decision; denied |

---

#### CliPermissionDecisionSourceEnum

`Approver`, `AllowRule`, `DenyRule`, `ConversationBypass` (allowed without asking because the Ask thread was set to
`Bypass` by `AllowForConversation`), `Timeout`, `Cancelled`, `DeliveryFailed`.

---

#### CliPermissionDecisionEnum

`AllowOnce`, `AllowAndRemember` (admins only; also stores an allow rule), `AllowForConversation` (the admin owner of the
Ask thread only: allows the call, sets the thread's CLI permission policy to `Bypass`, allows the thread's other pending
requests, and allows later prompts from the turn already running), `Deny`.

---

#### CliPermissionRuleActionEnum

`Allow`, `Deny` (deny rules win).

---

#### WorkspaceTerminalLocationEnum

`Harbor` (the Harbor that holds the vessel's checkout), `AdmiralHost` (the vessel's working directory on the Admiral's computer), `None` (no checkout).

---

#### CliPermissionRuleScopeEnum

`Global` (every captain of the rule's tenant, or of every tenant when the rule has no tenant), `Vessel` (missions on one
vessel), `Captain` (one captain).

---

### Request Types

#### EnumerationQuery

Query parameters for paginated enumeration. Used as the POST body for all `/enumerate` endpoints.

```json
{
  "PageNumber": 1,
  "PageSize": 25,
  "Order": "CreatedDescending",
  "CreatedAfter": "2026-03-01T00:00:00Z",
  "CreatedBefore": null,
  "Status": "InProgress",
  "FleetId": null,
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "CaptainId": null,
  "VoyageId": null,
  "MissionId": null,
  "EventType": null,
  "SignalType": null,
  "ToCaptainId": null,
  "UnreadOnly": null
}
```

All fields are optional. Omitted fields use defaults. See [Pagination](#pagination) for full details.

---

#### VoyageRequest

Request body for creating a voyage with missions.

```json
{
  "Title": "API Hardening",
  "Description": "Security improvements",
  "VesselId": "vsl_abc123",
  "SelectedPlaybooks": [
    {"PlaybookId": "pbk_abc123", "DeliveryMode": "InlineFullContent"}
  ],
  "Pipeline": "FullPipeline",
  "Missions": [
    {"Title": "Add rate limiting", "Description": "Add rate limiting middleware"},
    {"Title": "Add input validation", "Description": "Validate all POST endpoints"}
  ]
}
```

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Title` | string | yes | Voyage title |
| `Description` | string | no | Voyage description |
| `VesselId` | string | yes | Target vessel ID |
| `Missions` | array | no | List of MissionRequest objects |
| `SelectedPlaybooks` | array | no | Ordered [SelectedPlaybook](#selectedplaybook) rows to apply to the voyage |
| `PipelineId` | string | no | Pipeline ID override |
| `Pipeline` | string | no | Pipeline name override |
| `ObjectiveId` | string? | null | Objective (backlog item) to link the voyage to |
| `CaptainAssignments` | array? | null | Per-persona captain overrides: each entry binds a persona to a preferred captain and a fallback tier for every mission of that persona in the voyage |
| `LandingMode` | [LandingModeEnum](#landingmodeenum)? | null | Landing mode for this voyage's missions; null inherits the vessel's, then the global default |
| `IsEvaluation` | bool | false | Evaluation work: the voyage and its missions are flagged and their stage outcomes never count as production data |

---

#### MissionRequest

A mission within a VoyageRequest.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Title` | string | yes | Mission title |
| `Description` | string | no | Mission description/instructions |
| `RequestedCaptainId` | string? | null | Preferred captain; used when idle, otherwise dispatch falls back by `Tier` |
| `Tier` | string? | null | Required/fallback capability tier (`CaptainTierEnum`) |

---

#### StatusTransitionRequest

Request body for transitioning a mission status.

```json
{
  "Status": "InProgress"
}
```

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Status` | string | yes | Target status name (case-insensitive) |

---

#### CliPermissionDecisionRequest

Body of `POST /api/v1/cli-permissions/requests/{id}/decide` (and the WebSocket `decide_cli_permission_request` data).

```json
{
  "Decision": "AllowAndRemember",
  "Message": null,
  "RulePattern": "Bash(npm test:*)",
  "RuleScope": "Captain"
}
```

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Decision` | [CliPermissionDecisionEnum](#clipermissiondecisionenum) | yes | `AllowOnce`, `AllowAndRemember`, `AllowForConversation`, or `Deny` (default `Deny`) |
| `Message` | string? | no | Returned to the captain with a denial; recorded with an allow (max 2000 characters) |
| `RulePattern` | string? | no | Rule stored by `AllowAndRemember`; defaults to the request's `SuggestedRule` |
| `RuleScope` | [CliPermissionRuleScopeEnum](#clipermissionrulescopeenum) | no | Scope of that rule (default `Captain`); `Vessel` uses the request's vessel (missions only), `Global` the request's tenant |

---

#### CliPermissionPolicyUpdateRequest

Body of `PUT /api/v1/captains/{id}/cli-permission-policy` and `PUT /api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/cli-permission-policy`.

| Field | Type | Required | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Policy` | [CliPermissionPolicyEnum](#clipermissionpolicyenum)? | no | New policy, or null to clear the override (the next level of the resolution order applies) |

---

### Response Wrappers

#### DuplicateEntityErrorDetail

The `Data` of a `409 Conflict` for a value that must be unique and is already taken (see
[Duplicate entities](#duplicate-entities)).

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Code` | string | Always `DuplicateEntity` |
| `EntityType` | string | Entity type, for example `Captain`, `Fleet`, `User` |
| `Field` | string \| null | The unique field that is taken (`Name`, `Email`, `FileName`), or null when the database reported the duplicate without saying which field |
| `Value` | string \| null | The value that is taken, or null when unknown |

---

#### EnumerationResult\<T\>

Paginated result wrapper returned by all list and enumerate endpoints.

```json
{
  "Success": true,
  "PageNumber": 1,
  "PageSize": 25,
  "TotalPages": 4,
  "TotalRecords": 87,
  "Objects": [ ... ],
  "TotalMs": 3.14
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Success` | bool | Whether the operation succeeded |
| `PageNumber` | int | Current page number (1-based) |
| `PageSize` | int | Number of items per page |
| `TotalPages` | int | Total number of pages |
| `TotalRecords` | long | Total records matching the query |
| `Objects` | array\<T\> | Result objects for this page |
| `TotalMs` | double | Query execution time in milliseconds |

---

#### VoyageProgress

Response from `GET /api/v1/voyages/{id}` and each row of `POST /api/v1/voyages/progress/enumerate`: a voyage with counts
of its missions (missions are never embedded).

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Voyage` | [Voyage](#voyage) | Voyage details |
| `TotalMissions` | int | Missions in the voyage |
| `CompletedMissions` | int | Complete or WorkProduced missions |
| `FailedMissions` | int | Failed or LandingFailed missions |
| `InProgressMissions` | int | Assigned, InProgress, Verifying, Testing, Review, or PullRequestOpen missions |
| `MissionCountsByStatus` | object | Count per mission status (only statuses with missions; at most one entry per status) |
| `VesselId` | string | Vessel of the earliest mission, or null |
| `VesselCount` | int | Distinct vessels the missions target |

---

#### MissionDiff

Response from `GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/diff`.

```json
{
  "MissionId": "msn_abc123",
  "Branch": "armada/msn_abc123",
  "Diff": "diff --git ..."
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `MissionId` | string | Mission ID |
| `Branch` | string | Branch name |
| `Diff` | string | Git diff output |

---

#### MissionLog

Response from `GET /api/v1/missions/{id}/log`.

```json
{
  "MissionId": "msn_abc123",
  "Log": "line1\nline2\n...",
  "Lines": 100,
  "TotalLines": 542
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `MissionId` | string | Mission ID |
| `Log` | string | Log content (newline-delimited) |
| `Lines` | integer | Number of lines returned |
| `TotalLines` | integer | Total lines in log file |

---

#### CaptainLog

Response from `GET /api/v1/captains/{id}/log`.

```json
{
  "CaptainId": "cpt_abc123",
  "Log": "line1\nline2\n...",
  "Lines": 100,
  "TotalLines": 203
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `CaptainId` | string | Captain ID |
| `Log` | string | Log content (newline-delimited) |
| `Lines` | integer | Number of lines returned |
| `TotalLines` | integer | Total lines in log file |

---

#### ModelEndpointProbeResult

Result of `POST /api/v1/model-endpoints/{id}/validate`. Describes the outcome of a single real request against the provider.

```json
{
  "Success": true,
  "BaseUrl": "https://api.openai.com/v1",
  "LatencyMs": 92,
  "StatusCode": 200,
  "Error": null,
  "EmbeddingDimensions": 1536,
  "SampleText": null,
  "TimestampUtc": "2026-03-07T12:00:00Z"
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `Success` | bool | Whether the probe request succeeded |
| `BaseUrl` | string \| null | Base URL that was probed |
| `LatencyMs` | int | Round-trip latency in milliseconds |
| `StatusCode` | int \| null | HTTP status code returned by the provider, when available |
| `Error` | string \| null | Error text when the probe failed |
| `EmbeddingDimensions` | int \| null | Dimensionality returned by an embedding probe |
| `SampleText` | string \| null | Sample completion text returned by an inference probe |
| `Note` | string \| null | Validation only: set for an Admiral-local address (`localhost`, a loopback IP), saying a Harbor-hosted mission calls that address on the Harbor's computer, which the check did not reach |
| `TimestampUtc` | datetime | When the probe ran (UTC) |

---

#### ModelEndpointHealthSweepResponse

Result of `POST /api/v1/model-endpoints/health-check`.

```json
{
  "DistinctBaseUrlsProbed": 3
}
```

| Field | Type | Description |
|---|---|---|
| `DistinctBaseUrlsProbed` | int | Number of distinct base URLs probed during the sweep |

---

## Quick Endpoint Summary

Every REST route in the frozen 1.0 surface, generated from [api-surface-1.0.json](api-surface-1.0.json), with the
permission level each route declares in `RouteAuthorizationRegistry` (`NoAuthRequired`, `Authenticated`, `TenantAdmin`,
`AdminOnly`; see [Authorization Tiers](#authorization-tiers)). Handlers may narrow further by ownership or role, as
the matrix and each route's section describe. `/openapi.json` and `/swagger` remain the live source for schemas.

| # | Method | URL | Description | Auth |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | PUT | `/api/v1/account/password` | Change the caller's password | Authenticated |
| 2 | GET | `/api/v1/ask/quick-actions` | List quick actions | Authenticated |
| 3 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads` | Create an Ask Armada thread | Authenticated |
| 4 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/enumerate` | Enumerate my Ask Armada threads | Authenticated |
| 5 | GET | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}` | Read an Ask Armada thread | Authenticated |
| 6 | PUT | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}` | Update an Ask Armada thread | Authenticated |
| 7 | DELETE | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}` | Delete an Ask Armada thread | Authenticated |
| 8 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/actions` | Run a quick action | Authenticated |
| 9 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/cancel` | Stop the running turn | Authenticated |
| 10 | PUT | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/cli-permission-policy` | Set a thread's CLI tool permission policy | Authenticated |
| 11 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/messages` | Send a message | Authenticated |
| 12 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/messages/enumerate` | Enumerate thread messages | Authenticated |
| 13 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/proposals/{pid}/approve` | Approve a proposal | Authenticated |
| 14 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/proposals/{pid}/reject` | Reject a proposal | Authenticated |
| 15 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/read` | Mark a thread read | Authenticated |
| 16 | POST | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/summarize` | Summarize a thread | Authenticated |
| 17 | GET | `/api/v1/ask/threads/{id}/work/{workId}` | Read a work snapshot | Authenticated |
| 18 | POST | `/api/v1/authenticate` | Authenticate and get session token | NoAuthRequired |
| 19 | GET | `/api/v1/backlog` | List backlog items | Authenticated |
| 20 | POST | `/api/v1/backlog` | Create a backlog item | TenantAdmin |
| 21 | POST | `/api/v1/backlog/enumerate` | Enumerate backlog items | Authenticated |
| 22 | POST | `/api/v1/backlog/reorder` | Reorder backlog items | TenantAdmin |
| 23 | GET | `/api/v1/backlog/{id}` | Get a backlog item | Authenticated |
| 24 | PUT | `/api/v1/backlog/{id}` | Update a backlog item | TenantAdmin |
| 25 | DELETE | `/api/v1/backlog/{id}` | Delete a backlog item | TenantAdmin |
| 26 | POST | `/api/v1/backlog/{id}/refinement-sessions` | Create a backlog refinement session | TenantAdmin |
| 27 | POST | `/api/v1/backlog/{id}/refinement-sessions/enumerate` | Enumerate refinement sessions for a backlog item | Authenticated |
| 28 | GET | `/api/v1/backup` | Download backup | AdminOnly |
| 29 | POST | `/api/v1/battery/packs/enumerate` | Enumerate fixture battery packs | Authenticated |
| 30 | GET | `/api/v1/blackbox` | List black box entries | Authenticated |
| 31 | POST | `/api/v1/blackbox/backfill` | Backfill black box entries | AdminOnly |
| 32 | POST | `/api/v1/blackbox/bundle` | Build a maintainer bundle | Authenticated |
| 33 | POST | `/api/v1/blackbox/delete` | Delete black box entries | TenantAdmin |
| 34 | POST | `/api/v1/blackbox/enumerate` | Enumerate black box entries | Authenticated |
| 35 | POST | `/api/v1/blackbox/fingerprints/enumerate` | Enumerate black box fingerprints | Authenticated |
| 36 | GET | `/api/v1/blackbox/fingerprints/{fingerprint}/trend` | Get the trend of a black box failure mode | Authenticated |
| 37 | POST | `/api/v1/blackbox/gc` | Run black box garbage collection now | AdminOnly |
| 38 | POST | `/api/v1/blackbox/hotspots/enumerate` | Enumerate black box hotspots | Authenticated |
| 39 | POST | `/api/v1/blackbox/purge` | Purge black box entries by filter | AdminOnly |
| 40 | GET | `/api/v1/blackbox/retention` | Get black box retention status | TenantAdmin |

## Additional Ports

| Service | Default Port | Description |
|---|---|---|
| Admiral REST API | 7890 | This API (WebSocket available at /ws on the same port) |
| MCP Server | 7891 | Model Context Protocol (Voltaic) for AI tool use |

## CORS

All responses include permissive CORS headers:
```
Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *
Access-Control-Allow-Methods: GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, PATCH, OPTIONS
Access-Control-Allow-Headers: Content-Type, X-Api-Key, X-Token, Authorization
Access-Control-Expose-Headers: Retry-After
```

### .NET SDK persona priorities

`Armada.Client` 1.2.0 uses the shared `Armada.Core.Models.Persona` contract. `CreatePersonaAsync`, `GetPersonaAsync`, `ListPersonasAsync`, and `UpdatePersonaAsync` carry `CaptainPriorities`, `PriorityAvailability`, and `PriorityExhaustion` without additional SDK methods. Read the persona before editing it, then pass the updated object to `UpdatePersonaAsync`; this method sends the complete persona, including its routing settings. Clear `CaptainPriorities` to restore legacy routing.

```csharp
Persona persona = (await client.GetPersonaAsync("reviewer"))!;
persona.CaptainPriorities = new List<PersonaCaptainPriority>
{
    new PersonaCaptainPriority { Runtime = AgentRuntimeEnum.Codex },
    new PersonaCaptainPriority { Runtime = AgentRuntimeEnum.Mux, Endpoint = "local", Model = "gpt-oss:120b" }
};
persona.PriorityAvailability = PersonaPriorityAvailabilityEnum.FallThrough;
persona.PriorityExhaustion = PersonaPriorityExhaustionEnum.Wait;
await client.UpdatePersonaAsync(persona.Name, persona);
```

### Persona prompt improvements

The default persona guidance and additive replication procedure are documented in [Prompt and Handoff Improvements](../PROMPT_AND_HANDOFF_IMPROVEMENTS.md). Live prompt-template edits apply when future mission instructions are generated; they do not rewrite in-flight briefs. No request fields, output signals, plan schema, or endpoints change. Embedded defaults in upgraded builds include the guidance; startup upgrades only recognized untouched previous defaults and retains operator-edited templates.

### POST /api/v1/activity/synthetic

Global admin only. Body: `{ "BatchId": "screenshots-unique", "Days": 42, "Density": 3, "Entropy": 1.3, "Seed": 42, "ToUtc": "2026-10-14T01:00:00Z" }`. Supply a current/past UTC `ToUtc`. Days 1–90, density 0.1–5, entropy 0–2; a 50,000-record planning budget bounds the profile. Invalid profiles return 400, unauthenticated callers 401, and non-global admins 403.

Creates explicitly synthetic terminal missions, API records, events, read signals, model tokens, and disabled screenshot Harbor/vessel resources. Ownership comes from authenticated context. The .NET SDK exposes `GenerateSyntheticActivityAsync`. HTTP 200 returns `SyntheticActivityResult`: batch ID, Harbor/vessel IDs, `Created` counts per record type, and `Existing` replay count. Exact profile replays skip existing IDs; changed profiles are separate datasets. No inference or dispatch occurs. Partial failures can be resumed with the preserved profile. See [CLI and chart inventory](SYNTHETIC_ACTIVITY.md).

`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings` also exposes `DataRetentionDays` (completed mission/event/signal history) and `RequestHistoryRetentionDays` (API history). Both accept non-negative days; zero disables expiry. PUT patches only supplied values, applies changes live and persists them; negative retention values return 400 before mutation. Set these beyond the screenshot window to preserve historical samples.

## Storage inventory

Storage endpoints are global system administrator operations. Tenant administrators and regular users receive `403`; anonymous callers receive `401`. Existing bearer/session and administrator API-key authentication apply. Responses never contain connection strings or file contents. Paths and cross-tenant record counts are intentionally administrator-only.

### GET /api/v1/storage

Returns `200` with the latest cached `StorageSnapshot`. There are no path or query parameters and no request body. Reading this endpoint does not start scans or exact counts. Before the initial sample, `MeasuredUtc` is absent, `History` is empty, `HostCount` is 0, and the collection may already be running. Host reports are not embedded: page them with [`GET /api/v1/storage/hosts`](#get-apiv1storagehosts).

```bash
curl -H "Authorization: Bearer $ARMADA_TOKEN" "$ARMADA_URL/api/v1/storage"
```

| Field | Type | Meaning |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `MeasuredUtc` | UTC timestamp, optional | Last completed Admiral inventory |
| `Collecting` | boolean | A scan is running; concurrent refresh requests coalesce |
| `LastError` | string, optional | `CollectionTimedOut` or `CollectionFailed`; previous measurements remain |
| `Database` | object | Provider, measurement time, `AllocatedBytes`, `UnusedBytes`, `Status`, `Warnings`, `Tables` |
| `HostCount`, `FreshHostCount` | integers | Hosts with a report (the Admiral plus one per registered Harbor), and those whose report is current and `Complete` |
| `ObservedFileBytes` | integer | Observed file bytes across every host's measured locations |
| `History` | object array | Process-local samples (at most `HistoryCapacity`, which is at most 1000) with timestamp, observed file bytes, database bytes, and numeric `Categories`, `Hosts`, `Tables` maps. Each map keeps its 24 largest keys and folds the rest into one `(other)` key, so the totals still add up |
| `Retention` | integer map | Current settings: `DataDays`, `RequestHistoryDays`, `PlanningSessionDays`, `AskArchiveDays`, `AskDeleteDays`, `JobDays`, `ImportDays`, `CliPermissionDays`, `FleetActionDays`, `BuildSlots` |
| `IntervalSeconds`, `HistoryCapacity` | integers | Active collection cadence and recent-history limit |

`Database.Tables` lists at most 1000 tables (`TableLimit` warning when there are more).

Database table entries contain `Schema`, `Name`, optional `Rows`, `CountKind` (`Exact`, `Estimated`, `Unavailable`), optional `DataBytes`, `IndexBytes`, and `LogicalBytes`, and an optional warning code. SQLite uses page allocation and free pages; optional dbstat support supplies table allocation. Bounded background SQLite queries also supply exact row counts and logical visible-column value bytes independently of dbstat. Logical bytes count numeric values as eight bytes, text/blob byte lengths, and NULL as zero; they exclude record/page/index overhead and are not allocation. PostgreSQL uses database/table/index sizes and estimated live rows. MySQL reports catalog allocation and estimated rows. SQL Server reports database file allocation and catalog table/index pages with estimated counts. Missing metrics remain absent rather than becoming zero.

Location entries contain `Category`, `Path`, optional `EntityId` and `Activity`, optional `Bytes`, `FileCount`, optional oldest/newest UTC write times, `Status`, `Warnings`, and bounded `LargestFiles` entries (`Path`, `Bytes`, `LastWriteUtc`). Counts are examined files, not database records. More specific roots own their subtree; duplicate normalized roots are measured once. Symbolic links and reparse points, including ancestor components, are skipped. Hard links, shared mounts across reporting processes, and copy-on-write extents are not physical-byte accounting. Configured roots outside the data directory are included. Unknown files within the data directory are classified as `Other`.

Volume entries contain `Path`, optional `TotalBytes`, optional `AvailableBytes`, and `Status`. Capacity includes unrelated files and describes the process/container-visible mount. Remote database host capacity is not inferred. Volume capacity, logical file lengths and database allocation must not be summed together.

Statuses distinguish `Pending`, `Complete`, `Partial`, `Missing`, `Unavailable` and `Skipped`. Missing optional directories do not make the whole host incomplete. Warnings are stable codes translated by the dashboard: `BudgetExceeded`, `LinkSkipped`, `AccessDenied`, `FileChangedOrUnreadable`, `Unreadable`, `InvalidPath`, `LocationLimit`, `VolumesUnavailable`, `TableLimit`, `TableAllocationUnavailable`, `DatabaseAllocationUnavailable`, `DatabaseStatisticsUnavailable`, `ExactCountUnavailable`, `CollectionTimedOut`, and `HarborReportUnavailable`.

### GET /api/v1/storage/hosts

Returns `200` with one page (`EnumerationResult<StorageHostReport>`) of the cached host reports: the Admiral first, then
each Harbor by name and id. Each report has `HostId`, `Name`, `MeasuredUtc`, `Stale`, `Status`, `Warnings` (at most 20),
`Locations` (at most `MaxLocations`, 10 to 5000), and `Volumes` (at most 256). Querystring: `pageNumber` (default 1),
`pageSize` (1 to 1000, default 10). `POST /api/v1/storage/hosts/enumerate` takes the `EnumerationQuery` in the body
(querystring values override it; a malformed body is `400`). Global administrators only; reading does not start scans.

```bash
curl -H "Authorization: Bearer $ARMADA_TOKEN" "$ARMADA_URL/api/v1/storage/hosts?pageSize=25"
```

### POST /api/v1/storage/refresh

Requests background collection and returns `202` with a cached `StorageSnapshot` immediately. Optional JSON body:

```json
{ "ExactCounts": false }
```

`ExactCounts` defaults to false. True explicitly requests table scans, with a five-second per-query timeout and an overall database-stage budget. Exact counts on different tables are measured sequentially and are not a transactionally consistent cross-table snapshot. An already running request is coalesced; a later exact-count request does not upgrade the in-flight scan. Wait until `Collecting` is false and request again if exact counts are needed. Poll GET to observe completion.

Invalid JSON or a non-boolean exact-count value returns `400`. `401` and `403` have the same access rules as GET. No parameters select arbitrary paths, SQL, databases or hosts. The operation does not delete, prune, vacuum, checkpoint, restart services or force a remote Harbor scan.

```bash
curl -X POST -H "Authorization: Bearer $ARMADA_TOKEN" \
  -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"ExactCounts":true}' \
  "$ARMADA_URL/api/v1/storage/refresh"
```

### Configuration, freshness and retention

`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings` includes a `Storage` group. A PUT replaces the supplied group, with omitted fields taking defaults. Settings also persist in settings.json:

| Setting | Default | Bounds |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `IntervalSeconds` | 300 | 30..86400 |
| `TimeoutSeconds` | 30 | 1..300 per discovery/filesystem/database stage |
| `MaxEntries` | 250000 | 100..2000000 examined filesystem entries per host |
| `LargestFileLimit` | 20 | 1..100 per location |
| `MaxLocations` | 1000 | 10..5000 per host |
| `HistoryCapacity` | 288 | 2..1000 process-local samples (larger values are normalized to 1000) |

Local collection starts in the background. Harbors report asynchronously on their own five-minute cadence through an additive optional heartbeat field. A disconnected Harbor retains its last report and is marked stale; an old Harbor, one with heartbeats disabled, or a freshly restarted Admiral without a report shows unavailable coverage. Reports older than twice the greater of the source cadence and the configured Admiral interval are stale. Host reports include `IntervalSeconds` (300 on the Harbor app). An administrator-triggered local refresh does not force Harbor collection. The page identifies host coverage and timestamps beside observed totals.

Recent history and remote caches reset when the Admiral restarts. Prometheus owns durable telemetry history. `Armada` emits `armada.storage.collections` (outcome), `armada.storage.collection_duration` (seconds), `armada.storage.stage_duration` (discovery/filesystem/database), and `armada.storage.observed_bytes` (bounded category gauge), plus storage collection/stage spans. Category metrics have no file paths, host identifiers or table names as labels.

Retention values provide policy context rather than reclaimable-byte estimates. Zero days means expiry is disabled; `BuildSlots` counts retained builds. Storage inventory offers no cleanup controls.

### Strict captain assignments

Set `captainAssignments[].strictCaptain` to `true` alongside `captainId` to wait for that selected captain instead of falling back when busy/unavailable. The default is `false`; tier fallback remains unchanged. Exact persona overrides take precedence over `*`. Strict selection also prevents automatic rework escalation to a different captain: an exhausted or stagnant chain reports remediation and preserves its work. Without a selected captain this flag has no effect. C# clients share `CaptainAssignmentOverride.StrictCaptain`. See [PIPELINES.md](PIPELINES.md#validated-slice-contracts) for plan validation and full-pipeline rework.
